The trained heterogeneous models achieve comparable or better task performance than the original large model and established multi-agent baselines, including reinforcement-learning-based methods, while also achieving higher inference efficiency than the multi-agent baselines and exhibiting differentiated collaboration patterns across tasks. These findings show that learned collaboration turns model heterogeneity into complementary reasoning capabilities.

</details>


### [35] [Learning More by Training Less: Uncertainty-Guided Progressive Contraction for Efficient Reasoning Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12933)
*Suorong Yang,Jiaxin Wang,Jiaming Su,Chentao Yue,Furao Shen,Peng Ye,Soujanya Poria*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出不确定性引导的渐进收缩框架，通过蒙特卡洛推理树刻画过程级不确定性，将推理监督语料与SFT优化预算逐级压缩，在仅用0.22%优化预算、训练曝光减少450倍以上的条件下保持或提升推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用越来越大的语料扩展推理后训练会显著增加训练成本，却无法带来成比例的收益。作者因此探究：推理监督能被压缩到什么程度而不损失能力。

Method: 提出不确定性引导的渐进收缩框架。不再把教师生成的CoT视为单一实现路径，而是围绕中间推理状态构建蒙特卡洛推理树，暴露过程级不确定性。第一阶段用该不确定性将原始语料收缩为紧凑的教师知情池；第二阶段进行学习者感知收缩，在SFT中在线路由，依据学生不断变化的学习状态聚焦优化，从而同时压缩监督与优化。

Result: 在多种推理基准与LLM系列上，方法仅用全数据SFT优化预算的0.22%，训练曝光减少450倍以上，同时保持或提升推理性能。

Conclusion: 该框架可泛化到异构学生模型系列、黑盒教师以及弱到强迁移，说明它不仅是加速训练的手段，还能增强推理能力。

Abstract: Large language models have achieved remarkable advances in complex reasoning. However, scaling reasoning post-training with ever-larger corpora can substantially increase training cost without proportional performance gains. In this study, we therefore ask: how far can reasoning supervision be compressed without sacrificing capability? To this end, we propose an uncertainty-guided progressive contraction framework that concentrates supervision and optimization on the reasoning trajectories most valuable for learning. Specifically, rather than treating a teacher-generated chain-of-thought (CoT) as a single realized path, we construct uncertainty-profiled reasoning supervision by building Monte Carlo reasoning trees around intermediate reasoning states to expose process-level uncertainty. This uncertainty first contracts the original corpus into a compact teacher-informed pool. A second, learner-aware contraction then performs online routing during SFT, concentrating optimization according to the student's evolving learning state. Together, the two stages progressively contract both supervision and optimization, substantially reducing the student training budget. Experiments across diverse reasoning benchmarks and LLM families show that our method uses as little as 0.22% of the optimization budget of full-data SFT, over 450x less training exposure, while maintaining or improving reasoning performance. Notably, our framework generalizes well across heterogeneous student families, black-box teachers, and weak-to-strong transfer. This indicates that it is not only a way to accelerate training but can also enhance reasoning capability.

</details>


### [36] [MathVis: Can AI Reason through Drawing? A Benchmark for Mathematical Figure Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12954)
*Jingjing Wei,Hao Zhao,Xinke Li,Linxi Yu,Xiaosong Gao,Shanshan Xiao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 MathVis 基准，用于评估大语言模型可视化前沿数学问题并进行图示辅助推理的能力；基准含 512 个任务，各含题面、参考图、可执行 TikZ/SVG 代码、Gold IR、原子评分细则与错误码。GPT-6-Astra、Claude Fable 5、Gemini 3.1 Pro Preview 的 pass@1 分别为 31.25%、26.56%、22.85%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有评测缺乏对 LLM 在数学问题中"可视化"与"以图示为中介进行推理"能力的考察，且需要诊断模型可视化输出与人类可读性之间的差距。

Method: 构建 MathVis 基准，包含 512 个任务，每个任务由数学陈述、参考图、可执行 TikZ 或 SVG 代码、Gold 中间表示（IR）、原子评分细则（atomic rubrics）和错误码组成；据此对多个前沿 LLM 进行严格评测（pass@1）。

Result: GPT-6-Astra、Claude Fable 5、Gemini 3.1 Pro Preview 的 pass@1 分别为 31.25%、26.56%、22.85%；模型输出以形式语言、符号和图形程序为主，而非显式的图示中介推理；生成解虽可能包含全部必要元素，但在视觉上常令人困惑。

Conclusion: MathVis 旨在推动数学问题的可视化评测，并促进通过视觉图示简化 LLM 推理过程的研究。

Abstract: This paper introduces MathVis, a benchmark designed to evaluate LLMs' ability to visualize frontier mathematical problems and perform diagram-assisted reasoning. The benchmark consists of 512 tasks, each comprising a mathematical statement, a reference diagram, executable TikZ or SVG code, a Gold Intermediate Representation (IR), atomic rubrics, and error codes. Through rigorous evaluation, GPT-6-Astra, Claude Fable 5, and Gemini 3.1 Pro Preview achieve pass@1 scores of 31.25%, 26.56%, and 22.85%, respectively. We found that although the tasks require visualization, LLMs produce outputs dominated by formal languages, symbols, and graphics programs, rather than explicit diagram-mediated reasoning. Furthermore, while the solutions generated by LLMs may contain all the necessary elements, they are often visually confusing to humans. Ultimately, this benchmark aims to visualize mathematical problems and promote research on enabling LLMs to simplify their reasoning via visual diagrams.

</details>


### [37] [SmartSalesBench: A Resistance-Aware Multi-Turn Benchmark for Evaluating LLM Sales Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12975)
*P N Bhargav Teja,Velpula Lokesh Reddy,Madhu Basumata,Susen Sarkar,Rajasekhara Reddy Duvvuru Muni*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SmartSalesBench是首个面向LLM销售智能体的多轮对话基准，采用心理学的"抵抗型买家"人设，包含五类智能体画像、十二种买家角色、十五款手机产品、四个受控实验（共13,680场会话），并用RAG增强生成，提出三层九项指标，结合LLM-as-judge与人工评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有任务型对话基准假设用户是合作的，并以任务完成度为核心度量，无法反映真实销售场景中买家会抵抗、质疑和施压的情况。因此需要一个以"抵抗型买家"为对手、面向销售智能体能力评估的多轮对话基准。

Method: 构建SmartSalesBench：智能体与设定为抵抗、质疑的买家角色进行最多20轮对话；资源包括5个销售智能体画像、12个买家角色、跨6个价格档位的15款手机产品；设计4组受控实验、每组3次迭代共13,680场会话；销售方与买家方生成均通过RAG接地；评价体系分三层九项指标（主结果：CR、BRS、RIR；次级质量：SMS、PFS、ETI；探索性：ITS、ARI、SPI），并采用自动化LLM-as-judge与人工评估结合。

Result: 人工评估样本n=50，评分者间一致性κ=0.71，自动评分与人工BRS的Pearson相关系数r=0.81，表明LLM-as-judge与人工判断具有较好一致性。

Conclusion: SmartSalesBench为LLM销售智能体提供了以抵抗型买家为对手的、心理 grounded 的多轮对话评测框架，指标层次化且兼具自动化与人工验证，可支撑销售对话能力的系统评估，并保留探索性指标以备后续报告。

Abstract: We present SmartSalesBench, the first multi-turn conversational benchmark explicitly designed to evaluate large language model (LLM) sales agents against psychologically grounded, resistant buyer personas. Existing task-oriented dialogue benchmarks assume cooperative users and measure task completion; SmartSalesBench inverts this assumption, placing agents against personas programmed to resist and challenge across conversations of up to twenty turns. The benchmark includes five agent profiles, twelve buyer personas, fifteen smartphone products across six price tiers, and four controlled experiments with 3 iterations totaling 13,680 sessions. Both sales-agent and buyer persona generations are grounded through retrieval-augmented generation (RAG). We introduce nine evaluation metrics grouped into three tiers: primary outcome metrics (CR, BRS, RIR), secondary quality metrics (SMS, PFS, ETI), and exploratory metrics planned for future reporting (ITS, ARI, SPI). Our evaluation combines automated LLM-as-judge scoring with internal human assessment (n = 50, \k{appa} = 0.71, Pearson r = 0.81 with BRS).

</details>


### [38] [Beyond Semantic IDs: Finite-Beam Reachability and Continuous-Query Generation in Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12977)
*Yu Sun,Renkai Xiang,Zuodong Xiang,Junwei Zhou,Yike Zhang,Jianxin Zhang,Zhihao Zhu,Xiaoyu Cao,Hailu Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究生成式推荐（GR）中基于离散语义ID（SID）的有限宽度束搜索输出接口对目标可达性的限制，提出两种连续查询范式（CQG-Single、CQG-AR），消除SID特有的可达性约束并避免离散词表与预训练主干向量空间对齐的难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交平台生成式推荐通常以自回归方式解码离散语义ID（SID），并用有限宽度束搜索返回物品列表。这种输出接口会制约被留出目标能否进入有限返回列表，而现有工作往往混淆了"全目录可打分"与"经验性Top-K支持"两个概念，因此需要重新审视该输出接口的约束。

Method: 提出两种连续查询范式：CQG-Single 单次生成一个检索查询；CQG-AR 自回归生成连续残差更新，保留逐步解码但去除SID物品token词表、code查找与离散束扩展。二者均消除有限束解码导致的SID特有可达性约束，并规避离散SID词表与预训练主干向量空间的对齐难题；通过稠密或近似最近邻检索使每个编码物品可直接打分。在统一协议与合法前缀约束解码下进行评测。

Result: 在统一协议下，CQG-Single 在全部七次运行中均超过 TIGER 风格基线，且可训练参数量仅为后者的 55.3%；CQG-AR 在 ML-1M 上表现相当，在 Beauty 与 Sports 数据集上数值更强。受控深度消融表明，额外的连续精化并不单调提升浅层准确率。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，在评估生成式推荐系统时，应将输出表示、自回归建模与检索预算三者解耦，而非将其捆绑在同一评价框架下。

Abstract: Generative recommendation (GR) of social media platforms commonly retrieves items by autoregressively decoding discrete Semantic IDs (SIDs) with finite-width beam search. This work examines how this output interface constrains whether held-out targets enter finite returned lists, while distinguishing catalog-wide scoreability from empirical top-K support. We introduce two continuous-query paradigms: CQG-Single generates one retrieval query in a single pass, and CQG-AR autoregressively generates continuous residual updates, retaining step-wise decoding while removing the SID item-token vocabulary, code lookup, and discrete beam expansion. These designs thereby eliminate the SID-specific reachability constraint induced by finite-beam decoding and avoid the technical challenge of aligning a discrete SID vocabulary with the pretrained backbone's vector space. Both methods make every encoded item directly scoreable through dense or approximate-nearest-neighbor retrieval. Under a unified protocol with valid-prefix constrained decoding, CQG-Single exceeds the TIGER-style baseline in all seven runs while using 55.3% as many trainable parameters. CQG-AR remains comparable on ML-1M and is numerically stronger on Beauty and Sports datasets. A controlled depth ablation further shows that additional continuous refinement does not monotonically improve shallow accuracy. These findings motivate decoupling output representation, autoregressive modeling, and retrieval budget when evaluating generative recommendation systems.

</details>


### [39] [Work2Skill: Capturing Human GUI Experience for Reusable Agent Skill Induction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12984)
*Yuqi Huang,Hui Li,Yifei Cao,Zikun Cai,Tianci Xie,Yangfan Gao,Pinxue Guo,Chaoyun Zhang,Bingxuan Li,Rui Zheng,Hang Yan,Ning Liao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Work2Skill框架，通过采集人类GUI演示经验来归纳可复用的智能体技能，并构建数据集WorkDemo-350与技能归纳方法，在多个模型与基准上取得最优分数和成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算机使用智能体虽能完成日益复杂的GUI任务，但面对陌生软件及依赖人类领域知识的工作流仍具挑战。人类演示虽能传递流程知识，但复用需在上下文中解释动作、并把工作流规则与任务细节分离，因此需要一套能捕获并归纳人类GUI经验的框架。

Method: Work2Skill框架包含：WorkRecorder基础设施，负责对齐屏幕录制、鼠标键盘事件及可得的无障碍树信息；基于此构建WorkDemo-350，首个同步多模态人类桌面GUI演示数据集（350条演示，经完整性与任务成功筛选）；并提出按需检查证据的智能体式技能归纳方法，生成包含工作流规则、步骤依赖与完成校验的可复用技能。

Result: 在共享执行设置下，用各方法归纳的技能配备同四个执行模型时，Work2Skill取得最高分数与成功率。相比对应No Skill基线，MyPCBench Bounded Action平均分提升4.67–10.54分，OSWorld-Verified的286任务子集提升3.11–7.50分。

Conclusion: 人类GUI演示可被有效捕获并转化为可复用智能体技能，Work2Skill在技能归纳与任务执行上优于基线，且代码与数据已开源。

Abstract: Computer-use agents can perform increasingly complex GUI tasks, yet unfamiliar software and workflows requiring human domain knowledge remain challenging. Human demonstrations convey procedural knowledge through the same GUI interaction space, but reuse requires interpreting actions in context and separating workflow rules from task-specific details. We thus introduce Work2Skill, a framework that captures human GUI experience for reusable agent skill induction. WorkRecorder provides the framework's infrastructure for collecting this experience, aligning screen recordings, mouse and keyboard events, and available accessibility-tree information. Using WorkRecorder, we construct WorkDemo-350, the first synchronized multimodal dataset of human desktop GUI demonstrations for reusable agent skill induction and downstream evaluation. Its 350 demonstrations are screened for completeness and task success. We then propose an agentic skill-induction method that inspects evidence on demand to produce reusable skills capturing workflow rules, step dependencies, and completion checks. When the same four execution models are equipped with skills induced by each method under shared execution settings, Work2Skill achieves the highest scores and success rates. Mean score improvements over the corresponding No Skill baseline are 4.67--10.54 points on MyPCBench Bounded Action and 3.11--7.50 points on a 286-task subset of OSWorld-Verified. Our code and data are available at https://github.com/china-qijizhifeng/Work2Skill.

</details>


### [40] [Benchmarking Embedding Geometries and Clustering Algorithms for Unsupervised Patent Claim Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12997)
*Sujith Subramanian,P N Bhargav Teja,Susen Sarkar,Rajasekhara Reddy Duvvuru Muni*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究基于专利文本的无监督表征（双曲 vs 欧氏VAE）能否检索出分类代码限制下无法发现的跨类现有技术，并检验双曲空间的增益及其适用条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有技术检索系统以分类代码为核心组织结构，导致跨代码发现困难；作者希望验证无监督专利文本表征能否恢复代码受限检索遗漏的相关连接，并评估双曲几何是否有帮助。

Method: 在45,000件美国专利上训练双曲与匹配的欧氏变分自编码器（VAE），共96次运行、6个随机种子；以3,000条查询进行检索评测；另在10个技术领域（额外50,000件专利）上分析领域分类广度等特征与检索表现的关系；采用NMI等指标并与官方分类对比，报告统计透明度并发布91项测试的自动化实现。

Result: 双曲模型检索到真正跨类别现有技术的频率比欧氏模型高52%（Top-10结果中18.76% vs. 12.34%，3,000条查询）；双曲模型与官方分类一致性更低（NMI 0.2392 vs. 0.3206）；领域分类广度可预测方法表现（Pearson r=-0.642, p=0.045, N=10），而每子组专利数是更强的预测因子（r=0.715, p=0.020）。

Conclusion: 双曲表征在跨类别现有技术检索上带来实际增益；与官方分类一致性低并非缺陷，而是两种度量目标不同，其中只有一种契合现有技术检索需求；分类广度与每子组专利数可提供可检验的信号，帮助实践者判断该方法在哪些领域最有价值。

Abstract: Prior-art retrieval systems are structurally organized around classification codes, making cross-code discovery difficult. This paper asks if an unsupervised representation trained on patent text can recover relevant connections that code-restricted search cannot, and if hyperbolic space helps. We trained a hyperbolic and a matched Euclidean Variational Autoencoder (VAE) on 45,000 US patents (96 runs, 6 seeds). The hyperbolic model retrieves genuinely cross-category prior art 52% more often than the Euclidean model (18.76% vs. 12.34% of top-10 results, 3,000 queries). This represents a practical gain for the motivating search problem. The hyperbolic model agrees less with official classification (NMI 0.2392 vs. 0.3206), which we explain geometrically: the two measures capture different goals, and only one aligns with prior-art search needs. In a study of 10 technology domains (50,000 more patents), we find that a domain's classification breadth--the number of distinct CPC subgroups spanned--predicts approach performance (Pearson r=-0.642, p=0.045, N=10), and that patents per subgroup is an even stronger predictor (r=0.715, p=0.020). This provides a checkable signal for practitioners to determine where the method is most valuable. We report all findings with statistical transparency and release a complete, 91-test automated implementation.

</details>


### [41] [Hindsight Structured Advice Distillation: Steering Executor Agents with Advisors Trained on Their Failures and Task Characteristics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13000)
*O. Ozan Koyluoglu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 HSAD（后见之明结构化建议蒸馏）：用一个小型 advisor 模型针对每个输入生成结构化建议并追加到冻结 executor 的提示中，从而以低成本、低延迟提升语言模型智能体在专业任务上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专业领域（金融、医疗、法律）的语言模型智能体虽具备完成任务的能力，但常遗漏任务细节（如某数字来自哪份文件、公司如何披露某指标）。重训练成本高，闭源或共享系统下甚至不可行；而把缺失知识写入提示会导致提示冗长且对所有输入相同，无法满足不同输入需要不同指导的需求。

Method: HSAD 训练一个独立的小型 advisor 模型：它读取每个输入并生成结构化建议，追加到冻结 executor 的提示中。advisor 采用"后见之明+特权信息蒸馏"方式训练：在有分级答案的训练池上分析 executor 的失败，让其在多种策略下运行以判断哪种策略对每个输入有效，再由能看到评分与执行轨迹的 teacher 写出"本应有用"的建议；advisor 通过监督微调学习仅凭输入生成该建议。由于学生是独立小模型而非 executor 本身，训练成本低、延迟增加小。

Result: 在公开金融研究查询基准 FrontierFinance 上，8B advisor 将已位居榜首的 Samaya 专有研究智能体的合格率从 52.9% 提升至 56.6%，超过该智能体自身的高投入配置，且每次查询成本降低 37%。HSAD 在 WebShop、BIRD 文本转 SQL 和 ExpertLongBench 上也显示出一致的提升，验证了泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过训练小型 advisor 模型为每个输入生成结构化建议，可在不重训、不改动 executor 的前提下有效补足专业任务细节知识，在提升性能的同时降低成本与延迟，并具备跨任务泛化性。

Abstract: Language-model agents are used for specialized work such as financial research, healthcare and law. Such agents are often capable enough for a task but still miss its details. In finance, for example, these details include which filing reports a figure or how a company discloses a metric. Retraining the agent to learn these details is expensive, and often impossible when the agent is a closed or shared system. Writing the missing know-how into the agent's prompt makes the prompt long and identical for every input, although different inputs need different guidance. We propose to learn to generate this guidance instead. A small advisor model reads each input and writes structured advice, which is appended to the prompt of a frozen executor. The advisor is trained with hindsight, following work on distillation with privileged information. On a training pool with graded answers, we analyse the executor's failures, run it under several strategies to see which one worked on each input, and let a teacher that sees the grades and the traces write the advice that would have helped. The advisor then learns, by supervised fine-tuning, to write that advice from the input alone. Because the student is a separate small model rather than the executor, it is cheap to train and adds little latency. We call this approach hindsight structured advice distillation (HSAD). On FrontierFinance, a public benchmark of expert-written financial research queries, an 8B advisor raises the qualification rate of Samaya's proprietary research agent, which already leads the benchmark's leaderboard, from 52.9% to 56.6%. This is above the agent's own high-effort configuration, at 37% lower cost per query. We study the generalization of the proposed approach, and in particular show that HSAD also improves results on WebShop, BIRD text-to-SQL and ExpertLongBench.

</details>


### [42] [Scaling Empowered Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13002)
*Catherine Ji,Vivek Myers,Joshua Lin,Alexandra Wingate,Sergey Levine,Benjamin Eysenbach*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文将赋能（empowerment）与技能学习相联系，提出一种通用的赋能最大化方法，使在非表格、大规模环境下研究赋能成为可能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统赋能研究多局限于表格型（tabular）小规模环境，难以验证理论上预测的赋能特性（如资源收集、环境改造、生存等），本文旨在将赋能研究扩展到大规模场景。

Method: 提出一个简单且可扩展的赋能最大化方法：将赋能理解为从某状态出发可执行技能的数量，因此最大化赋能即寻找可作为多种技能起点的状态。该方法可与任意底层技能学习方法结合，不依赖特定技能学习算法。

Result: 实验表明该方法能够在更大规模环境中实现赋能最大化，并解锁有趣的新兴行为，例如在Crafter环境中搭建房屋。同时还能体现理论上预测但以往经验上难以观察的赋能特性。

Conclusion: 技能学习不等同于赋能，但通过将赋能定义为可执行技能的数量，可构建通用、可扩展的赋能最大化方案，从而在更接近现实的大规模任务中研究赋能现象及其涌现行为。

Abstract: Empowerment measures the capacity of an agent to actively control its environment. In this work, we explain the connection between skill learning and empowerment. While skill learning is not empowerment, we introduce a simple scalable recipe for empowerment maximization: empowerment corresponds to the number of executable skills from a state, so maximizing empowerment corresponds to reaching states that are good starting points for many skills. Our recipe applies to arbitrary underlying skill learning methods. Whereas most prior work on empowerment looks at tabular settings, this recipe enables us to study empowerment at a scale where we can realize theoretically-predicted but empirically-elusive properties of empowerment (e.g., gathering resources, environment modification, survival). Experiments show that empowerment maximization unlocks interesting emergent properties, such as building a house in Crafter. Website and code at \href{https://empowerment-geometry.github.io/scaling-empowerment/}{this https url}.

</details>


### [43] [Provenance-Aware Belief Updating in Human-AI Feedback Loops](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13008)
*Tianyi Huang,Eric Liu,Jay Chong,Emily Yin,Wanqi Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出潜商证据训练（LQET），使语言模型依据报告来源谱系而非报告数量更新信念，显著抑制重复引用造成的伪证据累积。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在重复的人机交互中，同一观察被引用、摘要并回投给模型，会形成多份报告，使重复看起来像独立佐证。论文关注语言模型能否利用所提供的报告谱系，按证据来源而非报告计数进行信念更新。

Method: 提出LQET关系型目标：结合稀疏贝叶斯监督，并施加约束以鼓励两类行为——当报告不带来新证据时预测保持稳定；在接力式历史中面对同一新证据时更新保持一致。在受控二分类基准上，以Qwen3-4B与Phi-4-mini为骨干，每个骨干使用三个配对训练种子进行评测。

Result: 相对仅任务训练，LQET将新证据更新RMSE降低41–44%，并在八步接力后将对数几率漂移降低89–91%。增益还延伸到闭环仿真：模型置信度决定后续轮次中重新进入上下文的中继报告数量。

Conclusion: 将信念更新anchoring于所提供的来源谱系，LQET为动态人机系统提供了一条路径：影响力取决于新证据本身，而非同一证据被重复返回的次数。

Abstract: In repeated human-AI interaction, a single observation can be quoted, summarized, and returned to a model as multiple reports, making repetition look like independent corroboration. We study whether language models can use supplied report lineage to update according to evidence provenance rather than report count. We introduce Latent Quotient Evidence Training (LQET), a relational objective that combines sparse Bayesian supervision with constraints that encourage stable predictions when reports add no new evidence and consistent updates to the same new evidence across relay histories. On a controlled binary benchmark, LQET reduces new-evidence update RMSE by 41-44% and log-odds drift after eight relay steps by 89-91% relative to task-only training across Qwen3-4B and Phi-4-mini, with three paired training seeds per backbone. The gains extend to a closed-loop simulation in which model confidence controls how many relayed reports re-enter the model's context in later rounds. By grounding belief updates in supplied provenance, LQET offers a route toward dynamic human-AI systems in which influence follows new evidence, not the number of times the same evidence returns.

</details>


### [44] [EmbodiedCLBench: Evaluating Continual Learning for Self-Evolving Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13021)
*Jie Huang,Yanan Chen,Ruixun Liu,Kaichen He,Xinyi He,Rui Huang,Zilong Zheng,Zhenliang Zhang,Yiwu Zhong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出首个具身仿真持续学习基准EmbodiedCLBench，以组合泛化为核心评估自演化智能体从基础任务学习并重组技能解决复杂新任务的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有持续学习研究大多局限于非具身环境，未扩展到具身场景；具身智能体持续学习这一基础能力尚缺乏系统评估手段。

Method: 构建EmbodiedCLBench基准，以组合泛化为核心评估原则，要求智能体先学习基础任务再重组已获技能解决复杂未见高级任务；基于多种智能体harness与学习方法开展大量实验。

Result: 零样本设置下高级任务几乎无法解决，而基础任务经验可带来稳定提升，且该收益在任务复杂度增加时保持稳定；但提升不随学习样本数量增长而扩展，当前智能体无法从额外经验中提取有效信息。

Conclusion: 持续学习是被低估且尚待探索的基础能力，该基准为在具身环境中设计有效自演化智能体提供资源。

Abstract: Continual learning requires models to accumulate past experience and transfer it to new tasks. Despite substantial research on continual learning, existing work remains largely confined to non-embodied settings, without extending to embodied environments. To address this gap, we introduce EmbodiedCLBench, the first benchmark in embodied simulation for evaluating the continual learning capability of self-evolving agents. With compositional generalization as the core evaluation principle, our benchmark evaluates whether agents can first learn from basic tasks and then recombine the acquired skills to solve complex, unseen advanced tasks. Based on EmbodiedCLBench, we conduct extensive experiments across multiple agent harnesses and learning methods, revealing consistent limitations and distinctive behaviors. Our results show that while advanced tasks can be hardly solved under zero-shot setting, the experience from basic tasks enables consistent improvement. Such benefit from the experience remains stable even if the task complexity increases. However, this improvement does not scale accordingly with the number of learning samples, and current agents cannot extract meaningful information from additional experience. Collectively, our findings highlight continual learning as a fundamental yet underexplored capability, and our benchmark offers valuable resources for designing effective self-evolving agents in embodied environments. Our code and dataset are available at https://pku-value-lab.github.io/EmbodiedCLBench-homepage.

</details>


### [45] [Travel Back To Move Forward: Retrospective Exploration and Evolving for Long Horizon Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13058)
*Ziang Ye,Wentao Shi,Lei Chen,Zhengzhou Cai,Yu Wang,Zhiyuan Yao,Zihan Wang,Xierui Song,Fuli Feng,Yixin Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ReTRAIL 让智能体在长时程任务中"回退环境状态但保留所学知识"，通过状态锚定 GRPO 与分支推进奖励训练共享策略，实现从死胡同轨迹中恢复并复用被放弃探索的经验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程环境中，早期错误会引发级联失败，使智能体陷入死胡同轨迹。智能体需主动从此类状态恢复，并将探索经验转化为后续决策的指导。作者指出，仅添加"回退原语"收益有限甚至损害性能，关键在于将环境回退与经验复用耦合起来。

Method: 提出 ReTRAIL 框架：1）允许智能体将环境回退到先前状态，同时保留有效进展、携带从被放弃探索中获得的知识，即"环境状态后退、智能体知识前进"；2）训练一个共享策略，使用两个互补信号——State-Anchored GRPO 从被重新访问的状态比较不同延续以改进执行，以及 branch-advancement reward 通过后续探索的进展来引导反思。

Result: 在四个交互式基准（MineSweeper、Sokoban、ALFWorld、ScienceWorld）上，单独添加回退原语收益有限，甚至可能降低性能；而 ReTRAIL 使智能体能够从无产出的状态回退，并利用被放弃分支的洞见指导后续探索。

Conclusion: 结果证明，在持续进行的任务中，将环境回退与经验复用相结合，对长时程任务求解具有重要价值。

Abstract: Large language model agents increasingly operate in long-horizon environments where early mistakes can trigger cascading failures and trap agents in dead-end trajectories. Agents must therefore be able to proactively recover from such states and turn their exploratory experience into guidance for subsequent decisions. We introduce ReTRAIL, a framework that enables agents to rewind the environment to earlier states while preserving valid progress and carrying forward knowledge learned from abandoned exploration. The environment state moves backward while the agent's knowledge continues to move forward. ReTRAIL trains a shared policy with two complementary signals: State-Anchored GRPO compares alternative continuations from revisited states to improve execution, while a branch-advancement reward guides reflection through the progress of subsequent exploration. Experiments across four interactive benchmark MineSweeper, Sokoban, ALFWorld and ScienceWorld show that simply adding a rewind primitive provides limited benefit and can even degrade performance. In contrast, ReTRAIL enables agents to rewind from unproductive states and use insights from abandoned branches to guide subsequent exploration. These results demonstrate the value of coupling environment rewinding with experience reuse within an ongoing task for long-horizon task solving. The code is available at https://github.com/whi497/ReTRAIL.

</details>


### [46] [LongHarness: Advancing Agentic Reasoning over 100M-Token Corpora via Stateful Evidence Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13075)
*Songtao Huang,Zhijie Zhong,Xiaohan He,Shuaiyu Zhang,Zhiyin Yu,Li Kang,Heng Zhou,Yutao Fan,Xiangchao Yan,Bo Zhang,Shiyang Feng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LongHarness 是一个模型无关的 agent 框架，通过显式建模“证据—结论”协同演化与证据生命周期管理，解决长上下文语料推理中的无界探索与证据—状态漂移问题，在多个长上下文基准上大幅超越直接推理及主流 agent 框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语料级长上下文推理要求 agent 跨超长文档集合获取并整合证据。现有 agent 框架虽支持增量访问，但已获取证据与演化中的结论纠缠在不断增长的交互历史中，属于隐式状态管理，导致两类耦合失效：一是无界探索（检索不断扩张历史却未解决决策相关信息缺口），二是证据—状态漂移（已有证据或后续修正未能影响当前结论）。

Method: 提出 LongHarness，一个模型无关框架，使“已获取证据”与“演化结论”之间的交互显式化。核心机制为 Evidence-Conclusion Coevolution（证据—结论协同演化）：用未解决的结论引导探索缺失信息，新获取的证据则强化或修正指导检索的结论；并配合 Evidence Lifecycle Management（证据生命周期管理），保证累积证据在整个交互过程中保持时效性与可追溯性。二者共同将检索聚焦于剩余信息需求，并使结论与当前证据保持一致。

Result: 在 OfficeQA Pro V1/V2、AA-LCR 与 LongBench v2 上，使用 GLM-5.3-Flash 和 Qwen3.8-Flash 评估。相比同底座直接推理，GLM-5.3-Flash 在 OfficeQA Pro 的 exact match 由 V1 的 15.8% 提升至 60.2%，V2 由 4.4% 提升至 61.1%；相对 SOTA agent 框架，V1 超过 Claude Code 12.0 个百分点，V2 超过 Codex 18.9 个百分点。使用 Qwen3.8-Flash 时，V1 达 61.7%，超 Claude Code 8.3 个百分点、Codex 12.0 个百分点。

Conclusion: LongHarness 通过显式管理证据与结论的协同演化及证据生命周期，有效缓解无界探索与证据—状态漂移，在多个长上下文基准上取得显著且跨模型一致的性能提升，验证了显式状态管理对语料级长上下文推理的有效性。

Abstract: Corpus-level long-context reasoning requires agents to acquire and integrate evidence across document collections that exceed their context windows. Existing agent harnesses enable incremental access to these corpora, but acquired evidence and evolving conclusions often remain entangled in a growing interaction history. This implicit state management can lead to two coupled failure modes: unbounded exploration, where retrieval expands the interaction history without resolving decision-relevant information gaps, and evidence-state drift, where previously acquired evidence or subsequent corrections fail to inform the current conclusion. We present LongHarness, a model-agnostic harness that makes the interaction between acquired evidence and evolving conclusions explicit. Its core mechanism, Evidence-Conclusion Coevolution, uses unresolved conclusions to guide exploration toward missing information, while newly acquired evidence strengthens or revises the conclusions that directed the search. LongHarness couples this process with Evidence Lifecycle Management, which keeps accumulated evidence current and traceable throughout the interaction. Together, these mechanisms focus retrieval on remaining information needs and keep conclusions aligned with current evidence. We evaluate LongHarness on OfficeQA Pro V1 and V2, AA-LCR, and LongBench v2 using GLM-5.3-Flash and Qwen3.8-Flash. Compared with direct reasoning using the same backbone, LongHarness improves GLM-5.3-Flash exact match on OfficeQA Pro from 15.8% to 60.2% on V1 and from 4.4% to 61.1% on V2. Against state-of-the-art agent harnesses, it surpasses Claude Code by 12.0 percentage points on V1 and Codex by 18.9 points on V2. With Qwen3.8-Flash, LongHarness reaches 61.7% on V1, outperforming Claude Code by 8.3 points and Codex by 12.0 points.

</details>


### [47] [Attention Sees, MLP Refuses: Tracing the Attention-to-MLP Safety Pathway in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13078)
*Zixuan Weng,Haoqi He,Weifei Jin,Yue Huang,Yang Bai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究将安全对齐LLM的越狱与过度拒答问题重构为"危害识别"与"拒答书写"两条通路的可测量问题，发现注意力更新更易解码危害信息、MLP更新主导拒答书写，并提出双信号方法Sentinel实现更优路由。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全对齐的大语言模型仍易受越狱攻击，同时会过度拒绝良性请求。理解这些失败需要区分"危害性识别"与"拒答计算"两个过程，但此前缺乏对二者在模型内部如何分离的机制性、可测量的刻画。

Method: 在decoder-only Transformer的残差流更新上，区分模块更新所暴露的危害信息与其产生的可读拒答证据变化；分析18对Base-Instruct模型（Qwen、Llama、Gemma）中注意力与MLP更新的作用差异；在6个推理模型上观察推理过程中的通路动态；通过因果干预（将下游MLP更新重置为干净值）验证通路关系；提出Sentinel，结合注意力导出的感知分数P与MLP导出的决策分数D，进行条件路由。

Result: 危害性从注意力更新（尤其早层）更易解码；MLP更新在对齐模型中更强地贡献危害选择性拒答书写。后训练在全部18对中增强MLP拒答书写，却未系统性提升危害可解码性，且正向MLP书写在深层更集中。在6个推理模型中，推理期间危害仍可解码，而强拒答书写集中于答案起始处，该模式在关闭推理时依然存在。因果干预显示，将下游MLP更新重置为干净值可大幅削弱上游注意力干预引起的拒答偏移。双信号条件路由的聚合错误率低于单信号路由及评估的固定提醒。

Conclusion: 安全失败可被理解为注意力主导的危害感知通路与MLP主导的拒答书写通路的分离与交互。Sentinel利用二者的联合信号画像刻画越狱与过度拒答，并通过条件路由在8个模型上取得更低的聚合错误率，为安全对齐的诊断与干预提供了通路级视角与实用工具。

Abstract: Safety-aligned large language models (LLMs) remain vulnerable to jailbreaks and can over-refuse benign requests. Understanding these failures requires distinguishing harmfulness recognition from the computation that writes refusal. We reframe this as a pathway question and make it measurable on residual-stream updates in decoder-only Transformers, separating the harmfulness information a module update exposes from the change it produces in readable refusal evidence. Across 18 Base-Instruct pairs spanning Qwen, Llama, and Gemma, harmfulness is decoded more readily from attention updates, particularly in early layers, whereas MLP updates contribute more strongly to harm-selective refusal writing in aligned models. Post-training strengthens MLP refusal writing in all 18 pairs without systematically improving harmfulness decodability, while positive MLP writing becomes more concentrated in deeper layers after post-training. Across six reasoning-enabled models, harmfulness remains decodable during reasoning while strong refusal writing concentrates at answer onset, a pattern that persists with reasoning disabled. Causal interventions further support this pathway: resetting downstream MLP updates to their clean values substantially reduces the refusal shift caused by upstream attention interventions. Building on these findings, we introduce Sentinel, which combines an attention-derived perception score P with an MLP-derived decision score D. Their joint profiles characterize jailbreaks and over-refusals, and conditional routing with both signals achieves a lower aggregate error rate than single-signal routing and evaluated fixed reminders across eight models.

</details>


### [48] [SWEAR: Section-Wise Efficient Audio Reasoning via Self-Rated Difficulty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13081)
*Yixuan Li,Minghao Lv,Xuenan Xu,Zihao Zheng,Mengyue Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SWEAR框架，通过五维难度评分与压缩推理轨迹，在SFT学习难度信号并用强化学习自适应分配推理资源，在MMAU-test-mini和MMAR上提升准确率的同时大幅减少生成token。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大型音频语言模型的自适应推理方法多依赖粗粒度难度估计，忽略了决定推理工作量的多种因素，导致推理资源分配不够精细。

Method: 从互补的离线评估中推导五维难度分数与压缩推理轨迹；在此基础上提出SWEAR难度感知推理框架，在监督微调阶段学习难度信号，并利用强化学习在推理各阶段自适应分配推理量。

Result: 在MMAU-test-mini与MMAR上的实验表明，SWEAR在提升准确率的同时显著减少生成token数量，对简单样本缩短推理，且不损害困难样本的性能。

Conclusion: 细粒度、多维度的难度建模结合分阶段的推理分配，可在音频语言模型中实现准确率与推理效率的更好平衡。

Abstract: Recent work on large audio-language models (LALMs) explores adaptive reasoning to balance accuracy and efficiency. However, existing approaches often rely on coarse-grained difficulty estimation, overlooking the diverse factors that determine reasoning effort. We address this limitation by deriving five-dimensional difficulty scores and compressed reasoning traces from complementary offline assessments. Based on this characterization, we propose SWEAR, a difficulty-aware reasoning framework that learns difficulty signals during supervised fine-tuning and uses reinforcement learning to adapt reasoning allocation across reasoning stages. Experiments on MMAU-test-mini and MMAR show that SWEAR improves accuracy while substantially reducing generated tokens, shortening reasoning for easy instances without sacrificing performance on difficult ones.

</details>


### [49] [When Faster Decisions Do Not Make Better Agents: An Empirical Study of Jev in Agent Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13092)
*Yuchen Ling,Shengcheng Yu,Zhenyu Chen,Chunrong Fang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文在ALFWorld与AndroidWorld两个场景、五类智能体、4500次任务执行中考察"高效决策模型（Jev）所带来的局部成本与延迟节省能否转化为高效可靠的完整任务完成"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM驱动的智能体依赖大量重复决策，Jev等高效决策模型可降低单步决策成本与延迟，但这种局部节省能否转化为端到端任务完成效率与可靠性尚不清楚，即"效率迁移"问题未被验证。

Method: 在两个代表性应用场景（ALFWorld具身任务、AndroidWorld移动应用操作）中，对五类智能体集成Jev，开展4500次任务执行，联合测量任务有效性、决策级与任务级资源消耗以及执行行为。

Result: Jev集成显著降低所有五类智能体的决策成本与延迟，任务级收益随工作流而异；ALFWorld任务成功率接近基线，两个移动端智能体下降更明显。辅助推理与额外交互吸收了决策节省，失败后的恢复不足和过早结束限制了成功交付；其中一类智能体单次尝试成本下降，但"每个成功结果的平均成本"反而上升。

Conclusion: 将高效决策模型集成到智能体工作流时，需重点保障进度维持、失败恢复与完成验证；其实际价值应以成功任务交付、恢复行为以及"每个交付结果所耗资源"来评估，而不能仅看单步决策节省。

Abstract: AI agents depend on repeated decisions to accomplish tasks, with large language models (LLMs) commonly serving as their core decision makers. The recent emergence of Jev offers an opportunity to make these decisions faster and at lower cost. However, it remains unclear whether such local savings can be translated into efficient and reliable task completion. In this paper, we investigate this efficiency transfer across five agents in two representative application settings, embodied tasks in ALFWorld and mobile app use in AndroidWorld. Across 4,500 task executions, we jointly evaluate task effectiveness, decision-level and task-level resource use, and execution behavior. Experimental results show that Jev integration substantially reduces decision cost and latency across all five agents, with task-level gains varying by workflow. Task success remains close to baseline levels on ALFWorld and declines more substantially in two mobile agents. Auxiliary reasoning and additional interactions absorb decision savings, while failed recovery and premature completion limit successful delivery. Mean cost per successful outcome increases in one agent even as spending per attempt falls. These findings highlight the importance of preserving progress, supporting recovery, and verifying completion when integrating efficient decision models into agent workflows. Their practical value should be assessed through successful task delivery, recovery behavior, and resources per delivered outcome.

</details>


### [50] [VETR: Execution-Grounded Self-Improving Agent for Symbolic Graphics Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13098)
*Yingbo Ma,Kuangshi Ai,Gordon Dai,Chenglong Yu,Chaoli Wang,Hua Shen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 VETR 框架，通过利用程序执行轨迹中的运行时证据进行约束化程序修复，实现可执行世界表征的智能体递归自我改进，在 BlenderGym 与 BlenderBench 上优于现有细化策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有面向符号图形推理的视觉智能体依赖可执行世界表征，但仅凭最终渲染的模糊监督已形成性能瓶颈；已有智能体系统虽通过反复交互细化场景，却缺乏从执行轨迹中递归提取可行动证据的机制，无法判断所提出的符号编辑在执行中是被保留、消失还是相互干扰。

Method: VETR 在每个任务中保留对齐的程序/渲染记录及有界运行时观测，引入执行细化器（execution refiner），将观测到的写入与状态变化编译为受约束的候选程序；细化器针对捕获的运行时状态验证候选程序的终态与保持条件（endpoints and preservation conditions），再由常规视觉验证器评估目标相关性。修复构造成本上不额外调用语言模型，但可能增加执行次数。

Result: 在 BlenderGym（280 个任务）与 BlenderBench（27 个已发布任务）上，使用 GPT-5.6-luna 与 Qwen3.8-27B 进行评测，VETR 一致优于包括 BlenderAlchemy 和 VIGA 在内的现有细化策略；并在固定生成器-交互次数上限下，通过受控消融实验评估了执行细化与角色非对称证据组织的贡献。

Conclusion: 利用执行轨迹中经过验证的运行时证据来构造受约束的程序修复，是提升符号图形推理中可执行世界表征递归自我改进的有效途径，验证了执行细化与证据组织方式的关键作用。

Abstract: Visual agents for symbolic graphics reasoning depend on executable world representations, yet continued improvement remains bottlenecked by ambiguous supervision from final renders. Recent agentic systems refine scenes through repeated interaction, but they lack mechanisms to recursively extract actionable evidence from execution traces, overlooking which proposed symbolic edits survived, disappeared, or interfered during program execution. To address this, we propose VETR (Verified Execution Trajectories for Refinement), a framework that enables agentic recursive self-improvement over executable world representations for symbolic graphics reasoning. Within each task, VETR retains aligned program/render records with bounded runtime observations and introduces an execution refiner that compiles observed writes and state changes into constrained alternative programs. The refiner validates their endpoints and preservation conditions against captured runtime state; the ordinary visual verifier then assesses goal relevance. Repair construction makes no additional language-model calls, but can require additional executions. Evaluations on BlenderGym (280 tasks) and BlenderBench (27 released tasks) with GPT-5.6-luna and Qwen3.8-27B show that VETR consistently outperforms existing refinement strategies, including BlenderAlchemy and VIGA. Controlled ablations evaluate the contributions of execution refinement and role-asymmetric evidence organization under fixed generator-interaction caps.

</details>


### [51] [Does Location Matter? A Black-Box Causal Audit Reveals Model-Dependent Audio Grounding in LALM Hallucination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13112)
*Mohammad Khalooei,Mohammad Sabokrou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种黑盒因果测试，通过定位并移除声学证据区域来验证LALMs幻觉检测中的证据真实性，发现AAD的"空白波形"基线存在时长混淆，且在固定时长下局部化优于随机移除；在此基础上提出门控集成的对比解码，将Qwen2-Audio准确率从57.1%提升至71.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型音频语言模型（LALMs）常出现对象幻觉，即肯定不存在的声音。主流缓解方法Audio-Aware Decoding（AAD）通过将真实音频下的logits与全零"空白"波形对比来检测幻觉，但此前综述指出全零波形属于分布外（OOD）输入，其对比基线的有效性存疑。因此作者希望直接隔离并检验AAD所依赖的证据本身是否真实有效。

Method: 提出一个黑盒因果测试：利用CLAP相似度定位某个声明对应的声学证据区域，通过谱门控（spectral gating）移除该区域，并在2×2×2网格上以随机窗口作为对照，在三个LALMs上进行对比实验。此外，将空白对比与语言先验对比结合，进行门控与集成的对比解码。

Result: 在Qwen2-Audio-7B上，局部消融的原始AUROC（0.613）低于空白基线（0.721），但这是时长混淆所致（空白移除整段音频，而网格仅2-4秒），并非定位失败；固定时长后，局部化在每个网格单元上均优于随机移除。原始检测性能仍依赖模型，尤其在Audio Flamingo 3上更明显。对比解码将Qwen2-Audio准确率从57.1%提升至71.9%，优于单独的AAD。

Conclusion: AAD的空白波形对比存在时长混淆，固定时长后局部化证据移除可稳定优于随机移除；统一的因果归因画像无法在不同模型间迁移，说明不同模型具有不同的声学归因机制。所提对比解码在Qwen2-Audio上显著提升准确率，代码与详细报告已开源。

Abstract: Large audio-language models (LALMs) frequently exhibit object hallucination, affirming absent sounds. The dominant mitigation, Audio-Aware Decoding (AAD), contrasts logits under real audio against an all-zero "blank" waveform, flagged by prior review as out-of-distribution. We introduce a black-box causal test that isolates the claimed evidence itself: localize a claim's evidence region via CLAP similarity, remove it by spectral gating, and compare against a random-window control across a 2x2x2 grid on three LALMs. On Qwen2-Audio-7B, localized ablation's raw AUROC trails blank's (0.613 vs. 0.721); this is a duration confound, not a localization failure, since blank removes the entire clip versus the grid's 2-4 s. Holding duration fixed, localization beats random removal at every cell on both models. Raw detection value remains model-dependent, especially on Audio Flamingo 3. The same evidence corrects decoding: contrastive decoding combining blank and language-prior contrasts, gated and ensembled, raises Qwen2-Audio accuracy from 57.1% to 71.9%, beating AAD alone. A unified causal-grounding profile does not transfer between models, indicating different grounding regimes. The source code and a detailed report of our findings, including identified limitations, are available at https://github.com/khalooei/causa.

</details>


### [52] [DRYLAB-Bench: Benchmarking the Biosecurity Risk of Agentic LLM Mutation Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13116)
*Yuzhen Wang,Chunxiao Li,Baichen Le,Lewen Yan,Jie Zhang,Yucheng T. Yang,Xing-Ming Zhao,Lijun Li,Jing Shao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出 DRYLAB-Bench，首个评估 LLM 智能体高危生物突变设计能力的综合基准，通过多轮工具增强环境与真实湿实验数据评分，量化 LLM 及其生物设计工具（BDT）放大下的双重用途生物安全风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 已具备高级生物设计能力，带来显著研究价值的同时引入严重的双重用途风险（如增强病原体、耐药细菌）。现有生物安全框架虽已识别此类威胁，但 LLM 执行危险生物改造的实际风险尚未被量化，尤其在其被专业生物设计工具（BDT）增强时。已有评估主要考察危险知识的持有量而非实际应用能力，忽视 BDT 带来的风险放大，并依赖不可靠、昂贵或本身有害的验证方法。

Method: 提出 DRYLAB-Bench（Design Risk Yielded by LLM Agent in Biodesign）基准。任务层面：从生物安全指南中提炼出 30 个真实高危突变设计场景，超越基础问答。环境层面：构建多轮、工具增强的环境，由 LLM 编排 BDT 迭代优化候选设计。评分层面：提出生物安全风险评分（BRS），直接对照 ProteinGym 与 ViroGym 的 187,695 条经验湿实验测量值进行评分，在量化风险相关效应的同时实现可扩展且安全的真值验证。

Result: 对 15 个前沿模型的评估显示存在显著基线威胁：在不使用工具时，67.6% 的一次性设计超过实测变体平均值，21.4% 达到最高风险十分位。配备 BDT 后风险显著放大，总体风险提升 ΔBRS=+0.116，最高十分位占比升至 23.5%。

Conclusion: LLM 智能体在生物设计上已具备可量化的实质双重用途风险，且 BDT 的接入会显著放大该风险。DRYLAB-Bench 为安全、可扩展地评估并量化此类风险提供了标准化基准与指标，凸显了对前沿模型进行生物安全治理与工具管控的紧迫性。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) acquire advanced biological design capabilities, they offer substantial research benefits but simultaneously introduce severe dual-use risks, such as engineering enhanced pathogens or drug-resistant bacteria. While biosecurity frameworks recognize these threats, the actual risk of LLMs executing dangerous biological modifications remains unquantified, particularly when augmented with specialized biological design tools (BDTs). Existing evaluations primarily assess hazardous knowledge possession rather than practical application, overlook risk amplification from BDTs, and rely on unreliable, expensive, or hazardous validation methods. To address these gaps, we introduce DRYLAB-Bench (Design Risk Yielded by LLM Agent in Biodesign), the first comprehensive benchmark evaluating the high-risk biological mutation design capabilities of LLM agents. Moving beyond basic question-answering, DRYLAB-Bench tasks agents with 30 genuinely hazardous mutation-design scenarios distilled from biosecurity guidelines. It evaluates models in a multi-round, tool-augmented environment where LLMs orchestrate BDTs to iteratively refine candidates. Crucially, it scores designs directly against 187,695 empirical wet-lab measurements from ProteinGym and ViroGym using our proposed Biosecurity Risk Score (BRS), quantifying risk-associated effects while enabling scalable and safe ground-truth verification. Evaluations of 15 frontier models reveal substantial baseline threats: without tools, 67.6% of one-shot designs exceed the average measured variant, and 21.4% reach the highest-risk decile. Equipping models with BDTs significantly amplifies this risk, raising the pooled risk by \(ΔBRS=+0.116\) and the top-decile share to 23.5%. Code: https://github.com/drylab-bio/DRYLAB-Bench

</details>


### [53] [Looking Ahead and Back: Anticipatory Generative Modeling for Road-Network Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13131)
*Zhenhao Huang,Li Sun,Philip S. Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出Anticipatory Generative Modeling生成范式及Foresight模型，将轨迹生成重新定义为路网上的生成式路径规划，通过"预见—选择—更新—再预见"循环解决交叉口下一跳转移选择问题，实验优于SOTA基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型等生成方法以位置序列模拟为核心，忽略了交叉口处多出边等概率但下游路径差异巨大的转移选择问题，甚至生成偏离路网的轨迹。论文主张从位置模拟转向路网上的行为建模，以填补下一跳转移选择的空白。

Method: 提出图约束生成范式Anticipatory Generative Modeling，引入对未来路径朝目的地展开方式的"预见"来消解下一跳选择；具体实现Foresight模型，对"anticipate–select–update–re-anticipate"循环建立概率模型，在沿历史轨迹顺序生成的同时实现更长时域的预见能力。

Result: 大量实验表明，Foresight在轨迹生成任务上优于当前最先进（state-of-the-art）的基线方法。

Conclusion: 轨迹生成应从位置模拟转向路网约束下的行为建模；通过预见机制捕获未来路径走向，可有效解决交叉口转移选择并保证路网一致性，为生成式路径规划提供了新范式。

Abstract: Trajectory generation underpins urban applications such as traffic management and urban planning. Within road networks, vehicle movement along road segments is largely deterministic, whereas transitions at intersections are critical--multiple outgoing paths may appear equally plausible, yet they can lead to substantially different downstream routes. Existing generative methods such as diffusion models simulate location sequences and fail short of next transition selection, even producing trajectories that drift off the road network. To fill this gap, we advocate a fundamental shift: moving beyond location simulation toward behavior modeling on the road network. In this paper, we revisit trajectory generation through the lens of generative route planning, and introduce a new graph-constrained generative paradigm, Anticipatory Generative Modeling, which resolves next transition selection by incorporating the Anticipation of how the future route may unfold toward the destination. Concretely, we propose a novel generative model named Foresight that formulates a probabilistic model for an "anticipate--select--update--re-anticipate" loop that enables longer-term foresight while continuing sequential generation along the historical trajectory. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Foresight wins state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [54] [DP-MIND: Differentially private memory-guided Long context Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13132)
*Zhihao Liu,Yitian Cheng,Shuang Liu,Rui Wen,Yuke Hu,Zhan Qin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 DP-MIND，一种记忆引导的差分隐私推理框架，将隐私保护推理扩展至百万级 token 长上下文，并在可扩展性与效用上优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 即服务模式下，用户查询可能包含敏感或机密内容，存在严重隐私风险。差分隐私（DP）可提供原理性保护，但现有 DP 推理方法主要面向短或中等上下文，在百万级 token 输入下受限于上下文容量和处理扰动导致的效用严重下降。

Method: DP-MIND 在本地对输入进行扰动，并以紧凑记忆逐块处理长序列，从而保留跨序列的任务相关信息；为使记忆对 DP 噪声鲁棒，采用强化学习直接在扰动输入上优化记忆构建策略，奖励信号结合最终答案正确性与记忆一致性；此外引入本地去噪模块，在不将原始上下文暴露给外部模型的前提下恢复被扰动破坏的答案片段。文中还给出理论隐私分析。

Result: 在多个长上下文基准上（上下文长度达百万 token 规模）进行了大量实证分析，DP-MIND 在可扩展性与效用两方面均持续优于现有 DP 推理与长上下文推理基线。

Conclusion: DP-MIND 通过记忆引导、RL 优化的记忆构建与本地去噪，实现了百万 token 级长上下文下的差分隐私推理，兼顾隐私保障与实用性。

Abstract: LLM-as-a-Service has become a dominant paradigm for accessing large language models without requiring local deployment. However, the process raises severe privacy concerns when user queries may include sensitive or confidential contexts. Differential privacy (DP) provides a principled approach to protecting such inputs, but existing DP inference methods are primarily designed for short- or moderate-context settings and struggle with million-token-scale inputs due to limited context capacity and severe utility degradation under DP perturbation.
  In this paper, we propose DP-MIND, a memory-guided framework that scales differentially private inference to million-token contexts. DP-MIND enables private long-context inference by locally perturbing the input and processing it chunk by chunk with a compact memory that preserves task-relevant information across the sequence. To make this memory robust to DP noise, we optimize the memory construction policy directly on perturbed inputs using reinforcement learning, guided by both final answer correctness and memory consistency. We further recover perturbation-corrupted answer spans through a local denoising module without exposing the original context to the external model. We provide theoretical privacy analysis of DP-MIND and further conduct extensive empirical analysis across multiple long-context benchmarks with context lengths up to the million-token scale, demonstrating that DP-MIND consistently outperforms existing DP and long-context inference baselines in both scalability and utility.

</details>


### [55] [Competence-Aware Orchestration for LLM-Based Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13144)
*Songmiao Wang,Yuchen Wang,Chenglin Yu,Hongxia Yang,Ming Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出两阶段"能力感知"框架，根据LLM骨干在当次查询上的任务条件能力，自适应调整多智能体系统的角色激活与通信图，实现"按需协作"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM多智能体系统虽能通过角色分工与协作提升复杂推理，但协作并非越多越好：更丰富的协调对难题有帮助，却带来额外token开销与冗余交互。因此核心挑战是——如何让协作程度适配骨干模型在当前查询上的能力水平。

Method: 两阶段能力感知的MAS自动编排框架：第一阶段建模LLM骨干针对当前查询的"任务条件能力"（task-conditioned competence）信号；第二阶段依据该信号自适应地控制角色激活（role activation）与通信图（communication graph）结构。

Result: 在六个基准上平均得分93.81%。在六种不同能力水平的LLM骨干上：高能力骨干平均准确率从87.22提升至89.97，同时token成本降低36.26%；低能力骨干相比现有方法再平均提升4.58分。该趋势在未见过的骨干上同样成立。

Conclusion: 以能力估计驱动MAS编排，可在高能力骨干上实现"提精度+降成本"的双赢，在低能力骨干上带来显著精度增益，且具备跨骨干泛化性，验证了"协作强度应与骨干能力匹配"这一设计原则的有效性。

Abstract: LLM-based multi-agent systems (MAS) have emerged as a promising paradigm for enhancing complex reasoning through role specialization and collaboration, yet a key challenge remains: how can collaboration be adapted to a backbone's competence on the current query? This problem matters because more collaboration is not always better--richer coordination may help on difficult cases, but it also incurs additional token cost and redundant interactions. We propose a two-stage competence-aware framework for automatic MAS orchestration. It first models the LLM backbone's task-conditioned competence for the current query, and then uses this signal to adapt role activation and the communication graph. Across six benchmarks, our method achieves an average score of 93.81%. Experiments with six LLM backbones of varying competence levels further show that our method adjusts collaboration according to the inferred competence: for higher-competence backbones, it improves average accuracy from 87.22 to 89.97 while reducing token cost by 36.26%; for lower-competence backbones, it yields a further average gain of 4.58 points over existing methods. The same trend also holds on unseen backbones.

</details>


### [56] [VIGOR: Verifiable Industrial Geometry Optimization and Repair in Scene Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13145)
*Fuqiang Wang,Zheng Guo,Xiaoming Wu,Hanlin Kong,Nayu Liu,Chuanting Zhang,Daojun Liang,Jingxuan Wei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文定义了"可执行性-工程可行性差距"，构建了工业场景程序数据集，并提出VIGOR方法，通过监督生成、执行验证与分阶段几何修复，实现高完整可行性的场景程序生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型能生成可执行的场景程序，但可执行不等于工程可行：最终状态可能违反边界、碰撞、间隙、通道或结构等耦合约束。局部合理的摆放可能因修正一个设备而在别处引发违规，导致全局不可行。作者旨在使这一差距在受控条件下可测量并加以解决。

Method: 构建了基于执行的工业场景程序数据集，含1,749个可构建场景、完整可执行程序及每场景94项最终状态几何检查；提出VIGOR，结合监督式场景程序生成、基于执行的验证与分阶段几何精修：监督微调实现完整程序生成，局部投影修复可独立纠正的违规，联合冲突图修复协调剩余的多目标冲突并保持结构关系。

Result: VIGOR达到96.86%的完整可行性（Complete Feasibility）和91.20的整体得分（Overall），在Overall上比最强基线高出10.42分。

Conclusion: 在受控工业布局条件下，可靠的场景程序生成不仅需要可执行程序，还需对耦合工程约束进行显式验证与协同求解。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly generate structured layouts and executable scene programs from natural-language instructions. However, executability does not imply engineering feasibility: an industrial scene program may execute successfully while its final state still violates coupled boundary, collision, clearance, aisle, or structural constraints. We identify this mismatch as the executability-engineering-feasibility gap, where locally plausible placements can remain globally infeasible because corrections to one device may introduce violations elsewhere. To make this gap measurable in a controlled setting, we construct an execution-grounded industrial scene-program dataset with 1,749 constructible scenarios, complete executable programs, and 94 final-state geometric checks per scenario. We further propose VIGOR, which combines supervised scene-program generation with execution-grounded verification and staged geometric refinement. Supervised fine-tuning establishes complete program generation, local projection resolves independently correctable violations, and joint conflict-graph repair coordinates residual multi-object conflicts while preserving structural relations. Experiments show that VIGOR achieves 96.86% Complete Feasibility and an Overall score of 91.20, outperforming the strongest evaluated baseline by 10.42 points in Overall. These results show that, under controlled industrial layout conditions, reliable scene-program generation requires not only executable programs, but also explicit verification and coordinated resolution of coupled engineering constraints.

</details>


### [57] [Quality, Not Just Outcome: A Structural Scoring Framework for Selecting Coding-Agent Trajectories for Supervised Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13152)
*Sida Li,Yuanxin Liu,Shicheng Li,Linli Yao,Xu Sun,Haoli Bai,Xiaohui Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出TQS（轨迹质量分数）结构评分框架，通过信息聚焦、意图-行动对齐和策略动态三维度刻画编码智能体轨迹质量，无需LLM评判且线性时间运行；在155,144条成功轨迹上排序筛选后微调，Top-ranked子集训练的模型在SWE-Bench-Verified上优于随机或底层子集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于监督微调的编码智能体仅以最终补丁是否通过测试套件进行结果级筛选，忽视过程质量：成功轨迹中仍可能包含重复文件检查、工具使用不稳定、修改行为延迟等低效交互模式，这些都会被当作模仿目标学习。

Method: 提出TQS（Trajectory Quality Score）结构评分框架，直接作用于轨迹结构，以"结构化收敛"为核心，通过信息聚焦（information focus）、意图-行动对齐（intent-action alignment）、策略动态（policy dynamics）三类结构信号刻画质量；无需LLM评判器，线性时间复杂度运行。基于公共开放许可语料中155,144条成功轨迹，按TQS排序并筛选子集进行微调。

Result: 在SWE-Bench-Verified上，使用TQS高排名轨迹训练的编码智能体一致优于使用随机或低排名子集训练的模型。

Conclusion: 过程级轨迹质量是编码智能体监督中的重要因素；基于结构的轨迹筛选为LLM驱动的数据选择提供了一种有效且可扩展的替代方案。

Abstract: Open coding agents are commonly trained through supervised fine-tuning on interaction trajectories whose final patches pass the benchmark test suite. However, this outcome-level filtering criterion ignores process quality: trajectories that eventually succeed may still contain inefficient interaction patterns such as repetitive file inspection, unstable tool usage, or delayed modification behavior, all of which become part of the imitation target. We propose TQS (Trajectory Quality Score), a structural scoring framework for coding-agent trajectories that operates directly on trajectory structure, requires no LLM judge, and runs in linear time. TQS characterizes trajectory quality through structural signals organized around structured convergence: information focus, intent--action alignment, and policy dynamics. Using 155,144 successful trajectories from a public open-license corpus, we rank trajectories by TQS and fine-tune coding agents on filtered subsets. Models trained on top-ranked trajectories consistently outperform those trained on random or bottom-ranked subsets on SWE-Bench-Verified. Our results suggest that process-level trajectory quality is an important factor in coding-agent supervision and that structural trajectory filtering provides an effective and scalable alternative to LLM-based data selection.

</details>


### [58] [RoleWorld: Knowledge Transfer in World Modeling with Functional Role Abstractions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13172)
*Boyuan Xiao,Pu Wang,Ji Feng,Yao-Xiang Ding*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 RoleWorld，一种基于功能角色抽象的符号世界模型，通过可复用角色原型与环境特定参数值表示物体交互行为，实现跨环境的知识迁移与鲁棒泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有符号规则或可执行程序形式的世界模型虽具组合性、理论上支持迁移，但仍围绕环境特定物体组织知识，导致难以高效迁移到交互结构与底层动态相似的新环境。

Method: RoleWorld 使用可复用原型加环境特定参数值构成功能角色抽象。由教师 LLM 从交互轨迹中抽取角色原型、定义在角色实例上的可执行转移规则，以及指导 actor LLM 决策的技能。在目标环境中无教师参与，actor 结合该技能、当前角色分配与参数估计进行决策，并在不确定性影响决策时主动通过交互检验角色实例假设；每次动作反馈在线精化角色分配与参数估计，不更新 LLM 参数且转移规则保持固定。

Result: 在 MARS 基准上，RoleWorld 在物体行为显著改变的环境中实现稳健泛化，表明功能角色抽象可作为世界建模知识迁移的有效组件。

Conclusion: 功能角色抽象能够有效支撑世界模型中的知识迁移，为 LLM 智能体在结构相似但物体行为不同的环境间泛化提供可行方案。

Abstract: World models enable large language model (LLM) agents to anticipate action effects and make informed decisions. For generalization, it is desirable for such world models to transfer efficiently to new environments with similar interaction structure and underlying dynamics. Recent approaches encode world dynamics as symbolic rules or executable programs, whose compositional structure in principle affords transfer, but still organize such knowledge around environment-specific objects. To address this limitation, we introduce RoleWorld, a symbolic world modeling approach which uses reusable prototypes and environment-specific parameter values as functional role abstractions to represent how objects behave in interactions. From interaction trajectories, a teacher LLM extracts role prototypes and executable transition rules defined over role instances, together with a skill that guides the actor LLM to use role information in decision making. In a target world, without the teacher, the actor uses this skill together with its current role assignments and parameter estimates, and actively tests a role instance hypothesis through interaction when its uncertainty affects the current decision. Feedback from each action refines role assignments and estimates parameter values online, with no LLM parameter updates and the transition rules held fixed. Experiments on MARS benchmark show that RoleWorld enables robust generalization across environments with significantly altered object behavior, suggesting that functional role abstractions can serve as an effective component for knowledge transfer in world modeling. Project Page: https://future-item.github.io/RoleWorld.

</details>


### [59] [AdaC-GRPO: Towards Mastering Long-Horizon Visual Sudoku Puzzles via Adaptive Curriculum Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13184)
*Sida Li,Yuanxin Liu,Linli Yao,Shicheng Li,Xu Sun,Haoli Bai,Xiaohui Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出细粒度基准 FG-Sudoku 与自适应课程学习算法 AdaC-GRPO，解决长程多模态推理任务中 RLVR 的奖励稀疏问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR 虽能提升大语言模型推理能力，但在长程多模态任务中效果尚不明确。视觉数独是代表性挑战，需要同时满足局部约束与全局依赖链的多步演绎推理。前沿多模态大模型在复杂长程依赖（如 9*9 数独）上近乎零成功率，导致后训练阶段奖励极度稀疏，标准 RLVR 难以启动。

Method: 1) 构建 FG-Sudoku 基准，按网格规模与所需演绎步数进行细粒度分类；2) 提出 AdaC-GRPO，不依赖固定或先验课程，而是通过在线 rollout 准确率动态追踪模型的"最近发展区"，据此构造训练批次。

Result: AdaC-GRPO 使 Qwen2.5-VL-7B 的性能接近 Gemini-2.5-Flash，并弥合了与 Gemini-2.5-Pro 之间 62.2% 的整体准确率差距；在 InternVL-3.5 与 Qwen3-VL 上进一步验证了泛化性。

Conclusion: 面向长程多模态推理，基于最近发展区的自适应课程数据构造能有效缓解奖励稀疏，显著提升模型在视觉数独等复杂任务上的表现，并具备跨模型泛化能力。

Abstract: While reinforcement learning with verifiable reward (RLVR) enhances reasoning in large language models, its efficacy in long-horizon multimodal tasks remains underexplored. Visual Sudoku puzzles pose a representative challenge, requiring multi-step deductive reasoning over both local constraints and global dependency chains. To this end, we introduce FG-Sudoku, a fine-grained benchmark categorized by grid size and required deductive steps. Evaluations reveal that frontier MLLMs fail on complex long-range dependencies (e.g., 9*9 puzzles). During post-training, this near-zero success rate causes severe reward sparsity, halting standard RLVR. We propose Adaptive Curriculum Group Relative Policy Optimization (AdaC-GRPO) to overcome this. Unlike rigid or prior-based schedules, AdaC-GRPO dynamically constructs training batches by tracking the model's "Zone of Proximal Development" via online rollout accuracy. Experiments demonstrate that AdaC-GRPO enables Qwen2.5-VL-7B to approach the performance of Gemini-2.5-Flash while bridging 62.2% of the overall accuracy gap to Gemini-2.5-Pro, with its generalizability further validated across InternVL-3.5 and Qwen3-VL.

</details>


### [60] [JANUS: Enhancing Long-Horizon Autonomous Research with Co-Evolving World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13194)
*Mengdi Liu,Chenghao Jia,Hong Chang,Mingjie He,Shiguang Shan,Xilin Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 JANUS 框架，用协同演化的世界模型驱动"预测-验证-演化"循环，通过在执行前预测候选价值并剪枝，缓解长周期自主研究中反馈延迟问题，在 MLS-Bench-Lite 与真实任务上超越 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长周期自主研究（Generate-Execute-Feedback 范式）依赖被动试错，昂贵的执行带来严重反馈延迟，在搜索空间爆炸下拖慢演化进度。

Method: JANUS 在演化图中：研究智能体提出候选，世界模型预测价值提升、信息增益与探索效用并打分，据此在选择、精炼或剪枝后再执行；获得真实反馈后蒸馏技能，包括研究智能体的分层技能、世界模型的探索策略技能及二者协调协议。

Result: 在 MLS-Bench-Lite 与真实研究任务上显著优于 SOTA 基线，同时获得更高的演化效率与演化上限。

Conclusion: 通过预测-验证-演化实现持续协同演化——研究智能体提升候选生成能力，世界模型精炼长时程预测判断力，从而验证了在执行前用世界模型筛选候选的范式有效性。

Abstract: Long-horizon autonomous research has shown promise in accelerating scientific progress through sustained cycles of hypothesis generation, implementation, and validation. However, this Generate-Execute-Feedback paradigm relies on reactive trial-and-error, where costly and time-consuming executions introduce severe feedback delays, throttling evolutionary progress under explosively expanding search space. To address this challenge, we propose JANUS, a framework that unifies future outcomes forecasting and past experience reflection through a Predict-Verify-Evolve loop driven by a co-evolving world model. Within an evolving graph, the research agent proposes candidates while world model scores them by forecasting value improvement, information gain, and exploration utility, then accordingly selects, refines, or prunes branches prior to execution. After receiving ground-truth feedback, JANUS distills experience into evolving skills: hierarchical skills for research agent, exploration-strategy skills for world model, and their coordination protocols. This mechanism enables continual co-evolution: the research agent sharpens candidate generation while the world model refines its predictive judgment over extended horizons. Extensive evaluations on MLS-Bench-Lite and real research tasks demonstrate that JANUS significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, achieving both higher evolving efficiency and ceiling.

</details>


### [61] [Forgetting is Not Always Bad: A Neuro-Inspired Memory Repair Mechanism for Poisoned LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13267)
*Lei Liu,Yunji Liang,Xiaowen Zhang,Jingqi Liu,Qi Li,Bin Guo,Zhiwen Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出免训练的智能体内在记忆修复框架DREAM，借助"弱再激活致记忆衰减"的神经科学机制，实现对大模型智能体中毒记忆的选择性功能遗忘，在高效低耗的同时显著提升鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体易受记忆投毒攻击，而现有防御依赖外部模块进行静态记忆隔离或持续在线审计，导致记忆效率低、计算开销大。受神经科学中"弱再激活可在缺乏强化时使记忆失稳并诱发衰减"的机制启发，作者希望构建一种智能体自身内在、免训练的记忆修复方案。

Method: DREAM采用三阶段流水线：（1）扰动诱导的隐式再激活，揭示恶意记忆的结构脆弱性；（2）基于多维激活模式的自适应异常诊断，检测拓扑异常；（3）动态记忆修复模块，选择性抑制有害记忆并强化良性记忆。整体为免训练的agent-intrinsic框架。

Result: 在多种攻击者和真实任务上，针对后门投毒将攻击成功率降低95%以上，同时保持较强的良性效用；对注入攻击也具备有竞争力的鲁棒性。运行时间最高加速2.13倍，token消耗较A-MemGuard降低37.7%；在中毒多智能体系统上任务成功率达92.96%。

Conclusion: DREAM通过类脑的"弱再激活—记忆衰减"机制，实现了免训练、智能体内在的选择性记忆修复，兼顾防御有效性与计算效率，为LLM智能体记忆安全提供了一种高效低耗的新范式。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are highly vulnerable to memory-poisoning attacks. Existing defenses primarily rely on external modules for either static memory isolation or continuous online auditing, resulting in low memory efficiency and high computational overhead. To address these limitations, inspired by neuroscientific mechanisms that weak reactivation can destabilize memories and induce decay in the absence of reinforcement, we propose an agent-intrinsic training-free memory repair framework, DREAM (Dynamic Reactivation for Engram Attenuation in Memory), that enables selective functional forgetting of poisoned memories. Specifically, DREAM implements a three-stage pipeline: perturbation-induced implicit reactivation to reveal the structural fragility of malicious memories, adaptive anomaly diagnosis based on multi-dimensional activation patterns to detect topological anomalies, and a dynamic memory repair module that selectively suppresses harmful memories while reinforcing benign ones. Extensive experiments across diverse attackers and real-world tasks demonstrate that DREAM reduces attack success rates by over 95\% against backdoor poisoning while preserving strong benign utility. DREAM also achieves competitive robustness against injection attacks. In terms of runtime and token consumption, DREAM achieves up to a 2.13$\times$ speedup and a 37.7\% reduction in token consumption compared with A-MemGuard. Furthermore, DREAM also achieves a task success rate of 92.96\% on poisoned multi-agent systems.

</details>


### [62] [BoundaryEvo: Data-Model Co-Evolution at the Capability Boundary for Long-Chain Reasoning Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13285)
*Jiaming Su,Suorong Yang,Xudong Li,Tengchao Yang,Peng Ye*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出两阶段数据-模型协同演化框架 BoundaryEvo：先蒸馏少量教师长链推理轨迹建立学生基础推理能力，再围绕学生演化中的能力边界反复筛选"学生未掌握但专家能可靠解决"的问题，仅用1000条训练样例即在11个推理基准上稳定超越静态与动态数据选择基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有从大教师模型蒸馏长链推理轨迹的方法通常在蒸馏前选定固定训练集，忽视了推理样例的有效性会随学生能力演化而变化。当可用教师轨迹数量有限时，这种不匹配代价尤为高昂：已掌握的样例提供的监督信号有限，而远超学生能力的样例又难以学习。因此训练数据应随模型能力演化而自适应，但学生初期能力弱时又难以进行可靠的数据选择。

Method: 提出 BoundaryEvo 两阶段数据-模型协同演化框架。第一阶段 Capability Priming：直接蒸馏一小组教师生成的长链推理轨迹，不引入独立模型进行判定与过滤，使学生在能力感知的数据选择之前先获得初始推理能力。第二阶段 Capability-Boundary Co-Evolution：反复筛选当前学生尚未掌握、但专家能可靠求解的问题，并随学生能力演化持续更新训练数据。

Result: 仅使用1000条训练样例，BoundaryEvo 在11个推理基准上稳定优于静态与动态数据选择基线，将 Qwen3.5-4B 的准确率从 IMO-AnswerBench 的62.9提升至75.3，从 AMO-Bench 的26.8提升至46.0。代码与数据将公开。

Conclusion: 训练数据应随学生能力边界协同演化；先奠定基础推理能力、再进行能力感知的数据选择，可有效缓解固定训练集与模型演化之间的错配。BoundaryEvo 以极少量训练样例（1000条）实现显著且稳定的推理性能提升，验证了数据-模型协同演化范式的有效性与数据效率。

Abstract: Distilling long-chain reasoning trajectories from large teacher models provides an effective way to improve the reasoning ability of small language models. However, existing methods typically select a fixed training set before distillation, overlooking that the usefulness of a reasoning example changes as the student evolves. This mismatch becomes especially costly when only a small number of teacher trajectories can be used for training. Besides, examples already mastered provide little new supervision, while examples far beyond the capability of student may be difficult to learn from. Training data should therefore adapt as model capability evolves, yet reliable data selection is difficult when the student is initially weak. To address these challenges, our key idea is to first establish the basic reasoning capability and then adapt the training data to the evolving capability boundary of the student. We propose BoundaryEvo, a two-stage data--model co-evolution framework. In Capability Priming, we directly distill a small set of teacher-generated long reasoning trajectories without using a separate model to judge and filter them, allowing the student to acquire an initial reasoning capability before capability-aware data selection. In Capability-Boundary Co-Evolution, we repeatedly select questions that the current student has not mastered but the expert can reliably solve, and update the training data as the student evolves. With only 1,000 training examples, BoundaryEvo consistently outperforms static and dynamic data selection baselines across 11 reasoning benchmarks, raising the original Qwen3.5-4B accuracy from 62.9 to 75.3 on IMO-AnswerBench and from 26.8 to 46.0 on AMO-Bench. The code and data will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [63] [Masked Diffusion Language Agents for Tool-Integrated Chemical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13330)
*Mengdi Liu,Chenghao Jia,Hong Chang,Shiguang Shan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 ChemDiffAgent——首个用于工具集成化学推理的掩码扩散语言智能体，将化学推理重构为对交替推理与工具使用轨迹的迭代去噪，并配套两阶段后训练框架与综合基准，性能较自回归智能体提升约20%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向复杂化学任务的工具集成推理智能体系统设计仍是基础性挑战。现有化学智能体虽通过外部工具集成展现出潜力，但基于自回归范式，受因果注意力与从左到右生成的固有限制，难以处理双向分子理解、全局协同的工具规划与多源证据整合。

Method: 提出 ChemDiffAgent，首个掩码扩散语言智能体：把化学推理重构为对交替的推理—工具使用轨迹进行迭代去噪，使工具使用决策在全轨迹上下文下联合确定，从而更好对齐决策边界与知识边界；分析该范式的优势来源，即训练时更难的填充子问题与推理时灵活的任意顺序解码；构建两阶段后训练框架，包括智能体监督微调与方差缩减的偏好优化；并建立覆盖六项化学任务、涵盖单轮与多轮工具使用场景的综合基准。

Result: 在匹配预算与评估条件下，ChemDiffAgent 持续优于自回归智能体，整体提升约20%；8B 模型性能可比甚至在某些情况下超越更大规模的前沿模型（如 GPT 5.5、Claude Opus 4.7）。

Conclusion: 掩码扩散语言建模范式为工具集成化学推理提供了优于自回归范式的路径，通过全轨迹上下文决策与两阶段后训练显著增强工具使用与推理能力，并结合综合基准验证了其效率与效果。

Abstract: Designing tool-integrated reasoning agent systems for complex chemical tasks remains a fundamental challenge. Although recent chemical agents have demonstrated promising performance through the integration of external tools, they are still built on the autoregressive paradigm, which is inherently constrained by causal attention and left-to-right generation. As a result, they often struggle with bidirectional molecular understanding, global coordinated tool planning and multi-source evidence integration. To address this challenge, we introduce ChemDiffAgent, the first masked diffusion language agent for tool-integrated chemical reasoning. It reformulates chemical reasoning as iterative denoising over interleaved reasoning and tool-use trajectories, enabling tool-use decisions to be jointly determined under full-trajectory context and thereby better aligning the agent's decision boundary with its knowledge boundary. We further analyze how this paradigm benefits agentic reasoning through harder infilling subproblems during training and flexible arbitrary-order decoding during inference. Then we develop a two-stage post-training framework, comprising agentic supervised fine-tuning and variance-reduced preference optimization, to enhance tool-use and reasoning capabilities. We also construct a comprehensive benchmark covering six chemistry tasks across both single-turn and multi-turn tool-use scenarios. Under matched budgets and evaluation, ChemDiffAgent consistently outperforms autoregressive agents, achieving an overall improvement of approximately 20%. Notably, our 8B model achieves performance comparable to, in some cases surpassing, significantly larger frontier models, such as GPT 5.5 and Claude Opus 4.7. Code is available at https://github.com/bilemond/ChemDiffAgent.

</details>


### [64] [AlphaNIST: Emergent Assembly Skills from Binary Task Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13354)
*Octi Zhang,Iretiayo Akinola,Rosario Scalise,Mateo Guaman Castro,Patrick Yin,Tobias Widmer,Miles Macklin,Chris Amevor,Dylan Turpin,Dirk Van Gelder,Jan Carius,Ankur Handa,Gavriel State,Yunrong Guo,Yashraj Narang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出AlphaNIST框架，仅用稀疏二值奖励与任务多样性，从零训练单一策略完成NIST装配任务板#1的全部20项任务，训练成功率98.2%，无需示教、进度塑形、脚本基元或按任务调参。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NIST装配任务板#1对学习型方法极具挑战：短时程需精密富接触控制小尺寸紧配合零件，长时程需跨长动作序列的恢复与协调。以往依赖示教、任务分解与稠密进度奖励来提供"如何装配"的指导，但究竟需要规定多少策略仍不明确。受AlphaGo到AlphaGo Zero的启发，作者希望验证任务无关的稀疏二值奖励加上任务多样性，是否也能让机器人自行发现装配策略。

Method: 在仿真环境中，使用稀疏二值任务奖励与跨任务多样性，从零训练单一策略覆盖全部20个装配任务；不使用示教、进度塑形奖励、脚本基元或逐任务调参。进一步训练策略在单回合内完成十组装配对，不规定顺序，仅当十组同时完成才给予成功奖励。

Result: 单一策略在全20项任务上达到98.2%总体训练成功率；十组装配对任务亦可成功完成。学习到的策略表现出灵巧性与类规划行为：为后续装配选择合适抓取、利用周围几何结构重新定向零件、先准备一个零件再装配另一个后返回完成第一个，且无显式规划器。

Conclusion: 表明任务无关的稀疏二值奖励配合任务多样性，可让机器人在无示教、无塑形、无基元、无按任务调参的条件下自行发现富接触装配与长时程协调策略，验证了类似AlphaGo Zero式规模化强化学习在机器人装配中的可行性。

Abstract: The NIST Assembly Task Board #1 remains a challenging benchmark for learning-based methods in robotic manipulation. Over short horizons, it demands precise, contact-rich control of small, tight-fitting parts. Over long horizons, it requires recovery and coordination across extended action sequences. Demonstrations, task decomposition, and dense progress rewards have enabled research advances by providing guidance on how to assemble. But how much of the strategy must be specified? The progression from AlphaGo to AlphaGo Zero showed that RL at scale can discover superhuman strategies without prescribing how to win. AlphaNIST asks whether a sparse binary task reward and task diversity admitted by this task-agnostic reward can similarly let a robot discover how to assemble. In simulation, we train a single policy from scratch across all 20 assembly tasks on the NIST Assembly Task Board #1, reaching 98.2% overall training success without demonstrations, progress shaping, scripted primitives, or per-task tuning. The framework also trains policies to complete ten assembly pairs in a single episode without a prescribed order, with task success rewarded only when all ten pairs are simultaneously complete. The learned policies discover dexterous and planning-like behaviors: choosing grasps for later assembly, using surrounding geometry to reorient parts, and preparing one part, assembling another, then returning to finish the first without an explicit planner. For videos, see our website: https://alpha-nist.github.io/

</details>


### [65] [SWE-Critix: Post-Training Generalist Verifiers for Assessing Coding-Agent Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13356)
*Jiajun Hu,Chun Yong Chong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 SWE-Critix，一个针对编码智能体轨迹的通用验证器后训练流程，通过 SFT+RL 训练 30B 模型，在留出测试集上将完成判定准确率从基线的 57% 提升至 78%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体越来越多用于端到端软件工程任务，但缺乏现成的 oracle 评估器来定义成功标准，因此需要能够可靠判定并解释智能体行为的验证器。然而，长时程任务的执行轨迹极长（包含数百次与代码库和外部工具的迭代交互），现有验证器要么依赖无专门训练的通用 LLM，要么训练只输出标量奖励的专用 critic，基于训练的通用验证研究仍较有限。

Method: SWE-Critix 是一个后训练流程：给定任务及其执行轨迹，训练出的验证器预测完成标签并生成自然语言分析以解释其判断。流程结合监督微调（SFT）与强化学习（RL），在 CodeForge-Preview 的轨迹上训练一系列 30B 模型。

Result: 在留出测试集上，分类准确率从基座 Qwen3-30B-A3B 推理模型的 57%，经 SFT 提升至 66%，再经 RL 提升至 78%。

Conclusion: SWE-Critix 证明了基于训练的通用验证器在编码智能体长轨迹判定任务上的有效性，SFT 与 RL 阶段均带来显著增益。作者开源了模型、数据集与训练代码以促进后续研究。

Abstract: Coding agents are increasingly deployed for end-to-end software engineering tasks, where a readily available oracle evaluator that defines the success criteria is often absent. This motivates verifications for coding agents that can reliably judge and explain their behavior. However, verification is non-trivial as coding agents are increasingly capable of solving long-horizon software engineering tasks. Their execution trajectories can be extremely long, containing hundreds of iterative interactions with code repositories and external tools. Existing verifiers either rely on general-purpose LLMs without specialized training or train dedicated critics that predominantly focus on producing scalar rewards, highlighting the relatively limited exploration of training-based generalist verification. We introduce SWE-Critix, a post-training pipeline for training generalist verifiers of coding-agent trajectories. Given a task and its execution trajectory, the learned verifier predicts the completion label and generates a natural-language analysis that explains its judgment. Our pipeline combines supervised fine-tuning (SFT) with reinforcement learning (RL) and trains a series of 30B models on trajectories from CodeForge-Preview. On a held-out test set, SWE-Critix improves classification accuracy from 57% for the base Qwen3-30B-A3B reasoning model to 66% after SFT and 78% after RL. We release our models, datasets, and training code to facilitate further research.

</details>


### [66] [RegulaTutor: A Theory-Informed Framework for Long-Term Personalized Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13399)
*Yonggang Li,Boqian Han,Rui Sheng,Yushi Sun,Zixin Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出RegulaTutor框架，将学习者的历史证据、学习者解释与教学指令显式连接，并配套PairRoute离线精炼流程，用于长期个性化辅导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的辅导系统虽利用记忆与检索实现个性化，但历史证据与教学策略之间的教学论依据往往不明确。

Method: 受自我调节学习（SRL）研究指导，其辅导机（harness）以认知、情感、元认知与动机（CAMM）框架为依据，推导有证据支持的学习者解释与教学策略；策略保留与证据的关联并随新观察更新。PairRoute利用中间执行记录区分harness、模型及混合失败，提出并评估针对性harness修订，再为选定harness下确认的模型失败构建经审核的阶段级监督。精炼重点为历史证据获取。

Result: 在被评估的模型配置上，RegulaTutor平均提升了历史证据准确性、学习者诊断与教学质量的评判结果；PairRoute在精炼轨迹上使独立模型的历史证据准确率获得端点提升。

Conclusion: 结果表明RegulaTutor有望通过随学习者历史演进的教学策略及系统性改进，支持终身个性化教育。

Abstract: Long-term personalized tutoring requires language models to translate a learner's evolving history into appropriate instructional support. Existing LLM-based tutoring systems use memory and retrieval to personalize responses, but the pedagogical rationale linking historical evidence to teaching strategies is not always explicit. We introduce RegulaTutor, a theory-informed framework for long-term personalized tutoring that connects historical evidence, learner interpretation, and learner-facing instruction. Guided by self-regulated learning (SRL) research, its tutoring harness operationalizes a cognitive, affective, metacognitive, and motivational (CAMM) framework for deriving evidence-supported learner interpretations and teaching policies. The resulting policies retain links to their supporting evidence and are updated as new observations arrive. This staged workflow also supports systematic refinement through PairRoute, an offline procedure that uses intermediate execution records to distinguish harness, model, and mixed failures. PairRoute proposes and evaluates targeted harness revisions, then constructs reviewed stage-level supervision for confirmed model failures remaining under the selected harness. We focus this refinement on historical evidence acquisition, which underpins personalized instruction and allows outputs to be checked against learning records and reference answers. RegulaTutor improves historical-evidence accuracy, learner diagnosis, and judged teaching quality on average across evaluated model configurations. PairRoute results show an endpoint improvement in standalone-model historical-evidence accuracy over the refinement trajectory. Together, these results highlight RegulaTutor's potential to support lifelong personalized education through instructional policies that evolve with learner histories and targeted improvements to the tutoring system.

</details>


### [67] [Local Diagnostics of Learned Event Representations for Anomaly Detection at the HL-LHC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13437)
*Donatella Genovese,Philip Ploner,Simone Scardapane,Stefano Giagu,Thea Klaeboe Aarrestad*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文在高亮度LHC模拟数据集COLLIDE-2V上评估监督与自监督神经嵌入策略用于对撞机异常检测，并以局部诊断流程对触发事件进行可解释性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异常检测是模型无关的新物理搜索策略，可避免先验地依赖手工构造的观测量；但在低维表示中搜索稀有事件会牺牲物理可解释性。为进入发现流程，被标记事件必须能被局部诊断，以区分新物理与建模误差或探测器效应，并识别可用于后续靶向搜索的观测量。

Method: 在750M事件的COLLIDE-2V数据集（真实高亮度LHC条件模拟）上，比较监督与自监督神经嵌入策略；以学习到的潜在空间中自编码器的重构误差作为异常分数。此外提出局部诊断流程：对SM潜在空间拟合高斯混合模型，无过程标签地通过高能观测量刻画各分量，定位被标记事件，并按局部判别力对观测量排序。

Result: 在所考虑的配置和基准信号中，利用模拟过程标签的监督目标总体优于自监督方法；其最佳配置优于直接在运动学特征上训练的自编码器，在所有留出过程上于10%本底接受率下提升信号效率。诊断流程的排序恢复了留出过程的特征运动学，同时将异常分数与相同观测量关联可量化分数跟踪它们的程度，揭示了最具判别力的观测量并非分数跟随最紧密的观测量。

Conclusion: 监督神经嵌入结合潜在空间重构误差的异常检测在高亮度LHC条件下具有优势；所提出的局部诊断流程可对标记事件提供物理可解释的定位与观测量排序，为异常检测进入发现流程提供支持。

Abstract: Anomaly detection, the identification of collision events that deviate from Standard Model expectations, is one of the most promising model-agnostic strategies for new-physics searches at the Large Hadron Collider. Searching for rare events in learned low-dimensional representations removes the need to commit to hand-crafted observables a priori, but sacrifices the immediate physical interpretation those observables provide. For anomaly detection to enter a discovery pipeline, flagged events must therefore be diagnosed locally, both to distinguish potential new physics from mismodeling or detector effects and to identify observables for targeted follow-up searches. We evaluate supervised and self-supervised neural embedding strategies on COLLIDE-2V, a 750-million-event dataset simulated under realistic High-Luminosity LHC conditions, using the reconstruction error of an autoencoder trained in the learned latent space as the anomaly score. Across the configurations and benchmark signals considered, supervised objectives, which exploit simulation process labels during training, generally outperform their self-supervised counterparts and, in their best configurations, improve on an autoencoder trained directly on kinematic features, raising the signal efficiency at 10% background acceptance on all held-out processes. We further introduce a local diagnostic pipeline that fits a Gaussian Mixture Model to the SM latent space, profiles each component through high-level observables without process labels, localizes the flagged events, and ranks observables by their local discriminating power. The ranking recovers the defining kinematics of the held-out processes, while correlating the anomaly score with the same observables quantifies how closely the score tracks them, revealing cases where the most discriminating observable is not the one the score follows most closely.

</details>


### [68] [Rollout-Time Intervention: Unlocking Agentic Reinforcement Learning Beyond Policy Limits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13438)
*Chen Xi,Ding Ruixue,Wang Zhigaoyuan,Zhang Fanrui,Zhang Qiang,Chen Boli,Wang Shihang,Wang Tianfu,Ren Ailin,Wang Pengyong,Xie Pengjun,Xiong Hui*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 Rollout-Time Intervention (RTI)，利用奖励侧证据在 rollout 阶段将错误轨迹转化为成功轨迹，突破 LLM agent 的 on-policy 推理瓶颈，实现能力边界外推。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
