Result: 当数据分布可被此类混合模型良好近似时，Transformer-based MDM以KL散度衡量达到$\varepsilon$-精确采样，所需训练样本为$\widetilde{O}(LK_{\mathrm{par}}/\varepsilon)$，其中$K_{\mathrm{par}}$是$L$、$V$、隐状态数与混合组件数的多项式。样本复杂度由潜在结构决定，而非$V^L$。

Conclusion: 研究给出了端到端理论保证，从潜在结构角度解释了MDM在真实数据上的有效性，为其经验成功提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: Masked diffusion models (MDMs) generate discrete data by iteratively unmasking multiple tokens in parallel using a learned mask predictor. Their output carries two errors: parallelization error due to parallel sampling, and estimation error from the mask predictor learned with finite samples. Existing theory treats these errors separately, and the available statistical bounds typically scale with the number $V^L$ of length-$L$ sequences over a vocabulary of size $V$, suffering from the curse of dimensionality. In this work, we establish end-to-end statistical guarantees showing that transformer-based MDMs learn structured distributions with a sample complexity governed by their latent structure. Specifically, we model this structure through mixtures of hidden Markov trees, which capture latent dependencies within components and heterogeneity across components. When the data distribution is well approximated by such a mixture, a transformer-based MDM with parallel unmasking schedules achieves $\varepsilon$-accurate sampling in KL divergence with $\widetilde{O}(LK_{\mathrm{par}}/\varepsilon)$ training samples, where $K_{\mathrm{par}}$ is polynomial in $L$, $V$, the number of hidden states, and the number of mixture components. The transformer network and sampling procedure require no knowledge of the tree topologies or component parameters, thereby adapting to the unknown latent structure automatically. Overall, our results provide theoretical justification for the empirical effectiveness of MDMs on real-world data.

</details>


### [103] [Sample-Adaptive Zeroth-Order Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12566)
*Haiyuan Wan,Yanyi Li,Weicheng Lin,Cong Fang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SAZO 提出一种样本自适应零阶优化方法，在线学习样本重要性并自适应分配查询，在无需额外模型评估、仅增加 O(N) 标量状态的条件下加速大模型微调收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零阶（ZO）优化在微调大语言模型时通常对所有训练样本均匀采样，忽略了样本重要性的动态变化，导致可避免的估计方差，拖慢收敛。

Method: SAZO 从已有的方向差分中提取梯度范数信息，在无真实梯度的情况下估计样本重要性；并用逐样本指数移动平均聚合多次更新中的稀疏小批量反馈，以跟踪动态变化的重要性。方法无需额外模型评估，相比 MeZO 仅增加 O(N) 标量状态。

Result: 理论证明 SAZO 渐近匹配最小方差采样 oracle 的主导收敛项，且收敛速率严格快于标准方法。在四个模型族上的实验显示，下游平均性能提升，达到相同训练损失所需查询数最多减少 6 倍（收敛速度最高提升 6×）。

Conclusion: SAZO 通过在线样本重要性估计与自适应查询分配，在不增加显著开销的前提下有效降低零阶优化方差、加速大模型微调，兼顾理论保证与实证效果。

Abstract: Zeroth-order (ZO) optimization offers a memory-efficient alternative to backpropagation for fine-tuning large language models. However, existing ZO methods typically sample training examples uniformly, overlooking their evolving importance and leaving avoidable estimation variance. To address this limitation, we propose Sample-Adaptive Zeroth-Order Optimization (SAZO), which learns sample importance online and adaptively allocates queries to accelerate convergence. SAZO extracts gradient-norm information from existing directional differences to estimate sample importance without access to true gradients. Furthermore, to track evolving importance from sparse minibatch feedback, SAZO maintains per-example exponential moving averages that aggregate observations across updates. The method requires no additional model evaluations and only $O(N)$ additional scalar state compared with MeZO. We prove that SAZO asymptotically matches the leading convergence term of the minimum-variance sampling oracle, and achieves a strictly faster convergence rate than standard methods. Extensive experiments across four model families demonstrate improved average downstream performance and up to $6\times$ faster convergence to the same training loss in terms of query count.

</details>


### [104] [An all-transistor physical-learning transformer can be trained to perform in-context learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12571)
*Michael C. Abbott,Benjamin B. Machta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该工作通过在双联电路中引入额外反向元件恢复平衡传播的双向信息流，利用亚阈值MOSFET的指数电流律构建注意力机制，并用交叉熵损失与新型浮动元件实现轻推，在仿真中完成任务内学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平衡传播与耦合学习类系统的表达能力与多层感知机同级，虽可为通用函数逼近器，却难以完成Transformer擅长的联想任务。其根源在于：平衡传播依赖输入输出间双向信息流，而实现点积注意力所需的乘法等复杂非线性会破坏反向流，因此需要显式恢复该反向通路。

Method: 在双联（dyadic）电路中为此目的引入额外电路元件，显式恢复双向流；利用亚阈值MOSFET的指数电流律构建注意力机制；采用与softmax同一MOSFET子电路的新型浮动电路元件生成适用于交叉熵损失的轻推（nudge）；用Lion优化器训练电路中的电阻，每参数一个电容存储历史梯度估计的局部记忆。

Result: 仿真表明，该电路可被训练完成一个简单的上下文学习任务；训练成功依赖于交叉熵损失而非均方误差；尽管反向元件仅传输近似梯度，训练仍可收敛。

Conclusion: 显式恢复反向流并结合指数律MOSFET、交叉熵轻推与Lion优化，可使物理电路实现可训练的注意力机制与上下文学习，为具有联想能力的物理学习系统提供可行路径。

Abstract: Most systems trained by equilibrium propagation or coupled learning are in the same class as multi-layer perceptrons: while they may be universal function approximators, they struggle to learn associative tasks which are easy for transformers. Equilibrium propagation relies on a bidirectional flow of information through the physical system between input and output. The reverse flow is broken by more complex nonlinear systems, such as those performing multiplication which is needed to express dot-product attention. Here we explicitly restore this bidirectional flow by introducing extra circuit elements for this purpose into a double or dyadic circuit. We then use these ideas to construct an attention mechanism exploiting the exponential current law of sub-threshold MOSFET devices. We show that in simulations this circuit can be trained to perform a simple in-context learning task, despite using realistic reverse components which transmit only an approximate gradient. For this training to succeed we need cross-entropy loss (not mean squared error). We generate nudges appropriate for this loss using a novel floating circuit element, based on the same MOSFET sub-circuit used for softmax. We train the resistors in this circuit using the Lion optimiser, which can be implemented using one capacitor per parameter to store local memory of past gradient estimates.

</details>


### [105] [DiffusionART: Efficient, Stable and Scalable Reinforcement of Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12572)
*Zhenglin Wan,Jiayu Chen,Zhengfeiyang Zhang,Pengfei Zhou,Wangbo Zhao,Fangyuan Kong,Pengfei Wan,Kun Gai,Yang You*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 DiffusionART，通过将每个 prompt 的奖励聚合为共享的奖励改进方向，并按构造消除前向-反向一致性力，同时用信任域掩码的第二次更新实现漂移自适应恢复，无需 EMA 调参，在图像和视频生成上训练高效稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线 RL 微调扩散模型存在效率与稳定性权衡：反向过程方法（如 Flow-GRPO）因信用分配不佳导致效率低；前向过程方法（如 DiffusionNFT）虽高效，但依赖精调的 EMA 离策略训练才稳定。作者指出 NFT 式目标隐含前向-反向一致性力，而正负联合训练只能部分抵消该力，EMA 的稳定作用其实是"恢复"而非依赖离策略 rollout。

Method: 提出 DiffusionART（Diffusion Reinforcement with Aggregation-First Reward Tilting）：将每个 prompt 的奖励聚合为反向过程状态共享的奖励改进方向，从构造上消除一致性力；并加入信任域掩码的第二次更新，实现漂移自适应恢复，无需 EMA 调参。方法不需要可微奖励模型，每个 prompt 所需样本更少，并支持成对奖励模型（DiffusionART-Pairwise）。

Result: 在图像与视频生成任务上，DiffusionART 无需逐任务调参即可高效稳定训练。在 HunyuanVideo-13B 上，以等权重视觉与运动质量奖励，DiffusionART 达到 DiffusionNFT 和 Flow-GRPO-Fast 后期平均奖励水平所需的 GPU 小时分别减少 12.02 倍和 11.72 倍，同时平均训练奖励分别高出 28.8% 和 53.0%。

Conclusion: DiffusionART 通过聚合优先的奖励倾斜与信任域恢复机制，解决了扩散模型在线 RL 微调中效率与稳定性的矛盾，具备无需 EMA、无需可微奖励、样本效率高、支持成对奖励等优势，并在大规模图像与视频生成上验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Online reinforcement learning (RL) fine-tunes diffusion models with black-box rewards, but reverse-process methods such as Flow-GRPO are limited in efficiency by poor credit assignment, while forward-process methods such as DiffusionNFT are efficient yet unstable without carefully tuned exponential-moving-average (EMA) off-policy training. We show that NFT-style objectives contain a hidden forward--reverse consistency force that joint positive--negative training cancels only partially, and that EMA stabilizes training through restoration rather than off-policy rollouts. We introduce DiffusionART (Diffusion Reinforcement with Aggregation-First Reward Tilting), which aggregates each prompt's rewards into a shared reward-improving direction for reverse-process states, removing the consistency force by construction, and adds a trust-region-masked second update for drift-adaptive restoration without EMA tuning. It requires no differentiable reward model, remains effective with fewer samples per prompt, and supports pairwise reward models (DiffusionART-Pairwise). Across image and video generation, DiffusionART trains efficiently and stably without per-task tuning. In particular, on HunyuanVideo-13B with equally weighted visual and motion quality rewards, DiffusionART reaches the late-stage mean reward levels of DiffusionNFT and Flow-GRPO-Fast with 12.02 and 11.72 times fewer GPU hours, respectively, while achieving 28.8% and 53.0% higher mean training rewards.

</details>


### [106] [Realism VS Accuracy: Event Sequence Forecasting from a Generative Modeling Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12586)
*Dmitry Osin,Egor Surkov,Petr Mokrov,Igor Udovichenko,Ivan Karpukhin,Evgeny Burnaev,Egor Shvetsov,Alexander Korotin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究事件序列的多步预测问题，将其建模为条件生成任务，同时评估预测准确性与分布真实感，构建了包含两类指标的基准，发现现有方法在准确性与真实感之间存在根本性权衡，并提出 Wasserstein BaryBooster 算法，将扩散模型的样本聚合到强预测器中，实现从分布真实感到预测准确性的转化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件序列由具有异构（数值与类别）特征的随机事件构成，其预测在金融、医疗、业务流程智能等领域具有重要应用。以往研究多只关注预测准确性，而忽视了预测分布的统计真实感，且缺乏统一基准来系统比较两类质量维度，因此需要研究准确性与真实感之间的关系。

Method: 将多步事件序列预测建模为条件生成问题；构建一个基准，包含衡量准确性与分布真实感两方面的指标、强统计基线、从相邻领域迁移的近期模型以及经过整理的真实数据集；并提出 Wasserstein BaryBooster 算法，将真实感强的扩散模型生成的样本聚合到最先进的预测器中。

Result: 细致的评估表明，没有任何一种方法能同时在准确性和真实感两方面表现优异，二者之间存在根本性权衡；Wasserstein BaryBooster 能够将分布真实感转化为预测准确性，说明两个质量维度可以通过样本聚合进行有效转换。

Conclusion: 事件序列预测中的准确性与分布真实感存在本质权衡，通过将强真实感生成模型的样本聚合到强预测器，可以在一定程度上弥合二者的差距。作者公开了基准与代码，为后续研究提供支持。

Abstract: Event sequences are chains of random events, each described by heterogeneous (numerical and categorical) features. The problem of event sequence forecasting occurs in finance, healthcare, business process intelligence, and other fields. In this work, we study multi-horizon forecasting of event sequences as a conditional generative modeling problem. We evaluate forecasts not only for prediction accuracy, but also for distributional realism. To this end, we present a benchmark comprising metrics for both quality aspects, strong statistical baselines, recent models adapted from adjacent fields, and curated real-world datasets. Careful evaluation reveals that no method excels at both accuracy and realism, highlighting a fundamental trade-off between the two. Finally, we introduce Wasserstein BaryBooster, an algorithm that aggregates samples from a realism-strong diffusion model into a state-of-the-art predictor, demonstrating that distributional realism can be converted into prediction accuracy. Code is available at https://github.com/xaosina/event-sequence-forecasting.

</details>


### [107] [STAPLE: Static Sparse Upcycling of Pretrained Checkpoints via Embedding Modules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12599)
*Ranajoy Sadhukhan,Euijin Hong,Sheng Cao,Zechun Liu,Shuowei Jin,Sha Li,Changlong Yu,Xin Liu,Qin Lu,Zhenyu Shi,Priyanka Nigam,Beidi Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出STAPLE，将静态稀疏记忆以残差路径方式集成到预训练模型中，实现样本高效、可扩展的容量扩充而无需从头稀疏预训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏计算可在不按比例增加激活计算量的前提下扩展LLM参数容量，但稀疏架构预训练昂贵且需大量数据；现有稀疏升级方法主要面向MoE，存在专家同质化、样本效率低和系统开销大的问题；静态稀疏记忆虽具可预测访问和token级更新优势，但直接集成到预训练计算中收益不佳。

Method: STAPLE基于STEM构建：STEM用token索引嵌入替换FFN上投影，而STAPLE保留FFN上投影，并额外加入token索引嵌入作为残差路径，从而保留预训练计算并学习互补的token特定信息。

Result: 在OLMo-2 1B上，相对持续稠密训练，HumanEval提升4.27、MBPP 2.80、GSM8K 2.27、DROP 1.65分；平均性能随静态容量增加而提升，并在OLMo-2 7B上保持优势。优于STEM和Engram风格模型，平均分高于MoE升级基线，解码快2倍，GPU模型内存与每token能耗不到其一半。host-offload实现下，生成吞吐在稠密基线1.5%以内、训练吞吐在3%以内，仅缓存约5%最高频嵌入。

Conclusion: 静态稀疏记忆可为扩展预训练模型提供样本高效、可扩展且可持续的路径，无需从头进行稀疏预训练。

Abstract: Sparse computation can scale the parametric capacity of LLMs without proportionally increasing active computation, but pretraining sparse architectures remains expensive and data-hungry. Existing sparse upcycling methods mainly target Mixture-of-Experts models, where expert homogeneity, sample inefficiency, and system overheads can limit practical scalability. Static sparse memory offers predictable access and token-specific updates that can help address these challenges, but naively integrating it into pretrained computation yields poor returns. In this work, we introduce STAPLE, a recipe built upon STEM for integrating static sparse memory into pretrained models. Whereas STEM replaces FFN up-projections with token-indexed embeddings, STAPLE retains them and adds the embeddings as a residual pathway, preserving pretrained computation while learning complementary token-specific information in a scalable manner. On OLMo-2 1B, STAPLE improves over continued dense training by 4.27 points on HumanEval, 2.80 on MBPP, 2.27 on GSM8K, and 1.65 on DROP. Average performance increases with static capacity, and improvements persist on the larger OLMo-2 7B backbone. STAPLE also outperforms static sparse baselines such as STEM and an Engram-style model, and achieves a higher average score than an MoE upcycling baseline while decoding 2x faster with less than half its GPU model memory and measured GPU energy per token in our throughput-oriented decoding evaluation. Importantly, despite introducing billions of parameters late in training, our host-offloaded implementation keeps generation throughput within 1.5% and training throughput within 3% of the dense baseline, while caching only the ~5% most frequent embeddings on GPU. These results show that static sparse memory can provide a sample-efficient, scalable, and sustainable path for expanding pretrained models without sparse pretraining from scratch.

</details>


### [108] [Machine Learning and Time-Series Foundation Models for Day-Ahead Electricity Forecasting in a University Building](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12638)
*Amey Vasulkar,Samuel Kernan Freire,Arie-Willem de Leeuw,Tadeo-Baldiri Salcedo-Rahola*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究对某大学建筑进行日前电力负荷预测基准测试，比较统计方法、机器学习、LSTM与预训练TimesFM；TimesFM 3.0（含协变量）整体误差最低，MAE较周季节朴素法降低约43%，XGBoost表现接近且本地拟合与推理更快，LSTM训练耗时更长但未超越二者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建筑运营商需要日前电力预测来安排供暖、通风等服务；但选择预测模型时，必须权衡精度与预测时所需输入、本地训练成本及计算需求，因此需要针对真实建筑场景的、可比的基准评估。

Method: 使用统一的时间序列验证流程，在同一57个测试日上评估统计模型、机器学习、循环神经网络及预训练TimesFM，并按工作日、周末、节假日分组比较精度，同时记录本地拟合与预测时间；天气协变量采用回顾性观测，另有历史评估与不同天气来源的对比分析。

Result: TimesFM 3.0含协变量整体误差最低，MAE相对周季节朴素预测降低约43%；XGBoost具竞争力，但逐日配对比较未显示TimesFM在MAE上具明确均值优势。TimesFM仅用历史用电也表现良好；加入协变量改善节假日预测，但略降低工作日与周末精度，整体MAE几乎不变。TimesFM无需建筑专属权重训练，XGBoost本地拟合与推理快，LSTM拟合更慢且未优于两者。历史评估显示季节性变化，天气来源改变对所选TimesFM精度影响较小。

Conclusion: 预训练TimesFM可作为该建筑强有力的初始预测基线，XGBoost是有竞争力的本地拟合替代方案；当节假日预测改善值得额外输入准备时，协变量尤其有用。

Abstract: Day-ahead electricity forecasts can help building operators schedule heating, ventilation, and other services around anticipated demand. Choosing a forecasting model also requires balancing accuracy with its forecast-time inputs, local training, and computational requirements.
  We benchmark statistical, machine-learning, recurrent neural-network, and pretrained TimesFM models for a university building. We use chronological validation and evaluate all configurations on the same 57 test days, comparing accuracy across workdays, weekends, and holidays alongside local fitting and forecasting time. Weather covariates use retrospective observations.
  TimesFM 3.0 with covariates achieves the lowest overall errors, reducing mean absolute error by approximately 43\% relative to weekly seasonal-naive forecasting. XGBoost remains competitive, although paired daily comparisons do not establish a clear mean-absolute-error advantage for TimesFM. TimesFM also performs strongly using only past electricity consumption. Adding covariates improves its holiday forecasts but slightly worsens workday and weekend forecasts, leaving overall mean absolute error almost unchanged. TimesFM requires no building-specific weight training, while XGBoost offers rapid local fitting and inference. The evaluated LSTM takes longer to fit without outperforming either approach.
  An exploratory historical evaluation reveals seasonal variation, while a separate weather-source comparison produces only small changes in the selected TimesFM configuration's accuracy. These results support pretrained TimesFM as a strong initial forecasting baseline for this building, with XGBoost as a competitive locally fitted alternative. Covariates are particularly useful when improved holiday forecasts justify the additional input preparation.

</details>


### [109] [SynAct: Synergistic Active Feedback Learning for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12651)
*Ning Liu,Ian Li,Kristina Klinkner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SynAct 将专家反馈定位到单个决策层面，用互信息排序决策价值，把纠正蒸馏为可迁移规则，在 16 个设置中平均提升 11.7pp。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体执行多步工作流时存在两类失败：可恢复的失误（重试可修复）与知识缺口（重试无法解决）。后者需专家反馈，但专家注意力稀缺。核心问题不是"是否求助"，而是"在哪里求助能改变结果"。现有方法要么完全回避专家反馈，使智能体自我强化错误；要么在任务层面分配反馈，无法定位关键决策。

Method: 提出 SynAct 主动学习框架，把专家注意力视为硬约束，在单决策粒度分配反馈。方法包括：1) 通过重复 rollout 计算所选动作与最终结果之间的互信息，对决策排序；2) 在最高价值决策处查询专家；3) 将专家纠正蒸馏为可迁移规则，使智能体在部署时无需专家即可复用。理论上刻画了有限重复采样下该信号的可辨识性：k 次 rollout、每动作至少 m 次观测时，结果恒定决策互信息为零，而结果可分离决策互信息至少为 H2(m/k) bit，对任意阈值 t∈(0,H2(m/k)) 实现精确分离。

Result: 在 4 个智能体基准和 3 个模型家族共 16 个设置中，SynAct 全部提升留出准确率，平均 +11.7pp。同一信号可迁移至推理时路由，在 16 个设置中的 15 个上优于预算匹配的级联、不确定性及专家评判路由器；单次定向纠正的效果优于 7 次额外的无目标重试。

Conclusion: 以互信息为决策价值信号可实现专家反馈的精准分配，并可迁移到路由场景；理论分析同时解释了基于成功率的方法在所有 rollout 都失败时会失效的原因——此时成功率恒定，即便不同失败轨迹到达了不同结果。

Abstract: LLM agents executing multi-step workflows fail in two fundamentally different ways: recoverable slips, which further attempts can repair, and knowledge gaps, which retrying cannot resolve. Expert feedback can address the latter, but expert attention is scarce. The key challenge is therefore not whether to ask for feedback, but where feedback can change the outcome. Existing approaches either avoid expert feedback, allowing agents to reinforce their own errors, or allocate feedback at the task level without identifying consequential decisions. We present SynAct, an active learning framework that treats expert attention as the binding constraint and allocates feedback at the level of individual decisions. SynAct ranks decisions by the mutual information between the selected action and the terminal outcome across repeated rollouts, queries an expert at the highest-value decisions, and distills the resulting corrections into transferable rules that the agent can reuse without expert access at deployment. We theoretically characterize what this signal identifies under finite repeated sampling: with k rollouts and at least m observations per action, outcome-constant decisions have zero mutual information, while outcome-separating decisions have mutual information at least H2(m/k) bits, yielding exact separation for any threshold t in (0,H2(m/k)). This also explains why success-based acquisition can be blind when all rollouts fail: success is constant even when failed runs reach different outcomes. Across four agent benchmarks and four models spanning three families, SynAct improves held-out accuracy in all 16 evaluated settings, by +11.7pp on average. The same signal transfers to inference-time routing, where SynAct outperforms budget-matched cascade, uncertainty, and expert-judge routers in 15 of 16 settings, while a single targeted correction outperforms seven additional unaimed retries.

</details>


### [110] [Kronecker Product Attention: Memory-Efficient KV Compression via Structured Factorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12657)
*Bahareh Nikpour,Hossein Rajabzadeh,Maryam Dialameh,Vahid Partovi Nia,Parsa Omidi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Kronecker Product Attention (KPA)，用紧凑矩阵因子的Kronecker积表示K/V激活，严格泛化TPA，配合结构化旋转嵌入消除解耦RoPE分支，实现最高10倍KV缓存压缩且精度不降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归生成受KV缓存内存占用限制，其随序列长度、批大小和模型深度线性增长。现有MQA、GQA、MLA、TPA等方法虽降低开销，但存在牺牲表达力、需额外位置编码机制或强加限制性低秩结构等问题。

Method: 提出KPA，将key和value激活表示为紧凑、token依赖的矩阵因子的Kronecker积之和。rank-one向量外积分解作为退化Kronecker分裂，非退化矩阵因子可表示更丰富的块结构模式。进一步设计匹配Kronecker表示的结构化旋转嵌入算子，仅旋转紧凑因子即可保持RoPE的相对位置性质，无需MLA中的解耦RoPE分支。

Result: 在四种模型规模和九个下游基准、相同训练条件下，KPA匹配或超越TPA、MHA、MQA、GQA和MLA，同时实现最高10倍KV缓存压缩。

Conclusion: Kronecker头分裂是可调超参数，允许实践者在设计阶段权衡压缩与精度而无需改变架构。

Abstract: Efficient autoregressive generation is increasingly limited by the memory footprint of the key-value (KV) cache, which grows linearly with sequence length, batch size, and model depth. Existing approaches such as Multi-Query Attention (MQA), Grouped-Query Attention (GQA), Multi-Head Latent Attention (MLA), and Tensor Product Attention (TPA) reduce this cost through different trade-offs, but often sacrifice expressivity, require additional positional-encoding machinery, or impose restrictive low-rank structure. We propose Kronecker Product Attention (KPA), which represents key and value activations as sums of Kronecker products of compact, token-dependent matrix factors. KPA strictly generalizes TPA: rank-one vector-outer-product factorization arises as a degenerate Kronecker split, while non-degenerate matrix factors can represent richer block-structured patterns. We further show that a structured rotary embedding operator matched to the Kronecker representation preserves the relative-position property of RoPE while rotating only the compact factors, removing the need for a decoupled RoPE branch as in MLA. Empirically, KPA matches or exceeds TPA, MHA, MQA, GQA, and MLA across four model scales and nine downstream benchmarks under identical training conditions, while achieving up to 10x KV cache compression. The Kronecker head split is a tuneable hyperparameter that allows practitioners to navigate the compression-accuracy trade-off at design time without architectural changes.

</details>


### [111] [Data-driven Optimal Sensor Placement for Aerosol Monitoring in the UK](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12658)
*Talha Ansar,Waqar Muhammad Ashraf,Alejandro Coca-Castro,Manvendra Janmaijaya,Ramit Debnath,J. Scott Hosking*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种数据驱动的传感器布局框架，用于英国气溶胶监测，结合卷积高斯神经过程与DeepSensor优化框架，实现高效且接近贪心算法的顺序布点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 环境观测系统（如气溶胶监测站网）的扩展需要合理的传感器布点。传统方法计算成本高，且布点准则与实际参考场（oracle）之间的一致性受气象条件影响，缺乏数据驱动且高效的布点方案。

Method: 将训练好的卷积高斯神经过程（convolutional Gaussian neural process）嵌入DeepSensor优化框架，采用带惰性求值与缓存的遗传优化进行传感器布点；利用CAMS参考场构建回溯式oracle基准，并以Joint NLL、Joint-MI等指标评价。

Result: 遗传优化配合惰性求值与缓存使平均计算时间降低约60%（10±3分钟 vs 25±7分钟），顺序布点性能与贪心求解器相当；在确定5个气溶胶站点后，Joint-MI驱动的遗传布点使Joint NLL改善约22%。季节与风况分析表明，模型准则与oracle基准的一致性随气象传输条件变化。

Conclusion: 该框架为环境观测系统的数据驱动扩展提供了一种计算高效的方法，兼顾布点性能与计算成本，但模型准则与真实基准的一致性受气象条件影响，需加以考虑。

Abstract: We develop a data-driven sensor-placement framework and apply it to aerosol monitoring in the United Kingdom (UK). A trained convolutional Gaussian neural process is embedded within the DeepSensor optimisation framework. Genetic optimisation with lazy evaluation and caching reduces mean computational time by 60\% (10$\pm$3 versus 25$\pm$7 minutes) while producing sequential placement performance comparable to the greedy solver. Using the CAMS reference field to construct a retrospective oracle benchmark, Joint NLL improves by about 22\% for Joint-MI-driven genetic placement after five locations for aerosol stations are identified. Seasonal and wind-regime analyses show that agreement between model-based criteria and oracle benchmarks varies with meteorological transport conditions. These results show a computationally efficient approach for data-informed expansion of environmental observing systems.

</details>


### [112] [Meta-Reflective Evolution Effectively Improves Production AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12794)
*Seung Won Wilson Yoo,Chang Zeng,Aditi Somayajula,Sahithi Ankireddy,Rohan Kosalge,Avishree Khare,John Mitchell,Aditya G. Parameswaran,Rajhans Samdani,Anupam Datta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 CoCoEvolve，一种基于"元反思"（meta-reflection）的进化优化器：智能体变异器（Cortex Code）从整个进化历史中选择性检索证据，并探测实际运行系统，再验证并提出变异。在数据智能体、LLM 数据流水线与数据工程工作流三类生产 AI 系统上均超越现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 引导的进化优化多从前沿（frontier）中选取候选，仅依据其失败情况进行一次 LLM 变异。这种"只看前沿"的方式在生产级 AI 系统中容易停滞：孤立失败难以揭示复杂且隐藏的交互关系，因而无法有效定位问题根因。

Method: 提出 CoCoEvolve（Cortex Code Evolve）。核心机制为元反思：由智能体变异器 Cortex Code 选择性地从完整进化历史中检索证据，并在提出变异前探测（probe）实时运行的系统，经验证后再生成变异方案，从而突破仅依赖前沿与单次失败驱动的局部优化范式。

Result: 1) DABStep：登顶公开排行榜，held-out 难样本准确率 92.8%，对比 Meta-Harness 的 78.4% 与 GEPA 的 30.4%（条件匹配）。2) 六个 LLM 数据流水线工作负载：优于全部所评估基线。3) data-eng-bench：测试集求解率由 59.1% 提升至 77.4%，超过最佳基线 73.6%。4) HotpotQA：准确率较 GEPA 高 11 个百分点，同时在数学与系统类基准上与专用优化器保持相当水平。匹配消融实验确认各组件均有贡献。

Conclusion: 实验结果证明元反思机制对优化生产级 AI 系统具有显著价值；CoCoEvolve 在多样化的真实生产场景中一致优于现有优化方法，且各设计组件均经消融验证具备独立贡献。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM)-guided evolutionary optimization has successfully improved prompts, programs, and even entire AI systems. Most optimization methods select a candidate from the search frontier, then mutate it in one LLM pass based only on its failures. This focus on the frontier can stall on production AI systems, where isolated failures reveal little about complex, hidden interactions. We introduce CoCoEvolve (Cortex Code Evolve), an evolutionary optimizer built around what we call meta-reflection, wherein an agentic mutator (Cortex Code) selectively retrieves evidence from the entire evolutionary history and probes the live system before verifying and proposing mutations. We apply CoCoEvolve to optimize three classes of production AI systems: data agents, LLM-powered data pipelines, and data engineering workflows. On DABStep, CoCoEvolve tops the public leaderboard, reaching 92.8% held-out hard accuracy compared to 78.4% for Meta-Harness and 30.4% for GEPA under matched conditions. Across six LLM data pipeline workloads, it outperforms every evaluated baseline. On data-eng-bench, CoCoEvolve lifts test-set solve rate from 59.1% to 77.4%, past the best baseline of 73.6%. It also has 11 percentage points higher accuracy than GEPA on HotpotQA while remaining competitive with specialized optimizers on established mathematical and systems benchmarks. Matched ablations confirm that each component of CoCoEvolve contributes to these gains, demonstrating the value of meta-reflection for optimizing production AI systems.

</details>


### [113] [Recurrent Harness for Agentic Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12668)
*Yuxin Xiao,Zhiyun Lu,Jianyu Wang,Yangtian Zhang,Yang Song,Qifan Wang,Bailin Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 ReHarness——一种用于智能体持续学习的循环式 harness 框架，在严格在线流式协议下，于前向读写之外引入反向修订：每个实例结束后以其轨迹为反馈，修订该实例所检索到的反思，从而将反馈沿文本 harness 状态反向传播。在 3 个持续学习基准与 3 个语言模型骨干上，ReHarness 优于原始上下文、记忆/反思类方法与提示优化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型智能体越来越多地运行在长交互流上，需要以持续学习实现在线自适应以应对分布漂移。但现有 agent harness 存在两类局限：要么仅通过前向信息流演化其状态，要么依赖额外的回放或验证数据来进行 harness 优化，难以满足严格在线的流式场景。

Method: 提出 ReHarness，一种循环式 harness 框架，面向智能体持续学习。其核心问法是：harness 状态中的哪些信息本可以被不同地捕获，从而更好地服务流中的当前实例。具体做法是：在严格在线流式协议下，除前向的读取与写入外增加反向修订；每个实例处理完成后，将产生的轨迹作为反馈，用于修订该实例所检索到的反思，使反馈以文本形式的 harness 状态为载体反向传播。

Result: 在 3 个持续学习基准与 3 个语言模型骨干上，ReHarness 一致优于强原始上下文基线、基于记忆与反思的方法以及提示优化方法。进一步分析显示：ReHarness 产生的修订有来源依据且有意义，能在分布漂移下纠正过度泛化或过时的经验，并同时提升持续学习中的稳定性与可塑性。

Conclusion: 通过以轨迹为反馈、沿文本 harness 状态反向修订反思，ReHarness 在不依赖回放或验证数据的前提下，为智能体持续学习提供了有效的在线自适应机制，兼顾稳定性与可塑性。

Abstract: Language model agents increasingly operate over long interaction streams, requiring online adaptation to distribution shifts through continual learning. However, existing agent harnesses typically either evolve their states only through forward information flow or rely on additional replay or validation data for harness optimization. To address this, we propose ReHarness, a recurrent harness framework for agentic continual learning that complements forward read and write with backward revision under a strictly online streaming protocol. We ask what information could have been captured differently in the harness state to better inform the current instance in the stream. Specifically, after each instance, we leverage the resulting trajectory as feedback to revise the reflections retrieved for that instance, thereby propagating feedback backward through the textual harness state. Across three continual learning benchmarks and three language model backbones, ReHarness consistently outperforms strong raw-context baselines, memory- and reflection-based methods, and prompt optimization approaches. Further analysis shows that ReHarness produces source-grounded and meaningful revisions, corrects overgeneralized or outdated experience under distribution shifts, and improves both stability and plasticity during continual learning.

</details>


### [114] [Amortised Inference through One-Step Implicit Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12679)
*Vincent Pauline,Kirill Tamogashev,Arran Carter,Sanghyeok Choi,Stefan Bauer,Esmeralda S. Whitammer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一步隐式采样器OSIS，利用短时MCMC动力学定义局部目标来训练隐式分布，使其在渐近极限下成为MCMC核的不动点，从而实现高维贝叶斯后验的高效一步摊销采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维贝叶斯后验采样仍是难题。现有无偏采样通常需要多步（如MCMC迭代或扩散摊销采样的积分步骤），而可扩展的一步摊销推断仍是未解决的开放问题，因此需要一种既准确又高效的一步摊销采样方法。

Method: 提出一步隐式采样器OSIS：用短时MCMC动力学为隐式建模分布的演化定义局部目标；在渐近极限下，所建模分布成为MCMC核的不动点，从而采样自目标分布。文中分析了其收敛性质，揭示了与其他生成建模和采样算法的联系，并研究了多种设计选择。

Result: OSIS在性能上优于多步摊销采样器和非摊销MCMC基线，同时在采样阶段效率显著更高。

Conclusion: 基于数据驱动生成建模的进展，OSIS为一步摊销采样提供了可行方案，兼顾采样质量与采样效率，并可与既有生成建模与采样算法建立理论联系。

Abstract: Efficient sampling from Bayesian posterior distributions in high dimensions remains a hard problem, despite advances in theory and algorithms. Multiple steps are usually required to draw unbiased samples, for example, iterations of an MCMC algorithm or integration steps in a diffusion-based amortised sampler. However, scalable one-step amortised inference remains a challenging open problem. In this work, we build upon recent progress in data-driven generative modelling to offer a possible solution for one-step amortised sampling. Our one-step implicit sampler (OSIS) uses short-time MCMC dynamics to define a local target for the evolution of an implicitly modelled distribution. In the asymptotic limit, the modelled distribution is a fixed point of the MCMC kernel and thus samples from the target. We analyse the convergence properties of OSIS and show connections with other generative modelling and sampling algorithms. We study various design choices and show that OSIS performs well compared to multi-step amortised samplers and non-amortised MCMC baselines while being far more efficient at sampling time.

</details>


### [115] [Returning to Development States: When Full Sandbox Snapshots Help Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13841)
*Daniel Thi Graviet,Tanmay Shah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文将编码智能体的失败恢复建模为搜索问题，在全量 Daytona 快照上实现 Go-Explore 式搜索，发现更高效、更高的求解率主要来自仓库之外的进度（数据库、服务等运行时状态）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体在失败前往往已取得有效进展，但从头重试会丢弃沿途产生的机器状态。若中间环境状态保留了有意义的进展，则可将其恢复并分支，从而可能优于反复从初始状态重启，这使失败恢复成为一个值得研究的搜索问题。

Method: 在全量 Daytona 快照上实现 Go-Explore 风格的搜索，对中间环境状态进行恢复与分支，以此替代从头重试，并分析何种进度带来性能提升。

Result: 更高的、更高效的求解率主要依赖于仓库之外的进展，例如数据库、服务以及其他运行时状态，而非仅由单一代码差异可重建的内容。

Conclusion: 当长时程编码任务跨越多个服务、且其状态无法由单个 diff 重建时，全快照搜索最为有效。

Abstract: Coding agents often make useful progress before they fail, but retry-from-scratch discards the machine state created along the way. This makes failure recovery an interesting search problem: if intermediate environment states preserve meaningful progress, restoring and branching from them could outperform repeatedly restarting from the initial state. We implement Go-Explore-style search over full Daytona snapshots and find that higher and more efficient solve rates depend mainly on progress that sits outside the repository, such as databases, services, and other runtime state. Our results suggest that full-snapshot search is most effective when long-horizon coding work spans multiple services whose state cannot be reconstructed from a single diff.

</details>


### [116] [Expert-level detection of low-voltage intervals in EEG as a robust biomarker of neonatal encephalopathy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12680)
*John M. O'Toole,Sean R. Mathieson,Aurel Luca,Soraia Ventura,Sean Griffin,Geraldine B. Boylan,Robert Hogan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出以振幅和时长定义的"低电压间期(LVI)"替代传统"爆发间隔(IBI)"，开发ConvNeXt模型自动检测LVI，并在444例新生儿数据上验证其作为脑损伤生物标志物的临床价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新生儿EEG的不连续性(discontinuity)是脑功能障碍的重要标志，但视觉量化困难。爆发间隔(IBI)虽是描述不连续性的关键特征，其定义依赖于对"爆发"的识别，难以直接转化为AI检测任务。因此需要一种仅由振幅和时长定义、与上下文无关的替代指标。

Method: 提出低电压间期(LVI)，仅以振幅和时长界定，作为IBI的上下文无关替代。开发ConvNeXt模型检测LVI并与现有方法比较。在123例新生儿共654小时逐通道EEG上标注51,162个LVI用于训练与验证。随后在四个应用场景测试LVI的临床相关性：背景EEG异常分级、HIE临床分级(与正常EEG对比)、癫痫预测、以及与癫痫负荷的关联。

Result: 在留出验证集上ConvNeXt的AUC为0.996，MCC为0.926；模型-读者一致性超过读者间一致性(Δκ=0.025, 95% CI: 0.007–0.045, p<0.001)，达到专家级等效。在444例新生儿合并数据集上，LVI百分比与EEG分级强相关(Spearman ρ=0.801)，与HIE分级中等相关(ρ=0.384)，与癫痫负荷关联较弱(ρ=0.244)，癫痫预测能力强(AUC=0.872)。

Conclusion: AI检测LVI可达到专家级等效水平，而现有定量特征无法做到。自动化LVI量化提供了一个稳健的新生儿脑病临床生物标志物，与背景EEG活动、HIE严重程度及癫痫风险均相关。

Abstract: Background: Discontinuity in neonatal EEG is a critical marker of brain dysfunction but can be difficult to quantify visually. Inter-burst intervals (IBIs) are a key characteristic of discontinuity, yet their definition does not easily translate to AI detection. Objectives: To introduce low-voltage intervals (LVIs), defined exclusively by amplitude and duration, as a context-independent alternative to IBIs, develop an AI detection method for LVIs, and assess their clinical utility as a marker of brain injury. Methods: We developed a ConvNeXt model to detect LVIs and compared its performance with existing methods. We then tested the clinical relevance of detected LVIs across four applications: 1) grading background EEG abnormalities on an open benchmark dataset, 2) classifying clinical hypoxic-ischaemic encephalopathy (HIE) grades and comparing with a normal EEG dataset, 3) seizure prediction, and 4) association with seizure burden. Results: For training and validation, 654 hours of per-channel EEG from 123 neonates were reviewed and 51,162 LVIs were annotated. On held-out validation, ConvNeXt had an AUC of 0.996 and a Matthews correlation coefficient of 0.926. Model-reader agreement exceeded inter-reader agreement ($Δκ= 0.025$, 95% CI: 0.007 to 0.045, $p<0.001$), attaining expert-level equivalence. On a combined dataset of 444 neonates, percentage LVI showed a strong association with EEG grades (Spearman's $ρ=0.801$), a moderate association with HIE grades ($ρ=0.384$), a weaker association with seizure burden ($ρ=0.244$), and a strong seizure prediction capacity (AUC $=0.872$). Conclusion: AI detection of LVIs achieves expert-level equivalence, whereas quantitative features do not. Automated LVI quantification provides a robust clinical biomarker of neonatal encephalopathy associated with background EEG activity, HIE severity, and seizure risk.

</details>


### [117] [Critical Mass: Structure-Disjoint Splits Drift With the Libraries They Are Built On](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12694)
*Tomo Oga,Devesh Shah,Cailum M. K. Stienstra,Gabriel Asher,Antonio H. de O. Fonseca,Niall O'Connor,Michael Widrich*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文指出分子机器学习中基于MCES单连接聚类的"结构不相交"划分准则在大规模公开库(227,690分子)下会导致图渗透:单一连通分量占95.5%,测试集被限制在4.5%,且剩余样本系统性地偏向更大、更复杂的分子。作者提出优化算法,将2.6×10^10分子对降至1.15×10^7整数规划(2259倍加速),并证明划分无损。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构不相交划分(out-of-distribution评估)的构建规则(单连接聚类+MCES距离+阈值10)是在29,000分子的库上设定的,但如今公开库已达227,690分子。由于MCES逐对为NP-hard,且分子对数量随规模二次增长,该准则从未在如此规模下被验证过,因此需要重新评估其在大规模库上的有效性与可计算性。

Method: 提出该划分准则的可计算优化实现:利用可容许下界(admissible bounds)与并查集跳(skip)技术,将2.6×10^10分子对裁剪至仅1.15×10^7个整数规划求解,并证明所得划分保持无损(与原始准则结果一致)。随后在227,690分子的公开库上应用该方法。

Result: 优化方法实现2,259倍加速(2.6×10^10 → 1.15×10^7整数规划),且划分无损。在227,690分子规模下,图发生渗透:一个连通分量包含95.5%的库,任何测试集上限仅为4.5%。剩余样本不仅更小,而且系统性不同——使用固定绝对预算(阈值10)时,对更大分子而言是更紧的相对容差,导致被留出的化合物有88.1%的概率"重于"训练化合物。

Conclusion: 在大规模分子库上,基于MCES单连接聚类的结构不相交划分会发生图渗透,使测试集被压缩至4.5%并系统性偏向更大、更复杂的化学样本。因此,使用该划分准则的大规模基准测试会把结构新颖性与向更大更复杂化学空间的分布外推相混淆,该惯例需在目标规模下重新审视。

Abstract: Molecular machine learning evaluates out-of-distribution performance with structure disjoint splits, a construction rule fixed once at the scale its library happened to have: threshold a pairwise distance, then assign whole connected components to folds. For predicting structures from mass spectra, the field has converged on single-linkage clustering under the myopic Maximum Common Edge Subgraph (MCES) distance at a threshold of 10. This convention was set on 29,000 molecules but public libraries now hold 227,690 molecules. MCES is NP-hard per pair over a quadratically growing set, and so the criterion has never been evaluated at that scale. We propose an optimized version of this criterion, allowing for computationally feasible application on the public libraries: Admissible bounds and a union-find skip reduce $2.6 \times 10^{10}$ molecular pairs to only $1.15 \times 10^{7}$ integer programs, a $2{,}259\times$ saving, while provably producing the lossless partition. We find that at this scale the graph percolates. A single component holds $95.5\%$ of the library, capping any test set at $4.5\%$. What remains is not merely smaller, but systematically different: a held-out compound outweighs a training compound 88.1\% of the time, because a fixed absolute budget is a tighter relative tolerance on a larger molecule. Large library benchmarks using this splitting rule naively therefore conflate structural novelty with extrapolation to larger, more complex chemistry.

</details>


### [118] [Primal-Dual Flow Matching for Sample-Wise Constrained Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12699)
*Zhengyan Huan,Peter Y. Lu,Shuchin Aeron*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Primal-Dual Flow Matching (PDFM)，通过学习满足样本级约束且可控制约束满足概率的生成模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成模型难以在样本级约束下生成数据，且难以精确控制约束满足概率；同时许多方法依赖可微距离、投影或约束集的凸性等特殊结构假设。

Method: 从群体级约束负对数似然最小化出发，采用流匹配（flow-matching）参数化，在正则性假设和速度场函数族充分表达的条件下，推导对应对偶优化算法约束满足概率的保证；并给出实用实现PDFM，仅需二元成员预言机反馈即可控制约束满足率。

Result: PDFM优于普通流匹配的约束满足，并在保持分布保真度的同时达到与约束感知基线相当的性能；无需可微距离、投影或凸性等约束集结构假设。

Conclusion: PDFM提供了一种可控制样本级约束满足概率、且实现门槛低的生成方法，兼具理论保证与实用性能。

Abstract: We consider the problem of learning to generate data while satisfying a prescribed \emph{sample-wise} constraint with a user-specified probability of constraint satisfaction. Starting with a population-level constrained negative log-likelihood minimization and using a flow-matching (FM) parameterization, under regularity assumptions and sufficient expressivity of the velocity field family, we derive guarantees on the probability of constraint satisfaction for the corresponding primal-dual optimization algorithm. We provide a practical implementation of this approach, called Primal-Dual Flow Matching (PDFM), which has direct control over constraint satisfaction rate and requires only binary membership oracle feedback for constraint satisfaction, avoiding the need for differentiable distances, projections, or special structure of the constraint set, such as convexity. We show that PDFM improves constraint satisfaction over vanilla FM and achieves competitive performance against constraint-aware baselines while maintaining distributional fidelity. Our code is available at https://github.com/ZhengyanHuan/PDFM.

</details>


### [119] [Byzantine-Resilient Federated Large Language Model via Hypergraph Message Passing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12711)
*Kai Li,Hanlin Cai,Zihao Liu,Yichen Li,Jose Alcaraz Calero,Wei Ni,Falko Dressler,Özgür B. Akan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HMP-GAE框架，通过超图消息传递识别并抑制联邦大语言模型中的拜占庭恶意更新，提升全局精度并降低语义熵与困惑度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦大语言模型（FedLLMs）旨在实现隐私保护下的协作智能，但其去中心化训练过程易受拜占庭攻击，恶意模型更新会破坏语义一致性与事实鲁棒性，导致语义污染知识在聚合过程中传播。

Method: 将防御问题形式化为在无法访问本地私有数据的前提下，联合最大化全局精度并抑制被操纵的模型更新。提出超图消息传递图自编码器（HMP-GAE）：把低秩模型更新表示为超图节点，通过迭代消息传递挖掘多智能体间的高阶语义关系，结合历史检查点与跨智能体关系推理，识别并抑制语义不一致更新，同时保持智能体间的知识对齐。

Result: 以Qwen-2.5和Llama-3.2为预训练FedLLM骨干，在AG News和Yahoo! Answers数据集上评测：相较现有防御，全局精度提升1.21%，最终模型分类语义熵降低44.08%，困惑度降低13.34%；相较基于GAE的防御，精度提升1.39%，语义熵降低54.5%，困惑度降低35.5%。

Conclusion: HMP-GAE能有效抑制拜占庭攻击下的语义污染传播，在不访问私有数据的前提下实现可信知识聚合，显著优于现有防御方法。

Abstract: Federated Large Language Models (FedLLMs) are promising to deliver privacy-preserving, collaborative intelligence. The decentralized training process is vulnerable to Byzantine attacks that corrupt semantic consistency and factual resilience through malicious model updates. This paper presents a new resilience strategy that prevents semantically corrupted knowledge from propagating during federated aggregation. A defense problem is formulated to jointly maximize global accuracy and suppress manipulated model updates, enabling trustworthy knowledge aggregation without accessing private local data. To strengthen the resilience of FedLLMs, we develop a novel Hypergraph Message Passing Graph Autoencoder (HMP-GAE) framework, where low-rank model updates are represented as nodes in a hypergraph and high-order semantic relationships among multiple agents are exploited through iterative message passing. By leveraging historical checkpoints and cross-agent relational reasoning, HMP-GAE identifies and suppresses semantically inconsistent updates while preserving knowledge alignment among agents throughout federated training. HMP-GAE is implemented using Qwen-2.5 and Llama-3.2 as pretrained FedLLM backbones and evaluated on AG News and Yahoo! Answers datasets. Compared to existing defenses, HMP-GAE improves global accuracy by $1.21$\%, reduces final-model classification semantic entropy by $44.08$\%, and lowers perplexity by $13.34$\%. HMP-GAE outperforms the GAE-based defense by improving the accuracy $1.39$\%, reducing the semantic entropy by $54.5$\%, and lowering perplexity by $35.5$\%.

</details>


### [120] [Spatial Context or Memory? Sizing the Inputs of Neural Surrogates for Partially Observed Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12723)
*Ruige Kong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文以贝叶斯最优误差为基准，量化神经代理模型中"观测窗口"与"历史快照"的权衡，发现在混沌系统中二者随预测提前期的相对作用发生反转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从业者通常通过模型间拟合对比来选择传感器窗口与历史长度，但这类比较无法区分误差来自模型不足还是信息缺失，因此需要一个与信息论下限对比的绝对基准。

Method: 先用精确高斯分析推导局部动力学场的贝叶斯最优预测；再在已知最优解的受迫线性场上验证窗口模型；随后在粘性Burgers流与混沌Kuramoto-Sivashinsky方程上训练窗口/历史模型，所有决策性比较在测试前固定，并跨传感器密度、三种架构及二维域验证。

Result: 线性场上仅读窗口的模型误差距最优值1%以内。除KS外，随提前期增长，八个快照消除单快照误差的比例下降；KS中窗口在一相关长度内时该比例上升，即出现"提前期反转"。该反转跨传感器密度、三种架构与二维域持续存在，并随阻尼降低Lyapunov指数而减弱。

Conclusion: 反转与线性不稳定性相关联，混沌与线性放大尚无法分离。设计建议：窗口按场的相关长度设定；固定输入预算应依模型服务的提前期在窗口与历史间分配，因最佳分配随提前期增大而偏向更宽窗口。Burgers流中提前期1所选分配在提前期8时误差为最优值的13.5倍。

Abstract: Neural surrogates that forecast a physical field from sparse, noisy sensors read a window of nearby sensors and a history of past snapshots. Practitioners choose both by comparing fitted models with each other. Such comparisons cannot tell a model's shortfall from missing information. We measure the trade-off between window and history against the Bayes-optimal error of the same readings. An exact Gaussian analysis shows that noisy or sparse observation of a field with local dynamics makes the optimal forecast read distant sensors only through spatial correlation. On a forced linear field with a known optimum, trained models that read only the window come within 1% of it. We then train such models on viscous Burgers flow and the chaotic Kuramoto-Sivashinsky (KS) equation, with every deciding comparison fixed before any test evaluation. In every field except KS, eight snapshots remove a smaller fraction of the one-snapshot error as the lead time, the number of steps to the forecast target, grows. In KS, for windows up to one correlation length, they remove a larger fraction. This lead-time inversion persists across sensor densities, three architectures and a two-dimensional domain. It shrinks as damping lowers the Lyapunov exponent. Damping also removes the linear instability of KS. We therefore tie the inversion to instability and leave chaos and linear amplification unseparated. For surrogate design the results give two rules. Size the window in correlation lengths of the field. Split a fixed input budget between window and history for the lead time the model will serve, because the best split moves toward wider windows as the lead time grows. In Burgers flow the split chosen at lead time one ends with 13.5 times the best error at lead time eight.

</details>


### [121] [A Categorical Atlas of Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning: Cones, Orbits, and Gauges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12728)
*Xiaoyu Li,Zhizhou Sha,Chiwun Yang,Dai Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文将参数高效微调（PEFT）方法重新形式化为权重空间中的“映射”，以切片范畴的视角统一比较数百种方法，并用三类映射不变量（像、纤维、基变换）解释其表达能力、训练动力学与初始化/合并等行为，进而对382种方法进行七维分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PEFT方法已有数百种，通常仅以参数量和基准分数进行比较，缺乏统一、可证明的理论框架来刻画方法间的等价性、表达能力边界与训练动力学。作者希望把预训练检查点视为权重空间中的基点，把微调方法视为从低维参数空间出发、以该基点为起点的光滑映射，从而用范畴论与几何不变量系统比较方法。

Method: 方法上，将预训练模型视为权重空间基点，将权重空间方法视为从微小参数空间出发的光滑映射；这些映射构成切片范畴，冻结模型为初始对象，全量微调为终端对象。核心使用三个不变量：1）基点附近的像，通过一阶像、维数、秩的min-cut上界以及基点是否光滑或为顶点来刻画表达能力；2）纤维及优化器度量，刻画训练动力学；3）基变换，覆盖不同初始化、量化、重启与合并。作者对LoRA、Householder反射适配等进行秩与奇偶性条件下的顶点分析，并研究Adam等优化器下的精确轨迹等价性、正交方法的奇异值不变性、LoRA+与普通LoRA的轨迹关系、Adam对LoRA连续对称群中带符号置换的保持，以及激活函数对缩放适配器折叠的影响。

Result: 论文给出多项精确结论：零初始化LoRA及在所述秩与奇偶性条件下默认起点的Householder反射适配始于锥的顶点；不存在精确初始化能使LoRA达到秩超过其自身两倍的上传更新；精确正交方法在精确算术下永不改变权重的奇异值，无论合并多少次；在无epsilon、无权重衰减或裁剪且共享调度的情况下，使用Adam时标准零初始化的LoRA+轨迹与普通LoRA将尺度乘以学习率比值的轨迹完全相同；共享超参数的Adam仅尊重LoRA满秩因子连续对称群内的带符号置换；激活函数决定缩放适配器何时折叠进前置权重。作者还按七个坐标对来自138篇论文的382种方法进行分类，给出403条模拟箭头和485个障碍。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个统一的范畴—几何框架，将PEFT方法视为权重空间映射，以像、纤维和基变换不变量连接表达能力、优化动力学与初始化/合并行为。所有编号结果均给出完整证明，并在重新推导他人结果时注明出处。该框架可用于系统分类、比较并指导PEFT方法设计。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning has hundreds of methods, usually compared by parameter count and benchmark score. We compare them as maps. A pretrained checkpoint is a base point in weight space, and a weight-space method is a smooth map from a small parameter space that starts at that point. These maps form a slice category in which the frozen model is initial and full fine-tuning terminal. Three invariants of the map do most of the work. Its image near the base point governs expressivity, through the first-order image, the dimension, a min-cut bound on rank and whether that point is smooth or an apex. Its fibres, with the optimizer's metric, govern training dynamics. Base change moves the base point and covers splitting initialisations, quantisation, restarts and merging. We prove, for example, that zero-initialised LoRA, and Householder reflection adaptation at its default start under stated rank and parity conditions, begin at the apex of a cone; that no exact initialisation lets LoRA reach an update of rank above twice its own; that exactly orthogonal methods never change a weight's singular values in exact arithmetic, however often they are merged; that under Adam with no epsilon, weight decay or clipping and one shared schedule, LoRA+ from the standard zero initialisation follows exactly the trajectory of plain LoRA with its scale multiplied by the learning-rate ratio; that Adam with shared hyperparameters respects only the signed permutations inside LoRA's continuous symmetry group of full-rank factors; and that the activation function decides when a scaling adapter folds into preceding weights. We classify 382 methods from 138 papers by seven coordinates read off their formulas, with 403 simulation arrows and 485 obstructions. Every numbered result is proved in full, and results due to others are credited where we re-derive them.

</details>


### [122] [Kernelized Parameter Sensitivity Atlases for Amortized Conditional Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12737)
*Davide Wiest,Ahanaf Hasan Ariq,Abdul Monaf Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出KPSA（核化参数敏感度图谱），通过对经验神经正切核的块分解来度量归一化的参数组敏感度，并将其嵌入显式查询空间，用轻量核估计未见输入的敏感度，从而在摊销重复梯度归因的同时保留输入依赖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于梯度的归因能提供与输入相关的模型参数信息，但对大量样本做反向传播代价高昂；而静态重要性分数又丢弃了输入依赖性。因此需要一种既能保持输入依赖、又能摊销重复梯度计算成本的方法，服务于可扩展的条件可解释性。

Method: 从经验神经正切核的块分解推导KPSA，度量归一化的参数组敏感度；将敏感度来源嵌入显式查询空间，用轻量核为未见输入估计敏感度。理论上推导了区分有限来源误差与不可约表示误差的界，并给出基于间隔的top-k恢复保证。实验覆盖视觉、时间序列预测、蛋白质建模三类任务，比较参数预算、稀疏敏感度回路、激活归因及核聚合消融。

Result: 在0.02%–0.1%的五倍参数预算范围内，KPSA选出的参数组达到一阶反向传播oracle扰动敏感度的39%–85%；在视觉和预测任务中形成跨深度的稀疏敏感度回路，并随输入系统性变化。在Transformer MLP特征激活归因中，视觉任务保留反向传播oracle与常数敏感度基线之间差距的81%，批量查询吞吐由551提升至2,041 images/s，峰值GPU内存由4.55GB降至0.44GB；蛋白质任务恢复该差距的68%。消融显示KPSA空间中的最近邻检索仍具竞争力。

Conclusion: 紧凑的核图谱能够存储有用的、输入依赖的参数敏感度，同时摊销重复的梯度归因计算，朝着可扩展的条件可解释性迈进一步。

Abstract: Gradient-based attribution provides input-specific information about model parameters, but computing it via backpropagation for many samples is expensive, while static importance scores discard input dependence. We derive KPSA (Kernelized Parameter Sensitivity Atlas) from a block decomposition of the empirical neural tangent kernel. It measures normalized parameter-group sensitivity, embeds those sources in an explicit query space, and uses a lightweight kernel to estimate sensitivities for unseen inputs. We derive bounds that separate finite-source from irreducible representation error and give margin-based top-$k$ recovery. This paper studies two interpretability applications. Firstly, KPSA identifies parameter groups with high susceptibility to perturbations across vision, forecasting, and protein-modeling setups. Across a fivefold range of parameter budgets ($0.02$-$0.1\%$), its selected groups attain $39$-$85\%$ of the perturbation susceptibility of a first-order backpropagation oracle. In vision and forecasting, these parameter groups form sparse sensitivity circuits that extend across depth and vary systematically with the input. Secondly, we use KPSA on an activation-attribution problem for MLP features in transformers. It retains $81\%$ of the gap between a backpropagation oracle and a constant-sensitivity baseline on vision while increasing batched query-time throughput from 551 to 2,041 images/s and reducing peak GPU memory from 4.55 to 0.44GB. On the protein-modeling task, it recovers $68\%$ of this gap. An ablation of KPSA's kernel aggregation shows that nearest-neighbor retrieval in KPSA space can remain competitive. The empirics show that compact kernel atlases can store useful input-dependent parameter sensitivity while amortizing repeated gradient attribution, a step toward scalable conditional interpretability. Code can be found at https://github.com/Axym-Labs/kpsa .

</details>


### [123] [KITE: A Knowledge-Injected Temporal Encoder for Time-Series Event Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12738)
*Jonas Petersen,Pratik Jawahar,Riccardo Maggioni,Gian-Alessandro Lombardi,Matteo Cozzi,Marek Gajewski,Federico Martelli,Camilla Mazzoleni,Philipp Christian Petersen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出将领域专家知识以"语义先验"形式注入自监督事件预测器（KITE），通过协变量与注意力偏置两种可切换机制实现，在短上下文下显著提升域内外稀有事件预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多元时间序列中的稀有高代价事件因前兆极少被观测、标签稀缺，纯数据驱动模型难以学习。而运维这些系统的工程师掌握先于事件发生的知识，这类信息在稀缺标签中缺失。

Method: 提出"语义先验"概念，通过两种可切换机制（协变量机制和注意力偏置机制）将其注入自监督事件预测器KITE，两机制在空值时均为恒等映射，因此单一架构即可适配所有先验类型，无需逐数据集架构搜索。

Result: 当先验提供观测窗口未包含的信息时有效：短上下文下有信息先验优于无先验（相比无先验基线，周期性时钟先验 pooled h-AUROC +14%，p<0.001；标签比例0.25时守恒残差先验 +47%），有效先验可跨域迁移（+53%）并替代特征迁移；窗口已能恢复的先验则无增益，注意力偏置机制未见可测量收益。

Conclusion: KITE提供了一条可切换、可归因的上下文条件化路径，并可作为向跨域、预训练时间序列基础世界模型扩展的基础，使其显式携带治理结构。

Abstract: Forecasting rare, high-cost events in multivariate time-series systems is hard because their precursors are seldom observed, so a purely data-driven model has little to learn from. The engineers who run these systems, however, hold knowledge that precedes such events, information absent from the scarce labels. We call this a semantic prior and inject it into a self-supervised event forecaster through two switchable mechanisms, a covariate mechanism and an attention-bias mechanism, each the identity at its null, so one architecture serves every prior type with no per-dataset architecture search. A prior helps when it supplies information the observed window does not already contain: in a short context an informative prior beats no prior in-domain (pooled h-AUROC over the no-prior baseline: a periodicity clock +14%, p<0.001; a conservation residual +47% at a 0.25 label fraction), and a working prior transfers out-of-domain (+53%), substituting for feature transfer, while priors the window already recovers stay inert and the attention-bias mechanism shows no measurable gain. KITE is a switchable, attributable route to contextual conditioning, and a substrate for scaling toward cross-domain, pretrained time-series foundation world models that carry their governing structure explicitly.

</details>


### [124] [Neural expectation-maximization and meta-models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12741)
*Zhuolin Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文将Transformer解释为结合max-product环状置信传播(LBP)与EM的混合概率推断过程的近似，并据此提出"元模型"设计范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer结构虽然经验成功，但其计算机理缺乏统一的概率推断视角，作者希望从概率推断角度解释其内部机制，并统一多种现有机制。

Method: 将Transformer视为在结构和参数均从数据中推断的部分观测马尔可夫随机场(MRF)上进行推断；证明自注意力实现输入条件化、动态结构MRF上max-product LBP的可微近似；在深度层面建立EM对应关系：每层前向pass为E步式隐表示更新，层间参数转移θ^(l)→θ^(l+1)为隐式M步；据此提出meta-models范式，用输入生成实例特定参数。

Result: 理论分析表明自注意力对应可微max-product LBP，注意力权重编码学习到的结构亲和性；层间深度对应EM交替；meta-models范式统一了自注意力、混合专家(MoE)和门控单元等机制。实验结果为该视角提供了支持。

Conclusion: Transformer可被理解为在动态结构MRF上结合LBP与EM的混合推断近似，meta-models为统一理解与设计多种架构机制提供了新框架。

Abstract: We interpret Transformer networks as learned approximations to a hybrid probabilistic-inference procedure that combines max-product Loopy Belief Propagation (LBP) with Expectation--Maximization (EM). In this view, a Transformer performs inference in partially observed Markov Random Fields (MRFs) whose structure and parameters are not given a priori but are inferred from data. We show that self-attention implements a differentiable approximation to max-product LBP on an input-conditioned, dynamically structured MRF, with attention weights encoding learned structural affinities. We further establish an EM correspondence at the level of depth: each layer updates latent representations in an E-step-like forward pass, while the transition $θ^{(l)} \to θ^{(l+1)}$ across layers acts as an implicit M-step. Building on this interpretation, we introduce \emph{meta-models}, a design paradigm in which inputs generate instance-specific parameters, unifying mechanisms such as self-attention, mixture-of-experts, and gated units within a single framework. Empirical results support the proposed perspective.

</details>


### [125] [Static Gradient Attribution Underperforms a Density-Matched Random Mask on Loss-Based Forgetting Within LoRA B-Matrix](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12744)
*Yu Sun,Junwei Zhou,Zuodong Xiang,Yike Zhang,Pengcheng Xu,Xinyu Guan,Ruoyun Ma,Hailu Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文指出在零初始化LoRA中比较梯度掩码与随机掩码存在"参数池混淆"（∂L/∂A=0导致梯度掩码只选B），控制参数池后，随机掩码在Qwen2.5-3B七个随机种子上反而取得更低的基于损失的BWT，负结果主要来自医疗任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参数高效持续学习常以梯度归因为准则选择待更新参数子集，但该方法需在校准数据上额外进行一次反向传播。作者怀疑既有"梯度掩码优于随机掩码"的结论可能源于实验设计缺陷，而非选择准则本身的优势。

Method: 识别标准零初始化LoRA（B=0）下的参数池混淆：此时∂L/∂A=0，梯度掩码严格只从B矩阵中选择参数，而无约束随机掩码从A、B中共同采样，因此两者比较同时改变了选择规则与候选参数池。为隔离选择准则，将两种方法限制在同一参数池内进行对照实验，在Qwen2.5-3B上使用七个随机种子，覆盖医疗、代码、数学、法律等任务序列，并比较基于损失的BWT及两项离散选项任务的准确率-BWT。

Result: 在同一参数池条件下，均匀随机掩码在七个种子上始终取得比梯度归因更低的基于损失的BWT。该效应集中于医疗任务，其贡献占聚合结果的80%；代码与数学任务方向一致，法律任务无可靠差异。该优势与所测任务序列下的稳定性—可塑性权衡一致，随机掩码略微降低了单任务拟合能力。在医疗与法律两项离散选项任务上，平均准确率-BWT差异与零不可统计区分。

Conclusion: 梯度掩码相对随机掩码的优势很可能部分来自参数池不一致这一混淆因素。控制参数池后，梯度归因的选择优势不再成立甚至反转，说明对其有效性需谨慎解读，同时提示可免除额外反向传播的随机选择方案在特定任务序列下具有竞争力。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient continual learning updates a restricted subset of adapter parameters to incorporate sequential tasks while limiting catastrophic forgetting. Gradient attribution is a common rule for selecting that subset, but it requires an additional backward pass over calibration data. We identify a parameter-pool confound in comparisons between initialization-time gradient masks and unrestricted random masks under standard zero-initialized LoRA ($B=0$): the gradient with respect to $A$ is identically zero ($\partial\mathcal{L}/\partial A=0$), so gradient masks select strictly from the $B$ matrix whereas unrestricted random masks draw from both $A$ and $B$. Such a comparison changes both the selection rule and the candidate parameter pool. Isolating the selection criterion by restricting both approaches to the same parameter pool, we find a negative result: a uniform random mask consistently achieves lower loss-based backward transfer than gradient attribution across seven seeds on Qwen2.5-3B. The effect is concentrated on the medical task, which contributes 80\% of the aggregate; code and math move in the same direction, and legal shows no reliable difference. This advantage is consistent with a stability--plasticity trade-off under the tested task sequence, in which random masking slightly reduces single-task fitting. On the two discrete-option tasks (Medical and Legal), the mean accuracy-BWT difference is not statistically distinguishable from zero.

</details>


### [126] [NV-Tesseract-AD: Curriculum-Enhanced Conditional Diffusion for Multivariate Time-Series Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12754)
*Aditi Gautam,Manikandan Ravikiran,Saira Qureshi,Sena Fatma Ekiz,Charan Jagwani,Mahmoud Elhadidy,Sanjeev Roka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出NV-Tesseract-AD，一种课程学习增强的条件扩散框架用于多元时间序列异常检测，通过掩码严重度与扩散噪声水平双轴课程显著提升F1与MSE，并识别阈值校准为主要瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多元时间序列异常检测中，重建难度未被显式结构化，训练优化不稳定且残差判别性不足；作者假设可从掩码严重度与扩散噪声水平两个互补维度组织课程，由易到难联合训练以稳定优化并提升残差可分性。

Method: 以Transformer扩散骨干为基础，构建掩码比例与扩散时间步的三阶段双课程；训练全程保留异常样本，依靠结构化损坏驱动自监督表征学习；评估含TSB-UAD-M分布内训练与对十个EasyTSAD基准族的严格零样本迁移（无需逐数据集调参）；推理采用DPM-Solver++加速。

Result: 双轴课程实现掩码不变重建，第一阶段内互补掩码策略损失差近零；相对非课程基线F1提升约3.7倍，MSE降低56%；零样本F1在NAB达0.984、NormA达0.948；异构族表现较弱源于无监督阈值校准而非重建质量；DPM-Solver++在数据集层面将采样成本降低3.5至5.7倍，但与阈值行为耦合。

Conclusion: 将扩散去噪难度与课程学习原则对齐，为复杂多元时间序列的鲁棒异常检测提供了有原理且实用的途径；阈值校准是剩余的主要检测瓶颈，采样器与阈值必须联合验证。

Abstract: We propose NV-Tesseract-AD, a curriculum learning-enhanced conditional diffusion framework for multivariate time-series anomaly detection. Our central hypothesis is that reconstruction difficulty can be explicitly structured along two complementary axes, masking severity and diffusion noise level, and that training from easier to harder tasks along both axes simultaneously stabilizes optimization and improves residual discriminability. We integrate a Transformer-based diffusion backbone with a three-stage dual curriculum over masking ratios and diffusion timesteps, retaining anomalous samples throughout training and relying on structured corruption to drive self-supervised representation learning. We evaluate NV-Tesseract-AD under two regimes: in-distribution training on the TSB-UAD-M corpus and strict zero-shot transfer to ten EasyTSAD benchmark families without per-dataset tuning. The dual-axis curriculum produces masking-invariant reconstruction, confirmed by a near-zero loss gap between complementary masking strategies within the first training stage, and yields an approximately 3.7-fold improvement in F1 and a 56 percent reduction in MSE relative to a non-curriculum baseline. Zero-shot performance reaches F1 = 0.984 on NAB and F1 = 0.948 on NormA. Weaker results on heterogeneous families are traced to unsupervised threshold calibration rather than reconstruction quality, identifying calibration as the dominant remaining detection bottleneck. Accelerated inference via DPM-Solver++ reduces sampling cost by a factor of 3.5 to 5.7 at the dataset level but couples to thresholding behavior, establishing that the sampler and threshold must be validated jointly. Together, these findings demonstrate that aligning diffusion denoising difficulty with curriculum learning principles provides a principled and practical approach for robust anomaly detection in complex multivariate time series.

</details>


### [127] [Provenance-grouped protocol for evaluating machine learning in perovskite photovoltaics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12766)
*Qiwei Han,Chi Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究揭示了钙钛矿太阳能电池机器学习模型评估中随机划分导致的数据泄漏问题，并提出基于机构来源的分组划分协议，重新基准了十三种模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有钙钛矿太阳能电池的机器学习模型普遍采用随机数据划分进行评估，但近三分之二的器件与另一器件共享完全相同的配方，造成大量内部重复，使随机划分主要通过"回忆历史数据"而非检验真正的物理泛化能力来产生性能假象。

Method: 作者梳理了钙钛矿数据库（Perovskite Database）的机构来源，建立了一套严格的分组划分层级体系，依次隔离重复器件、同一出版物及同一机构，并在该体系下对十三种模型进行基准测试，同时开展特征重要性分析。

Result: 基准测试在所有划分层级上确立了新的最优性能，并揭示了关键的范式转变：树集成模型在随机划分下凭借识别熟悉配方占优，但在真正未见过的机构及未来发现上排名崩塌，此时成分感知的神经网络架构表现优异。特征重要性表明，器件结构与制备工艺对最终效率的决定作用与吸收层化学相当。

Conclusion: 提出的标准化评估协议可帮助社区区分"记忆"与"真正的物理发现"，推动该领域更可靠地衡量模型的泛化能力。

Abstract: Machine-learning models for perovskite solar cells are typically evaluated using random data splits, which we show systematically leak information through massive internal duplication. Because nearly two-thirds of curated devices share their exact recipe with another, random splits primarily reward the mere recall of historical data rather than testing true physical generalisation. To solve this, we mapped the institutional provenance of the Perovskite Database and established a rigorous hierarchy of grouped splits that progressively isolate duplicate devices, publications, and institutions. Benchmarking thirteen models establishes new state-of-the-art performance across all splits and reveals a critical regime shift: while tree ensembles dominate random splits by recognising familiar recipes, their ranking collapses on genuinely unseen institutions and future discoveries, where composition-aware neural architectures excel. Furthermore, feature importance demonstrates that the device architecture and fabrication methodology dictate final efficiency just as strongly as the absorber chemistry. This standardised protocol empowers the community to separate memorisation from true physical discovery.

</details>


### [128] [REDaCT: Collapse-Free Unlearning in Large Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12769)
*Joshua Maccoby,Wendy Hui Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出REDaCT，一种推理时遗忘框架，通过泄漏检测与纠正式嵌入引导，在不破坏推理链的前提下实现对大型推理模型的遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型推理模型的遗忘需同时清除推理轨迹与最终答案中的遗忘集信息，但现有方法会造成"推理崩塌"——模型无法生成连贯的思维链且无法正常终止推理过程。

Method: REDaCT包含两个组件：(1)二分类泄漏检测器，识别推理轨迹中泄漏遗忘集信息的句子；(2)嵌入优化模块，离线学习一个纠正式嵌入，在推理时于首个检测到的泄漏处注入，引导后续推理转向不泄漏遗忘集信息的句子。

Result: 实验表明，REDaCT完全避免了推理崩塌，相比最先进的遗忘方法显著提升推理保持能力，同时维持完美的答案遗忘与近乎完美的思维链遗忘。

Conclusion: REDaCT以推理时干预方式在遗忘效果与推理能力保持之间取得更优平衡，为大型推理模型遗忘提供新思路，代码已开源。

Abstract: Unlearning in large reasoning models (LRMs) requires removing forget-set information from both the reasoning trace and the final answer. Existing approaches achieve this objective at the cost of reasoning collapse, where the model fails to produce a coherent chain-of-thought (CoT) and does not properly terminate its reasoning process. To address this challenge, we propose REDaCT (REgenerate via Detection and Corrective Tuning), an inference-time unlearning framework for LRMs. REDaCT consists of two key components: (1) a binary leak detector that identifies sentences in the reasoning trace that leak forget-set information, and (2) an embedding optimization module that learns a corrective embedding offline, such that when injected at the first detected leak during inference, it guides subsequent reasoning towards sentences that do not leak forget-set information. Experimental results show that REDaCT completely avoids reasoning collapse, substantially improving reasoning preservation compared to state-of-the-art unlearning methods while maintaining perfect answer forgetting and near-perfect CoT forgetting. Our code is available at: https://github.com/jmaccoby/redact.

</details>


### [129] [Coordinate-Free Automatic Differentiation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12776)
*Alex Guerra,Joshua Aduol,Yaqian Tang,Daniel Williams,Ryan P. Adams,Peter Orbanz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于现有AD原语的、与基无关的通用导数算子，可统一表达多种几何微分运算，并可与JAX的批处理、编译和硬件加速组合，实现几何作为代数数据的可复用处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动微分框架隐式固定欧氏空间基来定义梯度和雅可比，天然只处理固定坐标系下的数组对象，导致在AI for Science中表达旋转、体积形式、曲率等几何对象时很别扭，且定制化变通方案缺乏可复用性。

Method: 证明存在单一的、与基无关的导数算子，能够表示多种几何微分运算，并且该算子可完全用现有AD原语表达；将其作为可复用变换，与JAX的批处理、编译和硬件加速组合，使几何以代数数据形式进入计算流程。

Result: 实验验证了该算子的可复用性：可跨不同流形算子复用，可通过时空逆问题流水线进行组合，并可用于不同转子子群上的优化。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种原则性、可复用的几何自动微分方案，摆脱了对固定基的依赖，为AI for Science中几何相关应用提供了统一且可组合的工具。

Abstract: Automatic differentiation (AD) is a cornerstone of modern machine learning. Since all AD frameworks implicitly fix a basis of Euclidean space to define gradients and Jacobians, they natively process mathematical objects that are arrays of a fixed set of coordinates. This makes it awkward to express objects such as rotations, volume forms, and curvatures that arise, for example, in applications of AI to science. Bespoke workarounds lack reusability. We show that (i) a single, basis-independent derivative operator can represent many geometric differential operations, and (ii) this operator can be expressed entirely in terms of existing AD primitives. This reusable transformation can be composed with JAX batching, compilation, and hardware acceleration. Geometry enters as algebraic data. We experimentally demonstrate its reuse across different manifold operators, its composition through a spacetime inverse-problem pipeline, and its use in optimization over different rotor subgroups.

</details>


### [130] [The Expressive Power of Random-Feature MPNNs Across Graph Sizes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12809)
*Robert R. Nerem,Mitchell Black,Antonis Vasileiou,Christopher Morris,Yusu Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出群体水平的尺寸一致逼近框架，证明连续随机节点特征可被量化为有限颜色，从而将有限颜色可区分性限制迁移到连续随机特征MPNN的尺寸一致逼近限制上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续随机节点特征能提升消息传递神经网络（MPNNs）的表达能力，但现有理论分析不够精细，难以刻画其在跨图尺寸逼近或固定随机比特数下的局限。

Method: 建立群体水平（population-level）的尺寸一致逼近框架。针对初始特征具有与图尺寸无关Lipschitz界的MPNNs（包括标准mean、max及order-normalized sum架构），说明连续随机特征可被量化为有限多种颜色，且期望输出上的均匀误差可任意小。

Result: 借助该归约，将有限颜色可区分性的限制转化为连续随机特征尺寸一致逼近的限制；证明这些MPNNs并非尺寸一致通用；分析颜色与独立试验带来的表达性增益；建立随机颜色MPNNs与高阶Weisfeiler-Leman方法之间的不可比性；并给出基于color coding的树子图同构及局部MST任务的有限颜色MPNN构造。

Conclusion: 连续随机特征的表达能力受限于其可量化后的有限颜色机制，无法实现尺寸一致通用逼近，且与高阶WL方法不可比，但其在特定任务（树子图同构、局部MST）上仍可通过有限颜色构造实现。

Abstract: Continuous random node features increase the expressivity of message-passing neural networks (MPNNs), but existing theoretical analyses are not fine-grained enough to capture their limitations, particularly in approximating functions across graph sizes or under a fixed number of random bits. We develop a population-level framework for size-uniform approximation. For MPNNs with a graph-size-independent Lipschitz bound in their initial features, including standard mean, max, and order-normalized sum architectures, we show that continuous random features can be quantized to finitely many colors with arbitrarily small uniform error in expected output. This reduction lets us transfer limitations on finite-color distinguishability to limitations on size-uniform approximation with continuous random features. We use this framework to prove that these MPNNs are not size-uniformly universal, analyze the expressivity gains from colors and independent trials, and establish incomparability between random-color MPNNs and higher-order Weisfeiler-Leman methods. We also give finite-color MPNN constructions for tree subgraph isomorphism via color coding and a local MST task.

</details>


### [131] [Deploying RDMA for Large-Scale Inference and Training Workloads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12815)
*Yibo Huang,Yi Cui,Yukai Huang,Huaping Zhou,Jiancong Tong,Zhaoyu Wu,Gen Miao,Haoran Wei,Boyan Ding,Zhuo Jiang,Jianxi Ye,Yibo Zhu,Chuanxiong Guo,Yang Xu,Jie Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文报告了在生产环境（字节跳动参数服务器推理与训练系统）中部署基于硬件RDMA的软件通信层ByteExpress的经验，总结了三条部署教训，并给出显著性能收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RDMA可降低分布式机器学习的通信开销，但在生产环境中，仅替换TCP并不足以实现收益。变长消息、并发连接、突发incast和服务故障等问题无法仅靠硬件卸载解决，因此需要研究如何在实际系统中落地RDMA。

Method: ByteExpress是构建于硬件RDMA之上的软件通信层，集成了RDMA感知的内存分配器、零拷贝传输、QP多路复用、接收信用流控、软件发送窗口以及RPC辅助的恢复机制。作者基于在参数服务器架构的推理与训练系统中的部署经验，归纳出三条教训：高效RDMA需跨应用层与传输层进行缓冲区管理；可扩展通信需协同管理连接、接收容量与在途数据；服务可用性需在传输层之上实现恢复。

Result: 在生产推理中，相比内核TCP，ByteExpress将模型参数拉取的平均延迟和P99延迟分别降低35.7%和43.7%，总CPU使用率降低10.69%。在训练中，相比TCP，ByteExpress将模型参数推送的P99延迟最高降低74.0%，且运行时未观察到PFC暂停。

Conclusion: 在硬件RDMA之上构建软件通信层可在生产级分布式机器学习系统中有效释放RDMA性能，但需要通过跨层缓冲区管理、协同的资源与连接管理以及在传输层之上的恢复机制来应对变长消息、并发连接、突发incast和故障等挑战。

Abstract: RDMA can reduce communication overhead in distributed machine learning systems, but realizing these benefits in production requires more than replacing TCP. Variable-size messages, concurrent connections, bursty incasts, and service failures introduce challenges that hardware offload alone cannot address. This paper reports our experience deploying ByteExpress, a software communication layer over hardware RDMA, in ByteDance's parameter-server-based inference and training systems. We present three lessons from the deployment: efficient RDMA requires buffer management across application and transport layers; scalable communication requires coordinated management of connections, receive capacity, and outstanding data; and service availability requires recovery above the transport layer. ByteExpress combines an RDMA-aware allocator, zero-copy transfers, QP multiplexing, receive-credit flow control, a software send window, and RPC-assisted recovery. In production inference, ByteExpress reduces mean and P99 model parameter fetch latency by 35.7% and 43.7%, respectively, and total CPU usage by 10.69% compared with kernel TCP. In training, ByteExpress reduces P99 model parameter push latency by up to 74.0% compared with TCP, with no PFC pauses observed at runtime.

</details>


### [132] [Inverse Synthesis of Shock Time Series from Shock Response Spectra Using Ensembles of Conditional Variational Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12820)
*Adam Watts,Charles Young,Ryan Bowering,Andrew Jeon,Destry Newton*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种基于循环解码器的条件变分自编码器（CVAE）框架，通过预测多尺度小波系数并经逆离散小波变换重建波形，实现从给定冲击响应谱（SRS）到候选时域波形的反向合成，并以多模型集成提升多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从加速度时程（波形）到SRS的映射是多对一的，因此逆向合成需要在与给定谱一致的条件下生成候选波形。此前直接时域CVAE在潜变量采样下多样性有限，需要增强生成候选波形的变异性与精度。

Method: 在既有CVAE框架基础上引入循环（recurrent）解码器，预测多尺度小波系数，并通过逆离散小波变换重建波形；针对单模型潜采样变异性不足，构建由独立训练模型组成的集成，成员覆盖不同母小波与分解层数，每个成员对同一目标SRS生成一个独立候选波形。另开展条件化研究，考察频率网格与编码方式的选择。

Result: 在四个留出基准数据集上，所提小波模型的平均与中位谱误差均低于此前的直接时域CVAE和衰减正弦求和（SDS）基线。与已发表的逆SRS方法比较，在四个留出基准数据集（两个实际冲击数据集、一个地震数据集、一个合成冲击数据集）上表现更优，并在Brake报告的合成目标上具有竞争力。批量推理时单模型约0.1–0.6 ms/波形，SDS约需5–8 s（不含离线训练）。

Conclusion: 该方法以极低推理成本快速生成多个互异的、与SRS相容的候选波形，但除谱一致性外，其物理适用性仍需进一步评估。

Abstract: The shock response spectrum (SRS) characterizes the peak responses of single-degree-of-freedom systems to transient acceleration. Because the mapping from an acceleration time series (waveform) to its SRS is many-to-one, inverse synthesis seeks candidate waveforms consistent with a prescribed spectrum. We extend an earlier conditional variational autoencoder (CVAE) framework with a recurrent decoder that predicts multiscale wavelet coefficients and reconstructs waveforms through an inverse discrete wavelet transform. Individual models exhibit limited variability under latent sampling, motivating an ensemble of independently trained models spanning different mother wavelets and decomposition levels. Each member produces a separate candidate waveform for the same target SRS. Evaluation on four held-out benchmark datasets shows lower mean and median spectral errors for the illustrated wavelet model than for the earlier direct time-domain CVAE and a sum-of-decayed-sinusoids (SDS) baseline. A conditioning study examines frequency-grid and encoding choices. Comparisons with published inverse-SRS methods demonstrate superior performance across four holdout benchmark datasets (two operational shock datasets, one earthquake dataset, and one synthetic shock dataset) and competitive performance on synthetic targets reported by Brake. Single-model inference requires approximately 0.1-0.6 ms per waveform during batched inference compared with 5-8 s for SDS, excluding offline training. This low inference cost enables rapid generation of distinct SRS-compatible candidate waveforms across the ensemble, although their physical suitability requires assessment beyond spectral agreement.

</details>


### [133] [TreeHCA: Hindsight-Guided Exploration and Credit Assignment for Multi-Turn LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12828)
*Debajoy Mukherjee,Jeremy Carleton,Sushil Vemuri,Srinivas Shakkottai,Dileep Kalathil*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TreeHCA将回溯引导的探索与回溯信用分配相结合，通过参考答案似然变化构建推理与工具交互树，并引入逆回溯重要性校正，利用归一化树回溯将终端结果传播为轮级优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结果监督的GRPO为LLM智能体轨迹中每个token分配相同优势，无法区分有用与无用的决策；回溯信用分配虽可细化反馈，但其估计依赖轨迹在关键历史处包含有信息的替代分支。

Method: TreeHCA利用参考答案似然的变化重访中间历史并采样替代续写，形成推理与工具交互树；针对探索改变采样续写分布的问题，推导相应的逆回溯重要性校正（含精确有限池恒等式）；采用归一化树回溯结构，将终端结果传播为轮级优势。

Result: 在七个问答基准和三个Qwen主干模型上，TreeHCA在聚合精确匹配指标上优于所有基线。

Conclusion: 共享的回溯结构是TreeHCA的核心设计原则，将信息性探索与回溯引导的信用分配联系起来，验证了配对探索与信用分配对多轮LLM智能体训练的有效性。代码见github.com/TheBlackCat22/TreeHCA。

Abstract: Outcome-supervised group-relative policy optimization (GRPO) assigns the same advantage to every token in an LLM agent trajectory, leaving useful and unhelpful decisions undifferentiated. Hindsight credit assignment (HCA) can refine this feedback, but its estimates rely on trajectories containing informative alternatives at consequential histories. We introduce TreeHCA, which couples hindsight-guided exploration with hindsight credit assignment. TreeHCA uses changes in reference-answer likelihood to revisit intermediate histories and sample alternative continuations, forming a tree of reasoning and tool interactions. Because this exploration changes the distribution of sampled continuations, we derive the corresponding inverse-hindsight importance correction, including an exact finite-pool identity. We use this structure in a normalized tree backup that propagates terminal outcomes into turn-level advantages. This shared hindsight structure is TreeHCA's central design principle, linking informative exploration with hindsight-guided credit assignment. Across seven question-answering benchmarks and three Qwen backbones, TreeHCA outperforms all baselines in aggregate exact match, validating the effectiveness of paired exploration and credit assignment for multi-turn LLM agent training. Our code is available at github.com/TheBlackCat22/TreeHCA.

</details>


### [134] [Cross-Behavior Policy Optimization: Unified Learning from Offline Demonstrations and On- and Off-Policy Rollouts via a Shared Regression Target](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12830)
*Du Cheng,Junyu Lu,Jingfan Zhang,Yuzhuo Zhang,Haoxuan Jia,Shichao Weng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Cross-Behavior Policy Optimization（CBPO），用固定参考目标的统一回归损失兼容离线示范、参考回答与历史rollout，实现SFT与RL目标合一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统“先SFT后RL”将两类目标分离，限制了对已收集回答的持续复用；离线示范与策略生成rollout难以在同一优化框架下兼容使用。

Method: 通过固定参考目标（不含生成策略）构建统一回归损失，使正向/负向离线完成、参考回答以及当前与历史rollout共享统一奖励标注记录格式与回放缓冲区；优化中无需协调SFT与RL两套目标，也无需存储行为策略概率、重要性权重或学习评论家。按可用数据源可派生出离线、同策略、异策略变体，CBPO-Unified 则整合全部来源。

Result: 在两种模型规模和四个数学推理基准上，CBPO 在各基准与数据机制下均取得领先的 avg@32 与 pass@32。Qwen3-1.7B 上 CBPO-Unified 相比 SFT DAPO，基准平均 avg@32 与 pass@32 分别提升 2.33 和 4.44 个百分点。回放消融中，保留历史rollout相比无历史配置提升 1.87 和 3.72 个百分点。

Conclusion: 统一回归式训练目标可有效整合离线与在线、历史与当前数据，简化推理后训练流程并持续提升数学推理性能，历史rollout的回放具有显著增益。

Abstract: Reasoning post-training draws on offline demonstrations and policy-generated rollouts, yet conventional supervised fine-tuning (SFT) followed by reinforcement learning (RL) separates their objectives and limits continued reuse of collected responses. We propose \textbf{Cross-Behavior Policy Optimization} (CBPO), which makes these sources compatible with one regression loss through a fixed-reference target. Because the generating policy does not enter this target, positive and negative offline completions, reference responses, and current and historical rollouts can share a common reward-labeled record format and replay buffer. As new responses enter the buffer, optimization retains the same regression objective without coordinating separate SFT and RL objectives or requiring stored behavior-policy probabilities, importance weighting, or a learned critic. Restricting the available sources gives offline, on-policy, and off-policy variants, while CBPO-Unified combines them. Across two model scales and four mathematical reasoning benchmarks, CBPO delivers leading \texttt{avg@32} and \texttt{pass@32} performance across benchmarks and data regimes. On Qwen3-1.7B, CBPO-Unified improves benchmark-averaged \texttt{avg@32} and \texttt{pass@32} over SFT DAPO by 2.33 and 4.44 percentage points, respectively. In the replay ablation, retaining historical rollouts improves these metrics by 1.87 and 3.72 percentage points over the no-history configuration.

</details>


### [135] [When Generative Models Act as Simulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12847)
*Sevan Brodjian,Michael Hobley,Pietro Perona*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究生成模型能否作为模拟器：只要改变潜表示能产生与编辑模拟器状态相同的下一步输出即可编辑。作者在Othello和Rayworld中比较探针解码+引导与逆映射两种映射，发现哪种可行强依赖环境，即使状态始终可解码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模拟器以可读可改的状态概括环境，而生成模型虽能无监督学习世界表示，却缺乏编辑世界状态的内置机制。作者希望探究生成模型在何种条件下可作为模拟器，并建立其潜状态与模拟器状态之间的映射。

Method: 将可编辑性定义为改变潜表示后能得到与编辑模拟器状态相同的下一步输出；模型仅用观测训练，映射事后再学。比较两种由“观测—模拟器状态”配对学得的映射：一是训练探针从模型中解码模拟器状态并沿其引导；二是用逆映射直接学习映射，在引导失败时仍可能成功。实验环境为离散的Othello与连续的Rayworld（部分渲染下运动圆盘，更接近物理场景与视频）。

Result: 可编辑性表现强依赖环境，尽管在所有变体中状态都可解码。Othello中可编辑性随游戏简化而平滑下降，微小规则变化也会造成巨大影响。Rayworld中探针无法沿模拟器连续状态引导，但逆映射仍能将模型设为该状态。

Conclusion: 生成模型可以被一个它可能从未学习过的模拟器驱动；探针引导与逆映射的成功与否取决于环境，而状态可解码并不保证可编辑。这为利用外部模拟器控制生成模型提供了可行路径。

Abstract: A simulator summarizes an environment in a state that users can read and change. Generative models learn representations of the world without explicit specification, but offer no built-in way to edit that world's state. We investigate the conditions under which a generative model can act as a simulator through $\textit{editability}$: whether changing a model's latent representation produces the next-step outputs we get by editing a simulator's state. Plainly stated, our goal is to find a mapping between a generative model's latent state and the simulator state, where the model is trained on observations alone and the mapping is learned afterward. We test two kinds of mappings, both learned from observations paired with simulator states. In one, we train probes to decode the simulator state from the model, then steer along them. In the other, we learn the mapping directly with an inverse map, which can succeed even where steering fails. We study both in two contrasting environments: the discrete board game Othello, and Rayworld, our own continuous world of moving discs seen through partial renderings, closer to physical scenes and video. We find that which mapping succeeds depends strongly on the environment, even though the state is decodable in every variant. In Othello, editability falls smoothly as the game is simplified, and even small rule changes have large consequences. In Rayworld, probes fail to steer along the simulator's continuous state, yet an inverse map sets the model from that state anyway. A model can therefore be driven by a simulator it may have never learned.

</details>


### [136] [Reinforcement Learning via Fixed-point Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12848)
*Denis Blessing,Onur Celik,Gerhard Neumann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出定点流匹配（FFM），一种无需训练数据的流策略训练与微调方法，用于最大熵与行为约束强化学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最大熵与行为约束RL的最优策略为Boltzmann分布，该分布通常复杂且多模态，采样困难。流匹配训练的流策略虽能表示此类分布，但传统上依赖训练数据，而在线RL中通常无法获得这些数据。

Method: 利用目标流恒等式（target flow identity）将actor更新表述为定点迭代，采用流匹配风格的回归目标，以critic的动作梯度作为回归目标，从而在无数据条件下训练与微调流策略；并证明最优策略是该迭代的不动点。

Result: 大量实验表明，FFM能在离线到在线（offline-to-online）设置中有效微调预训练流策略，并支持在线RL中从零开始高效且准确地训练流策略。

Conclusion: 定点流匹配是一种无需训练数据即可学习表达性流策略的有原则的方法。
