Abstract: In maximum entropy and behavior-constrained reinforcement learning (RL), the optimal policy is given by a Boltzmann distribution. Generating samples from this distribution can be challenging, as it is often complex and multimodal. While flow policies trained with flow matching provide a powerful framework for representing such distributions, they traditionally rely on access to training data, which is generally unavailable in online RL. To address this limitation, we propose fixed-point flow matching (FFM), a data-free procedure for training and fine-tuning flow policies in RL. FFM leverages the target flow identity to formulate the actor update as a fixed-point iteration, utilizing a flow-matching-style regression objective where the critic's action gradients serve as regression targets. We show that the optimal policy is a fixed point of this iteration. Through extensive experiments, we demonstrate that FFM effectively fine-tunes pretrained flow policies in the offline-to-online setting and enables efficient and accurate training of flow policies from scratch in online RL. These results establish fixed-point flow matching as a principled approach for learning expressive flow-based policies without requiring training data.

</details>


### [137] [Robust Langevin Sampling under State-Dependent Heavy-Tailed Noise with Applications to LLM Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12865)
*M. Berk Sahin,Ege C. Kaya,Arda Fazla,Behzad Sharif,Abolfazl Hashemi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文放宽了非对数凹采样中SG-LMC对全局Lipschitz梯度与一致有界噪声方差的假设，通过广义光滑性与状态依赖重尾oracle（有限p阶矩，1<p≤2），提出R-LMC、A-LMC与RA-LMC三种方法，并在不依赖耗散性或函数不等式的前提下给出Fisher信息下的非渐近收敛保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有非对数凹采样（SG-LMC）的收敛保证通常要求梯度全局Lipschitz且噪声方差一致有界，但这两项假设即便在基础采样问题中也可能失效，放宽它们更能反映LLM贝叶斯微调等实际应用的需求。

Method: 采用广义光滑性放宽梯度Lipschitz假设，并提出状态依赖重尾oracle（有限p阶矩，1<p≤2，p<2时允许无穷方差且涵盖BG-0条件）。分别与联合研究两类松弛，提出三种方法：R-LMC通过几何中位数中心化与残差裁剪处理重尾oracle；A-LMC在精确梯度下控制广义光滑性带来的离散化误差；RA-LMC结合稳健估计与噪声感知步长同时应对两类松弛。

Result: 对三种方法均在不假设耗散性或函数不等式的情况下建立了Fisher信息意义下的非渐近收敛保证。合成实验验证了FI收敛及各项机制的有效性；LLM与视觉-语言模型的贝叶斯微调实验支持所提oracle模型，并显示RA-LMC在准确率与不确定性量化上达到或优于当前最优基线。

Conclusion: 通过广义光滑性与状态依赖重尾oracle，可在更弱的假设下实现非对数凹采样的收敛保证，所提R-LMC、A-LMC与RA-LMC在理论与实际任务中均具可行性，尤其RA-LMC在大模型贝叶斯微调中兼具精度与不确定性量化优势。

Abstract: Existing convergence guarantees for non-log-concave sampling with stochastic gradient Langevin Monte Carlo (SG-LMC) typically assume globally Lipschitz gradients and uniformly bounded noise variance. Both assumptions can fail even in elementary sampling problems, and relaxing them better reflects practical applications such as Bayesian fine-tuning of large language models (LLMs). We relax both assumptions through generalized smoothness and our proposed state-dependent heavy-tailed oracle with finite $p$-th moments, $1<p\leq 2$. The oracle permits infinite variance when $p<2$ and includes the Blum-Gladyshev (BG-0) condition. We study these relaxations separately and jointly, proposing three methods. Under globally Lipschitz gradients, robust LMC (R-LMC) handles the proposed oracle through geometric-median centering and residual clipping. With exact gradients, adaptive LMC (A-LMC) controls discretization error under generalized smoothness. Robust adaptive LMC (RA-LMC) addresses both relaxations through robust estimation and a noise-aware step size. For all three methods, we establish non-asymptotic convergence guarantees in Fisher information (FI) without assuming dissipativity or a functional inequality. Synthetic experiments demonstrate FI convergence and the benefit of each mechanism. Bayesian fine-tuning experiments with LLMs and vision-language models support our oracle model and show that RA-LMC matches or outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in accuracy and uncertainty quantification.

</details>


### [138] [HART: Hybrid Attention-RWKV Time series Foundation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12883)
*Qingwen Lin,Boyan Xu,Shifeng Xie,Zhifeng Hao,Keli Zhang,Ruichu Cai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对RWKV-7在非自回归多步预测中的长时程性能退化问题，作者提出Placeholder Idling Decay（PID）机制解释该现象，并据此设计混合注意力与RWKV的时序基础模型HART，在长时程预测上以极小参数量取得优异表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非自回归多步预测（NAF）要求模型单次前向即可预测整个未来时程。RWKV-7虽具备强序列建模能力，但在NAF设定下存在长时程退化问题：未来片段被表示为缺失输入，递归状态需在无新观测的情况下跨越整个预测时程演化。作者希望揭示该退化的内在机制并加以缓解。

Method: 作者从RWKV-7的广义delta规则出发，分析并提出Placeholder Idling Decay（PID）机制：当占位符驱动的写入补偿不足时，反复的状态转移会衰减历史信息。逐层分析显示多数层状态注入范数下降，而转移矩阵范数在历史区与预测区之间相近，与PID一致。基于此提出HART（Hybrid Attention-RWKV Time-series Foundation Model），交替堆叠注意力块与RWKV块，兼顾对历史token的直接访问与递归时序建模。在GIFT-Eval与TIME-Benchmark上进行受控实验验证。

Result: HART在中、长时程上优于纯RWKV，短时程性能相当；总体表现优于同等规模的纯Transformer。以36M参数量在GIFT-Eval上取得长时程MASE 0.752，与Toto-2.0-1B（0.754）相当，参数量约为其1/28。

Conclusion: RWKV-7在NAF中的长时程退化可由PID机制解释。通过混合注意力与RWKV架构，HART在保持短时程性能的同时显著改善长时程预测，并以远小得多的参数量达到与大规模模型可比的长时程精度，表明架构混合是提升时序基础模型长时程能力的有效途径。

Abstract: Non-autoregressive multi-step forecasting (NAF) enables time series foundation models to predict an entire future horizon in a single forward pass. Despite its strong sequence modeling capabilities, RWKV-7 exhibits long-horizon degradation in our NAF experiments. In this setup, future patches are represented as missing inputs, requiring the recurrent state to evolve across the prediction horizon without new observations. Our analysis of RWKV-7's generalized delta rule suggests a mechanism we term Placeholder Idling Decay (PID): repeated state transitions can attenuate historical information when placeholder-driven writes provide insufficient compensation. Layer-wise analysis reveals reduced state-injection norms in most layers, while transition-matrix norms remain similar between historical and prediction regions, consistent with PID. Motivated by this analysis, we propose HART, a Hybrid Attention-RWKV Time-series Foundation Model that alternates attention and RWKV blocks, combining direct access to historical tokens with recurrent temporal modeling. Controlled experiments on GIFT-Eval and TIME-Benchmark show that HART improves over pure RWKV at medium and long horizons while maintaining comparable short-horizon performance, and achieves better aggregate performance than a comparably sized pure Transformer. With 36M parameters, HART achieves long-horizon MASE comparable to Toto-2.0-1B on GIFT-Eval (0.752 vs. 0.754), using approximately 1/28 of its parameters.

</details>


### [139] [CoGameReg: Cooperative-Game-Based Collaborative Semi-Supervised Regression for Seismic Reservoir Property Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12884)
*Zhiyu Guo,Jiwei Jia,Lei Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 CoGameReg，一种基于合作博弈的半监督回归框架，用异构回归器的贡献加权预测构建共享伪标签，在稀疏井控下实现地震储层参数预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从地震数据准确估计储层属性存在困难：井-log 标签稀疏，而三维地震观测数据丰富，标签与观测之间极不平衡。

Method: CoGameReg 采用合作博弈框架，用异构回归器的贡献加权预测生成共享伪标签，使每个回归器在学习目标中的影响显式化。核心为 Tri-Game Shapley 机制，结合基于 rank-MSE 的性能博弈与成对一致性博弈，从有标签训练数据估计非负回归器权重；权重在协同训练中固定，并复用于最终预测融合。候选伪标签在重训练前通过统一的局部 k 近邻误差下降准则筛选。

Result: 在 10 个公开回归数据集、一个合成三维 P 波速度预测任务以及一个实际含水饱和度应用中评估。基准优势在 2.5% 标签比例时最显著，随监督量增加而减弱；合成实验中获得最佳盲井指标；实际应用中，预测裁剪至 0–100%，三口盲井合并决定系数为 0.5942，均方根误差为 5.7441 个百分点，Pearson 相关系数为 0.8265，且在四种现场方法中 Pearson 相关最高。

Conclusion: 结果支持使用 CoGameReg 将稀疏井标签与密集地震属性相融合，但其性能在不同指标和井位之间存在差异。

Abstract: Accurate estimation of reservoir properties from seismic data remains challenging when well-log labels are sparse but three-dimensional seismic observations are abundant. We present CoGameReg, a cooperative-game-based semi-supervised regression framework that uses contribution-weighted predictions of heterogeneous regressors to construct shared pseudo-labels. This design makes each regressor's influence explicit in the learning targets generated for unlabeled seismic samples under sparse well control. The Tri-Game Shapley mechanism combines a rank-MSE performance game with a pairwise consistency game to estimate nonnegative regressor weights from labeled training data. These weights remain fixed during collaborative training and are reused for final prediction fusion. Candidate pseudo-labels are screened using a common local k-nearest-neighbor error-reduction criterion before retraining. We evaluate CoGameReg on 10 public regression datasets, a synthetic three-dimensional P-wave velocity prediction task, and a field water-saturation application. The benchmark advantage is most pronounced at the 2.5% labeled-data ratio and decreases as supervision increases. CoGameReg obtains the best blind-well metrics in the synthetic experiment. In the field application, predictions clipped to 0-100% yield a pooled coefficient of determination of 0.5942, a root mean square error of 5.7441 percentage points, and a Pearson correlation of 0.8265 across three blind wells. CoGameReg has the highest Pearson correlation among the four field methods. The results support the use of CoGameReg to integrate sparse well labels with dense seismic attributes, with performance varying across metrics and well locations.

</details>


### [140] [Personalized Federated Direct Preference Optimization via LoRA Adapters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12885)
*Zhanhong Jiang,Soumik Sarkar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出pFedDPO：共享全局基座+本地私有LoRA适配器的个性化联邦DPO框架，在理论上用有效异质性 κ²_eff 替代梯度多样性 κ²，实验上获得更低梯度范数且保持竞争性性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有FedDPO分析假设所有客户端收敛到单一共享策略，这与现实中普遍存在的异构偏好分布相矛盾，导致难以在保证隐私的同时实现有效的个性化对齐。

Method: 每个客户端在共享全局基座模型上维护一个小型私有LoRA适配器；每轮通过FedAvg同步基座模型参数，LoRA适配器保持本地更新，以捕获客户端特定的偏好模式，从而在共享与个性化之间做参数分解。

Result: 理论证明该分解将FedDPO界中的梯度多样性 κ² 替换为残余异质性 κ²_eff，当适配器吸收客户端特定方向时 κ²_eff ≤ κ²，代价是引入随适配器收敛而消失的额外适配器偏差项；在偏好数据集上，pFedDPO梯度范数持续低于vanilla FedDPO，训练损失与逐客户端奖励准确率保持竞争性。

Conclusion: pFedDPO通过全局基座与本地LoRA的参数分解，缓解了FedDPO单策略假设与异构偏好之间的冲突，在理论上给出更优的异质性刻画，在实验中展现更稳定的优化行为，是个性化联邦对齐的一种可行方案。

Abstract: Federated Direct Preference Optimization (FedDPO) enables privacy-preserving alignment of language models across distributed agents without centralizing sensitive preference data. However, existing FedDPO analysis assumes all clients converge to a single shared policy, which is fundamentally at odds with the heterogeneous preference distributions present in practice. We propose Personalized FedDPO (pFedDPO), in which each client maintains a small private LoRA adapter atop a shared global base model. In pFedDPO, the base model is synchronized via FedAvg each round, while LoRA adapters remain local and capture client-specific preference patterns. We prove that this decomposition replaces the gradient diversity $κ^2$ in the FedDPO bound with a residual heterogeneity $κ^2_{\mathrm{eff}}$, which is no larger than $κ^2$ when adapters absorb client-specific directions, at the cost of an additive adapter-bias term that vanishes as adapters converge. Experiments on preference datasets show that pFedDPO consistently attains a lower gradient norm than vanilla FedDPO while remaining competitive in training loss and per-client reward accuracy.

</details>


### [141] [GIST: Generator-state-Informed Supervision for Tabular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12904)
*Zhangyang Peng,Jingze Shi,Tongyi Zhang,Yuyu Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GIST方法，利用生成器中间状态对条件标签分布平均查询损失，替代硬标签监督，在不改变架构与推理的情况下提升表格基础模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于先验数据拟合网络（PFN）的表格基础模型（TFM）通常使用采样的硬查询标签，导致预训练中存在可避免的采样变异性。作者观察到生成器的中间状态能够揭示超出单次采样结果的标签不确定性。

Method: GIST（Generator-state-Informed Supervision for TFMs）：联合设计任务生成与查询监督，利用生成器中间状态在条件标签分布上对查询损失取平均；采用熵校准编码（entropy-calibrated encodings），在受支持的标签依赖分支上获得可计算且与输入一致的目标。

Result: 理论上，该平均保持总体目标，并在固定参数下于每个生成分布内不增大梯度协方差。在匹配预训练计划下，GIST在TabArena与TALENT两个基准、三种架构上均提升平均排名；跨基准与架构，GIST在数据集级配对比较中以62.1%的比例胜过对应的硬标签对照组。

Conclusion: 以生成器中间状态引导的软监督可有效消除硬标签采样变异，作为通用监督改进，无需改变模型架构或推理流程即可稳定提升TFM性能。

Abstract: Tabular foundation models (TFMs) based on prior-data fitted networks typically use sampled hard query labels, leaving avoidable sampling variability in pretraining. A key observation is that intermediate generator states can reveal label uncertainty beyond a sampled outcome. We propose Generator-state-Informed Supervision for TFMs (GIST), which uses these states to average query losses over conditional label distributions. GIST jointly designs task generation and query supervision, using entropy-calibrated encodings to obtain tractable, input-consistent targets on supported label-dependent branches. We show that this averaging preserves the population objective and yields no larger gradient covariance at fixed parameters within each generating distribution. Under matched pretraining schedules, GIST improves mean rank across three architectures on both TabArena and TALENT without changing architecture or inference. Across these benchmarks and architectures, GIST wins 62.1% of paired dataset-level comparisons against the corresponding hard-label controls.

</details>


### [142] [Controlled-Minimax: Stabilizing Adversarial Optimization via Control Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12915)
*Tianjiao Luo,Jianfei Chen,Jun Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文把极小极大优化视为连续时间动力系统，用二阶ODE建模训练动力学，指出非收敛根源在于Hessian特征结构不良，并提出可控化框架Controlled-Minimax，通过反馈控制器实现局部指数稳定，在多个病态基准与StyleGAN2-ada上抑制振荡并快速稳健收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极小极大优化是对抗学习的核心，但存在严重稳定性问题，常表现为持续极限环或发散。已有方法多为启发式正则化，缺乏对不稳定根源的理论解释与稳定性的理论保证，因此需要从动力学角度定位非收敛机理并给出可证明稳定的解决方案。

Method: 将训练动力学建模为二阶常微分方程（ODE），在连续时间框架下分析平衡点附近损失函数Hessian的特征结构，从而识别非收敛的根本原因；在此基础上提出Controlled-Minimax稳定化框架，把优化轨迹重新表述为可控物理系统，并推导具体的反馈控制器，使其不依赖Hessian谱即可获得局部指数稳定性保证。

Result: 在Dirac-GAN、二维高斯混合、Swiss Roll等病态基准以及StyleGAN2-ada高分辨率图像合成任务上进行大量实验，结果表明Controlled-Minimax能有效抑制振荡行为，并在传统梯度下降-上升方法失效的场合实现快速且稳健的收敛。

Conclusion: 极小极大优化的不稳定性源于平衡点附近Hessian的不良特征结构；将其重构为可控动力系统并施加理论推导的反馈控制器，可在不依赖谱条件的情况下保证局部指数稳定，从而稳定一般而言传统梯度下降-上升无法处理的极小极大问题，并在实验中验证了抑制振荡与快速稳健收敛的效果。

Abstract: Minimax optimization is fundamental to adversarial learning yet suffers from severe stability challenges, often manifesting as persistent limit cycles or divergence. This paper investigates these instabilities through the lens of continuous-time dynamical systems. By modeling training dynamics as a second-order Ordinary Differential Equation (ODE), we rigorously identify the root cause of non-convergence: the problematic eigenstructure of the loss function's Hessian near equilibrium points. To address this, we introduce Controlled-Minimax, a novel stabilization framework that reformulates the optimization trajectory as a controllable physical system. Unlike heuristic regularization techniques, our approach derives a specific feedback controller that provides theoretical guarantees of local exponential stability, regardless of the underlying Hessian spectrum. Consequently, our method stabilizes general minimax problems where traditional gradient descent-ascent methods fail. Extensive experiments on pathological benchmarks including the Dirac-GAN, 2D Gaussian mixtures, and Swiss Roll datasets, as well as high-resolution image synthesis with StyleGAN2-ada, demonstrate that Controlled-Minimax effectively suppresses oscillatory behavior while ensuring fast and robust convergence.

</details>


### [143] [DataCupid: Stable Matching Repair for Training Data Acquisition with Delayed Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12960)
*He Sun,Junyuan Mao,Wenhao Jiang,Mingjun Xiao,Shinan Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究效用变化后的匹配修复问题，提出RLR与RLR-P方法，在容量约束与乱序回报下实现可靠的再认证，并给出与残差端点负载相关的最优调度界及信息负载下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 效用变化后已有观测可复用，但新评估必须遵守匹配容量限制且回报可能乱序。需要一种在announced invalidations、有界且可能依赖奖励的回报延迟、以及不占用未来匹配容量的pending evaluations条件下，仍能保证正确性与效率的修复机制。

Method: 提出RLR：将可靠结果证书与批式匹配调度结合，处理样本赤字不等的情况；采用完整dyadic quota schedule，给出与终端残差端点负载相比系数cb<1.607的尖锐issue-round界，并分别给出延迟界与证据生命周期界。进一步提出RLR-P，在验收前监控完整issue-order前缀而不改变采样计划。对fixed priority-star markets且Bernoulli gaps未知的情形，提出PrioritySearch，分析其历史调整一阶常数、线性历史缩放与次对数回报延迟。

Result: RLR的dyadic quota schedule获得尖锐issue-round界，系数cb<1.607；并给出独立的延迟与证据生命周期界。确定纯oracle下，从同一drained初始态出发的单次修复耦合不增加认证时间、已发放观测数或到达可复用检查点的时间；同时导出信息负载下界。PrioritySearch在δ→0时达到期望认证时间的历史调整一阶常数。配对仿真与实测反馈回放显示RLR-P在平均封顶更新认证时间上优于受监控的width-based基线，但oracle CPU成本更高。

Conclusion: RLR/RLR-P在受限匹配容量与乱序、延迟回报条件下提供了具有理论保证的匹配修复框架，兼顾认证时间、观测发放量与检查点复用；PrioritySearch给出渐近最优的一阶认证时间常数。实证表明其在更新认证时间上具优势，代价是更高的oracle计算开销。

Abstract: Matching repair after utility changes can reuse valid observations, but new evaluations must respect matching capacity and may return out of order. We study announced invalidations with bounded, possibly reward-dependent reporting delays and pending evaluations that do not occupy future matching capacity. Residualload repair (RLR) combines sound outcome certificates with batched matching schedules for unequal sample deficits. Its complete dyadic quota schedule admits a sharp issue-round bound with coefficient cb < 1.607 relative to the terminal residual endpoint load, together with separate delay and evidence-lifetime bounds. RLR-P monitors complete issue-order prefixes without changing the sampling plan before acceptance. With a deterministic pure oracle, a single-repair coupling from the same drained initial state guarantees no increase in certification time, issued observations, or time to a reusable checkpoint. We also derive an information-load lower bound. On fixed priority-star markets with unknown Bernoulli gaps, PrioritySearch attains the history-adjusted first-order constant for expected certification time as δ {\sim} 0, under hypothesis-independent linear history scaling and sublogarithmic reporting delays. Paired simulations and measured-feedback replay quantify the trade-offs: in the replay, RLR-P has lower mean capped update-certification time than a monitored width-based baseline, at higher oracle CPU cost.

</details>


### [144] [Diffusion Precipitation Generation with Medium-range Weather Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12976)
*Guowen Li,Yang Liu,Mengxuan Chen,Jiashun Cheng,Haoyuan Liang,Shilei Cao,Yujie Wang,Juepeng Zheng,Fang Wang,Yerong Feng,Meng Jin,Hong Cheng,Yutong Lu,Haohuan Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出即插即用框架"动力学-降水解耦"（Dynamics-Precipitation Decoupling），用独立训练的扩散生成器将冻结天气模型的非降水分量映射为与观测对齐的逐日降水，在Pangu-Weather、GraphCast、HRES上均提升精度与事件检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逐日降水预报对天气敏感型决策至关重要，但现有天气基础模型（WFM）基于再分析数据训练，存在区域性与强度相关偏差；在观测目标上重训WFM代价高昂，因此需要一种无需重训的插拔式方案，将已有模型的预报校准到观测对齐的降水分量。

Method: 提出Dynamics-Precipitation Decoupling架构：以冻结的天气模型输出的非降水预报为条件，单独训练一个扩散生成器（diffusion generator）预测观测对齐的逐日降水，训练监督为卫星-雨量计融合降水估计。为应对降水分布异质性与日累积量同大气信号的时空对齐，采用多尺度循环编码器（multi-scale recurrent encoder）汇总大气演变，并在去噪过程中用交叉注意力（cross-attention）对齐大气与降水特征。

Result: 在全球与区域预报上对多种骨干（Pangu-Weather、GraphCast、HRES）评估，逐日降水精度与事件检测均有提升，局地累积型态刻画更好。全球集合评估在所有报告时效上Brier分数低于ENS与从头训练的GenCast，多数对比中CRPS更低。中国2288站点强降水个例研究进一步支持逐日累积型态与事件检测对比地面观测的改善。

Conclusion: 动力学-降水解耦是一种有效的即插即用后处理/生成式框架，可在不重训天气基础模型的前提下显著改善逐日降水预报的精度、事件检测与集合概率表现，具备跨骨干与跨区域的泛化能力。

Abstract: Accurate daily precipitation forecasts are important for weather-sensitive decisions. Although recent Weather Foundation Models (WFMs) have achieved significant progress, they are trained on reanalysis data, which exhibit regional and intensity-dependent biases. Since retraining WFMs on observation-based targets is costly, we study Dynamics-Precipitation Decoupling, a plug-and-play framework that uses a separately trained generator to map non-precipitation forecasts from a frozen weather model to observation-aligned daily precipitation. Its key challenges are to capture heterogeneous rainfall distributions and align daily accumulations with atmospheric signals that evolve across space and time. To address them, we train a diffusion generator conditional on atmospheric states and satellite-gauge estimates of precipitation. A multi-scale recurrent encoder summarizes atmospheric evolution, while cross-attention aligns atmospheric and precipitation features during denoising. Extensive evaluation of various backbones, including Pangu-Weather, GraphCast, and HRES, on global and regional forecasting shows improved daily precipitation accuracy and event detection, with better representation of localized accumulation patterns. Global ensemble evaluation shows lower Brier scores than ENS and training-from-scratch GenCast at all reported lead times, with lower CRPS in most comparisons. Remarkably, a heavy-rainfall case study using 2,288 stations in China further supports improved daily accumulation patterns and event detection against ground observations.

</details>


### [145] [Gaussian-Width Bounds for Offline-to-Online Linear Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12990)
*Kushagra Chandak,Volodymyr Tkachuk,Xiaoqi Tan,Csaba Szepesvári*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究部分覆盖离线数据下的随机线性bandit，通过高斯向量Lipschitz函数集中获得更紧的奖励预测误差控制，提出相位消除算法，遗憾界从Õ(√dkT)改进到Õ(k√T)，并给出匹配下界及纯探索结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线数据可辅助在线学习的随机线性bandit。关注"部分覆盖"情形：d个特征方向中仅k个覆盖不足。已有基于相位消除和乐观原则的算法分析给出Õ(√dkT)遗憾界，作者认为该依赖源于对奖励预测误差的控制过于宽松，希望获得更紧界。

Method: 利用高斯向量Lipschitz函数的集中不等式，对奖励预测误差进行更紧的控制；据此设计基于相位消除(phased elimination)的在线算法。同时构造部分覆盖下单位欧氏球上的匹配遗憾下界，以及均匀离线覆盖下的匹配上下界。进一步研究带离线数据的纯探索，针对固定设计算法给出简单遗憾的匹配上下界，并在部分覆盖与均匀覆盖下分析自适应算法。

Result: 新款相位消除算法在部分覆盖下达到Õ(k√T)遗憾，优于已有Õ(√dkT)；在单位欧氏球上证明部分覆盖的匹配下界，并在均匀离线覆盖下给出匹配的上下界；纯探索方面，对固定设计算法给出简单遗憾的匹配上下界，并对部分/均匀覆盖下的自适应算法给出相应界。

Conclusion: 已有Õ(√dkT)界并非本质，而是预测误差控制松弛所致；通过更精细的集中分析可将遗憾降至Õ(k√T)，且该界在部分覆盖下是最优的（下界匹配）。均匀覆盖情形亦达到最优，纯探索问题同样获得匹配界。

Abstract: We study how offline data can help online learning in stochastic linear bandits. We focus on partially covered offline data, where only $k$ of the $d$ feature directions are poorly covered. Existing analyses of algorithms based on phased elimination and optimism give regret bounds of order $\widetilde O(\sqrt{dkT})$ in this regime. We show that this dependence arises from loose control of the reward prediction error, and obtain tighter bounds using concentration of Lipschitz functions of Gaussian vectors. Based on this analysis, we develop a phased elimination algorithm with regret $\widetilde O(k\sqrt T)$. For the unit Euclidean ball, we prove a matching regret lower bound under partial coverage, as well as matching upper and lower bounds under uniform offline coverage. We also study pure exploration with offline data, providing algorithms and matching upper and lower bounds on simple regret for fixed-design algorithms and also for adaptive algorithms under partial and uniform coverage of offline data.

</details>


### [146] [Evidential Multimodal Transformers with Dempster-Shafer Fusion for Emotion Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13013)
*Muhammad Usama,Yeoreum Jung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出EMT-DS（证据多模态Transformer + Dempster-Shafer融合），用主观意见与Dirichlet分布建模多模态情感识别中的认知/偶然不确定性，并以冲突系数显式量化模态矛盾，在四个基准上提升准确率与校准性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于Softmax的多模态情感识别在传感器数据损坏或模态矛盾时（如面部表情示正效价、语音韵律示负唤醒）仍给出高置信预测，缺乏形式化不确定性量化，容易被迫形成无依据的一致性结论。

Method: 将预测建模为主观意见（Dirichlet分布），分离认知不确定性（证据不足）与偶然不确定性（传感器噪声）；各模态经独立Transformer编码器输出信念质量分布，再用Dempster组合规则融合，以冲突系数1/(1-c)对联合信念质量归一化，使矛盾证据被显式量化为冲突，融合结果变弥散而非强行共识。融合复杂度为O(MK^2)，低于跨模态注意力的O(M^2 d^2)。

Result: 在受试者独立/说话人独立评估下：IEMOCAP 84.3%（最优基线81.7%，p<0.01）、DEAP留一受试者 82.4%（78.1%）、高度不平衡KEMDy20达96.8%、CMU-MOSEI较最强证据基线提升2.4个点；消融中ECE由0.153（softmax Transformer）降至0.042；音频特征严重高斯噪声（σ=1.0）下保持71.2%（注意力基线降至45.3%），整模态缺失时优雅退化。冲突度量可标记错误：复查20%样本可覆盖80%误分类。

Conclusion: EMT-DS通过证据深度学习与Dempster-Shafer融合实现形式化不确定性量化，兼顾准确率、校准性与鲁棒性，且冲突指标支持临床与安全关键系统中的选择性预测。

Abstract: Softmax-based multimodal emotion recognition methods often assign high confidence even when sensor data are corrupted or contradictory, for example when facial expressions suggest positive valence while vocal prosody indicates negative arousal. We present an Evidential Multimodal Transformer with Dempster-Shafer Fusion (EMT-DS) that addresses this problem through formal uncertainty quantification. EMT-DS models predictions as Subjective Opinions (Dirichlet distributions), separating epistemic uncertainty (lack of evidence) from aleatoric uncertainty (sensor noise). Modality-specific transformer encoders produce belief mass distributions that are fused with Dempster's Rule of Combination, which normalizes the joint belief mass by a conflict coefficient 1/(1-c), so that contradictory evidence is explicitly quantified as conflict and the fused prediction becomes diffuse rather than being forced into an unjustified consensus. Under subject-independent and speaker-independent evaluation on four benchmarks, EMT-DS achieves 84.3% accuracy on IEMOCAP versus 81.7% for the best baseline (p < 0.01), 82.4% on DEAP under leave-one-subject-out evaluation versus 78.1%, and 96.8% on the highly imbalanced KEMDy20, and it improves accuracy on CMU-MOSEI by 2.4 points over the strongest evidential baseline. In controlled ablation, it reduces Expected Calibration Error from 0.153 (softmax transformer) to 0.042. It maintains 71.2% accuracy under severe Gaussian noise on audio features (sigma = 1.0), where attention baselines drop to 45.3%, and degrades gracefully when entire modalities are absent. The fusion step has O(MK^2) complexity, compared with O(M^2 d^2) for cross-modal attention. The learned conflict metric also flags likely errors: reviewing 20% of samples captures 80% of misclassifications, which supports selective prediction in clinical and safety-critical systems.

</details>


### [147] [Generalized Proximal Policy Optimization: Unifying LLM RL Algorithms from the Perspective of Divergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13015)
*Zhicheng Cai,Xinyuan Guo,Hanlin Wu,Weinan Dai,Yuyan Zhou,Wei-Ying Ma,Ya-Qin Zhang,Hao Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GPPO统一框架，从散度视角理解与设计LLM强化学习算法，通过"散度×估计器"二维设计空间提升推理能力，在数学、编程基准上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流PPO依赖几何退化的裁剪代理，无法自适应调节对策略偏移的阻力，导致过早裁剪与探索退化；后续变体多为碎片化、启发式修补，缺乏统一的理论解释与设计原则。

Method: 建立广义f-散度信任域理论并给出单调性能提升保证，揭示不同f-散度诱导的几何与策略更新动态；引入新的估计器拓扑，相比传统单样本蒙特卡洛估计更忠实地估计散度；将PPO、DAPO、DPPO、RIPO等统一为该框架特例，形成{散度×估计器}二维设计空间。

Result: 在八个竞赛级数学与编程基准上验证两个维度均关键；最佳GPPO实例在数学与编程任务上分别超过标准GRPO最高达50%和14%，取得SOTA性能。

Conclusion: GPPO提供了原理化、统一的LLM强化学习算法理解与设计范式，表明散度选择与估计器选择是性能的决定性因素，并为后续算法设计给出可扩展的二维搜索空间。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning has become the foundational paradigm for enhancing the reasoning capabilities of LLMs. Yet the dominant approach, Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO), relies on a geometrically degenerate clipping proxy that cannot adapt its resistance to policy shifts, leading to premature clipping and degraded exploration. Subsequent variants alleviate these issues through fragmented and heuristic modifications. We propose Generalized Proximal Policy Optimization (GPPO), a principled and unified framework for understanding and designing LLM RL algorithms through the lens of divergence. We establish a generalized $f$-divergence trust-region theory with a monotonic performance improvement guarantee, and show that different $f$-divergences induce distinct geometries and policy update dynamics. We further introduce novel estimator topologies that provide more faithful divergence estimates than the conventional single-sample Monte Carlo estimation. Under this framework, PPO and recent variants such as DAPO, DPPO, and RIPO emerge as special cases, establishing a principled two-dimensional design space of {Divergence $\times$ Estimator}. Extensive experiments on eight competition-level math and coding benchmarks show that both dimensions are critical. The best-performing GPPO instantiation surpasses standard GRPO by up to 50% and 14% on math and coding, delivering SOTA performance.

</details>


### [148] [Trajectory-Aware Policy Optimization: Closing the State Distribution Gap in Long-Horizon LLM RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13020)
*Zhicheng Cai,Weinan Dai,Yuyan Zhou,Wei-Ying Ma,Ya-Qin Zhang,Hao Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TAPO框架，通过对状态分布进行轨迹感知校正，解决长程自回归展开中局部策略失配累积导致的训练不稳定与崩溃问题，在八项竞赛级基准上显著超越GRPO等现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习已成为提升LLM推理能力的主流范式，但长程自回归展开存在根本性局限：局部策略失配会沿轨迹逐步累积为严重的状态分布偏移。现有方法（如GRPO、GSPO）仅在token或序列层面控制策略失配，忽视了这种由轨迹引发的状态分布偏移，导致随长度增长训练不稳定并最终崩溃。

Method: 提出轨迹感知策略优化（TAPO）。理论上从off-policy策略梯度中推导出状态分布校正项，并将其纳入RL目标函数以弥合状态分布差距；进一步采用几何平均估计器降低方差，并引入长度感知动态裁剪机制，防止轨迹上出现过大的偏差更新。

Result: 在八项竞赛级基准上，TAPO显著优于现有LLM RL算法；在数学推理和算法编码任务上分别较GRPO最高提升48%和12%，同时训练稳定性大幅增强。

Conclusion: TAPO通过将状态分布校正显式纳入RL目标，从理论上解决了长程轨迹中的状态分布偏移问题，配合方差缩减与动态裁剪，在性能与训练稳定性上均取得显著收益，为长程LLM RL提供了更优的优化框架。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has become a dominant paradigm for enhancing LLMs' reasoning capabilities. Yet long-horizon autoregressive rollouts introduce a fundamental limitation: local policy mismatch can compound into severe state distribution shift over trajectories. Existing LLM RL methods, such as GRPO and GSPO, control policy mismatch at the token or sequence level, but overlook this trajectory-induced shift, causing training instability and eventual collapse as the horizon grows. To close the state distribution gap, we propose Trajectory-Aware Policy Optimization (TAPO), a principled framework that theoretically derives the state-distribution correction from the off-policy policy gradient and incorporates it into the RL objective. TAPO further employs a geometric-mean estimator for variance reduction, together with a length-aware dynamic clipping that prevents overly deviated updates across the trajectory. Extensive experiments demonstrate that TAPO significantly surpasses existing LLM RL algorithms across eight competition-level benchmarks, achieving up to 48% and 12% improvement over GRPO on mathematical reasoning and algorithmic coding, with substantially enhanced training stability.

</details>


### [149] [Low-Rank Kolmogorov-Arnold Generator Flows for Interpretable Operator Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13028)
*Minjong Cheon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: KAFlow将物理算子生成元学习为低秩符号，用单变量KA函数构建，配以规范固定、精确指数积分与自动符号蒸馏，实现OOD泛化与闭式恢复，全面超越KANO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经算子应能从数据泛化到训练分布之外，并以闭式暴露所学物理规律。Kolmogorov-Arnold神经算子（KANO）通过在全部位置与频率上学习稠密伪微分符号实现了这两点，但其代价随分辨率平方增长，且闭式形式需人工读出。作者指出这种稠密符号是冗余的：有限阶变系数微分算子的符号，其分离秩被系数函数个数界定，与分辨率无关，而KANO在其自身基准上学到的符号秩仅为二。

Method: 提出Kolmogorov-Arnold flow（KAFlow）：将物理算子生成元学习为由单变量Kolmogorov-Arnold函数构成的低秩符号；固定这些函数的规范（gauge），使每一项具有唯一位置；采用精确指数积分器推进动力学；并自动蒸馏出算子的闭式表达式。

Result: 在KANO的合成基准上，KAFlow将分布外损失降低3.4至11倍，无需符号模板即可将每个系数恢复到10^-4以内，达到数据精度下限。在长时程量子动力学中，状态不保真度最多降低6个数量级，仅用位置与动量测量训练时降低30倍。KAFlow学习变阶分数算子的精度比现有算子网络高15倍，并能发现稀疏回归失败的nonlocal、分数及非多项式色散关系。

Conclusion: 低秩符号假设为物理算子的神经算子学习提供了兼具泛化性与可解释性的有效路径，KAFlow在精度、OOD泛化、闭式可读性与复杂算子（含分数阶、非局部）发现上均优于KANO及现有算子网络，且无需人工符号读出。

Abstract: Neural operators that learn physical laws from data should generalize beyond their training distribution and expose the learned law in closed form. The Kolmogorov-Arnold neural operator achieves both for position-dependent dynamics by learning a dense pseudo-differential symbol over all positions and frequencies, at a cost that grows with the square of the resolution and with closed forms that must be read out by hand. This dense symbol is unnecessary: a finite-order variable-coefficient differential operator has a symbol whose separation rank is bounded by the number of its coefficient functions, independently of the resolution, and the symbols that this network learns on its own benchmarks have rank two. We introduce the Kolmogorov-Arnold flow (KAFlow), which learns the generator of a physical operator as a low-rank symbol built from univariate Kolmogorov-Arnold functions, fixes the gauge of these functions so that every term has a unique place, integrates the dynamics with exact exponential integrators, and distills its closed form automatically. On the synthetic benchmarks of the Kolmogorov-Arnold neural operator, KAFlow lowers the out-of-distribution loss by 3.4 to 11 times and recovers every coefficient to within 10^-4 without symbolic templates, reaching the precision floor of the data. On long-horizon quantum dynamics, it lowers the state infidelity by up to 6 orders of magnitude, and by 30 times when trained only on position and momentum measurements. KAFlow also learns a variable-order fractional operator 15 times more accurately than existing operator networks and discovers nonlocal, fractional and non-polynomial dispersion relations on which sparse regression fails.

</details>


### [150] [Target-Constrained Pooling for Trajectory Extrapolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13034)
*Ning Wang,Claire Cardie,Sainyam Galhotra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出目标约束池化（TCP）方法，利用相关完整轨迹补全欠定的部分目标轨迹外推，在5个领域、双向预测下将RMSE平均降低35.8%–42.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从部分目标轨迹进行轨迹外推本质上是欠定问题，但与之相关的完整轨迹可提供关于未解析延续部分的信息，因此需要一种能有效利用这些相关轨迹来约束外推的方法。

Method: TCP将有限时域端点与延续形状分离，从两个互补源信号估计端点：均匀池化捕获最终响应水平，匹配增量池化在对齐源与目标状态后捕获剩余变化；随后选择代表性源，将两个端点估计投影到目标一致区域，转化为对自由预测的修正，并通过强度校准与退化感知加权控制其影响。

Result: 在5个领域、双向预测方向共25,245个目标上，TCP使全部10种仅用目标的预测器平均RMSE降低35.8%至42.1%，并改善全部100组预测器、数据集与方向的比较；两种端点信号均持续优于单独使用任一信号；代表性选择在全供体池的0.64%–1.02%误差范围内，仅用12.7倍更少的供体输入和138.8倍更少的匹配距离计算。

Conclusion: TCP通过分离端点与形状、融合互补信号并施加目标一致性投影与自适应加权，显著且稳健地提升轨迹外推精度并大幅降低计算开销。

Abstract: Trajectory extrapolation from a partial target trajectory is underdetermined, but completed related trajectories provide information about the unresolved continuation. We propose target-constrained pooling (TCP), which separates the finite-horizon endpoint from continuation shape and estimates that endpoint from two complementary source signals. Uniform pooling captures final response levels, while matched-increment pooling captures the remaining change after aligning source and target states. TCP selects representative sources, projects both endpoint estimates onto a target-consistent region, converts them into corrections of the free forecast, and controls their influence through strength calibration and degradation-aware weighting. Across 25,245 targets from five domains and both forecasting directions, TCP reduces mean RMSE by 35.8% to 42.1% for all ten target-only forecasters and improves all 100 forecaster, dataset, and direction comparisons. The two endpoint signals consistently outperform either alone, while representative selection remains within 0.64% to 1.02% of the full donor pool using 12.7x fewer donor inputs and 138.8x fewer matching distance computations.

</details>


### [151] [Towards Easier-to-Tune Training Algorithms: New Results from AlgoPerf](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13036)
*Priya Kasimbeg,Ahmed Khaled,Aahlad Chandrabhatta,Yifan Wang,Andy Liu,Niccolò Ajroldi,David Tweedle,Xingyu,Yang,Isaac Patole,Frank Schneider,George E. Dahl*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文报告了更新版 AlgoPerf V1 基准在自调优规则集下的结果：13 个 PyTorch/JAX 提交中 Schedule-Free AdamW 排名第一，并分析了步效率与墙钟时间的权衡及超参数敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估深度学习优化器时，超参数调优、优化器与模型协同设计等混杂因素会掩盖算法的真实性能，因此需要在固定硬件、统一规则下进行公平的墙钟时间对比。

Method: 采用更新后的 AlgoPerf V1 基准，在固定硬件上以墙钟 time-to-target 为指标，对九个工作负载评估新一批训练算法；聚焦自调优规则集，要求提交在全部工作负载上使用单一配置，共 13 个 PyTorch 与 JAX 提交。

Result: Schedule-Free AdamW 在 13 个提交中排名第一；步效率与墙钟执行存在权衡，矩阵预条件方法与双环方法样本效率较好但需更激进的算力优化才能在运行时上具备竞争力；超参数显著性与保留分析表明学习率和动量跨领域、跨算法高度敏感。

Conclusion: 需要更加鲁棒、能泛化到新任务的优化方法，以降低对超参数调优的依赖，同时兼顾样本效率与墙钟运行效率。

Abstract: Evaluating deep learning optimizer is challenging because confounding factors such as hyperparameter tuning and optimizer-model co-design often obscure true algorithmic performance. We present results of the updated AlgoPerf V1 benchmark, in which we evaluate a new set of training algorithms on wall-clock time-to-target across nine workloads on fixed hardware. This report focuses on the results of the benchmark's self-tuning ruleset, which requires submissions to use a single configuration across all workloads. Out of the 13 submissions in PyTorch and JAX, Schedule-Free AdamW ranked first. In addition to the wall-clock scores, we also analyze the trade-offs between step efficiency and wall-clock execution, revealing that matrix-preconditioned and two-loop methods achieve good sample efficiency but require more aggressive computational optimizations to be competitive in runtime. Finally, our hyperparameter saliency and retention analyses show that the learning rate and momentum are highly sensitive across domains and algorithms, highlighting the need for methods that are more robust against hyperparameter tuning and can generalize well to new tasks.

</details>


### [152] [CB-Orchestrator: Adaptive Workflow Optimization for LLM Agents via Contextual Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13064)
*Jiahang Sun,Zhiwei Shang,Zhipiao Liu,Hongwei Yang,Guoqing Xie,Shuang Qiu,Zhongxiang Dai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CB-Orchestrator框架，将LLM智能体的工作流生成与选择解耦：先通过进化搜索构建候选工作流池，再用上下文多臂赌博机进行高效选择，在五个基准上超越基线并在MATH数据集上显著降低训练阶段API token开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体通过智能工作流在复杂任务上表现优异，但人工设计工作流费时费力且难以适应多样查询；现有自动优化方法存在两个瓶颈：训练阶段反馈收集的API成本高昂，且无法自适应复用高质量工作流，制约了性能与成本效益。

Method: CB-Orchestrator将工作流生成与选择解耦为两阶段：第一阶段通过进化搜索构建多样化的候选工作流池；第二阶段将工作流选择建模为上下文多臂赌博机问题，通过平衡探索与利用实现样本高效学习，大幅减少训练所需反馈；推理时模型针对每个查询自适应选择最优工作流。

Result: 在五个多样化基准上，CB-Orchestrator持续优于所有基线。在MATH数据集上，其性能优于近期强基线，同时将训练阶段API token开销降低不少于40.55%，包含工作流池生成的总端到端训练token开销降低不少于29.73%。

Conclusion: CB-Orchestrator通过解耦生成与选择、结合上下文赌博机实现自适应工作流编排，在保证乃至提升性能的同时显著降低训练成本，验证了自适应复用高质量工作流对性能与成本效益的双重价值。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) agents have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in tackling complex tasks through agentic workflows. However, manually designing these workflows is labor-intensive and lacks flexibility to adapt to diverse queries. While automated workflow optimization is a promising direction, existing methods often incur expensive API costs during training-phase feedback collection. Furthermore, they fail to adaptively reuse high-quality workflows, which creates a bottleneck for both performance and cost-effectiveness. To address these limitations, we propose CB-Orchestrator, an adaptive framework that decouples workflow generation from selection. In the first stage, we construct a diverse pool of candidate workflows via evolutionary search. In the second stage, we formulate workflow selection as a Contextual Bandit problem, which enables sample-efficient learning by balancing exploration and exploitation, thereby significantly reducing the feedback required for training. During inference, the model adaptively selects the optimal workflow tailored to each query. Evaluations across five diverse benchmarks demonstrate that CB-Orchestrator consistently outperforms all baselines. Notably, on the MATH dataset, CB-Orchestrator achieves better performance than strong recent baselines while reducing training-phase API token overhead by no less than 40.55% and total end-to-end training (including workflow pool generation) token overhead by no less than 29.73%.

</details>


### [153] [Double-Take: Improving Capacity, Binding, and Selectivity In Long-Context Within Bidirectional Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13073)
*Vakul Raghavan,Utkarsh Grover,Mingyang Mao,Chengwen Wang,Xiaomin Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究长上下文下记忆可用性问题，提出Double-Take (DT)方法修复大块测试时训练的两个失败模式，指出容量、绑定、选择性是可用的长程记忆的三大要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 处理长序列并不保证远距离信息仍可用：固定大小记忆在上下文增长时需保留大量关联、跨位置连接信息并保持相关结构而不被淹没。这些需求彼此独立——记忆可能存储更多信息却无法表示检索所需的关系，也可能传递关系却丢失关键信息。因此核心挑战不是单纯延长上下文长度，而是构建随上下文增长仍可用的记忆。

Method: 在大块测试时训练（large-chunk test-time training, TTT）框架下研究该问题，识别出两个基本失败：单步写入累积干扰、限制容量；自关联写入无法形成跨位置绑定。提出Double-Take (DT)，通过多步非固定（unpinned）更新与跨位置混合修复上述失败，并加入双向变化敏感写入以实现选择性保留。

Result: 仅靠容量时端到端召回停留在0.10的随机水平；加入绑定后提升至0.92。仅在32 kb基因组上下文上训练，DT在131 kb处达到0.927 AUROC，在接近1 Mb处达到0.856，并能从人类零样本迁移到小鼠。

Conclusion: 容量、绑定与选择性是可用长程记忆的三个要求。

Abstract: Processing a long sequence does not guarantee that distant information remains usable. As context grows, a fixed-size memory must retain many associations, connect information across positions, and preserve relevant structure without being overwhelmed by everything it observes. These are distinct requirements: a memory may store more information without representing the relationships needed for retrieval, and it may transport those relationships without preserving the ones that matter. Thus, the challenge is not simply extending context length, but constructing a memory that remains usable as context grows. We study this problem in large-chunk test-time training (TTT) and identify two basic failures: single-step writes accumulate interference, limiting capacity, while self-associative writes cannot form cross-position bindings. We introduce Double-Take (DT), which repairs these failures with multi-step unpinned updates and cross-position mixing, and adds bidirectional change-sensitive writes for selective retention. Capacity alone leaves end-to-end recall at the $0.10$ chance floor; adding binding raises it to $0.92$. Trained only on $32\,\mathrm{kb}$ genomic contexts, DT reaches $0.927$ AUROC at $131\,\mathrm{kb}$ and $0.856$ near $1\,\mathrm{Mb}$, while transferring zero-shot from human to mouse. Together, these results identify capacity, binding, and selectivity as three requirements for usable long-range memory.

</details>


### [154] [Human and LLM Facilitation in Collaborative Design Meetings: A Computational Behavioral Comparison](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13082)
*Mohammed Azeez Khan,Aaron Dsouza,Ashutosh Mishra,Ummul Faiz Z. Bibi,Neha K. Nair,Amar Kumar Behera*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究对比了人类项目经理与Llama 3.1 8B在AMI会议语料库199组配对情境中的引导行为，发现人类在情境语义距离与模糊限制语密度上更高，Llama在话题发散度、词汇详尽性代理指标与阶段词汇对齐上更高，但强调这些差异不代表引导质量的高低排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM日益被用于促进群体讨论与协作，但LLM引导与人类引导之间的行为差异尚缺乏充分的刻画与量化对比。

Method: 从AMI会议语料库中提取情境化协作设计会议里人类项目经理的引导话轮，与Llama 3.1 8B Instruct的回应在199组配对情境中进行对比。分析涵盖五个验证性维度：情境语义距离、词汇详尽性代理指标、共情与模糊限制语的词汇密度、话题发散度、阶段词汇对齐。另有一项探索性指令性测量未通过领域效度检验，不予解读。

Result: 人类项目经理话轮在情境语义距离（r = 0.644）和共情与模糊限制语密度（r = 0.203）上更高，后者主要由模糊限制词驱动，故解读为词汇性模糊限制而非心理共情。Llama引导在话题发散度（r = 0.703）、词汇详尽性代理指标（r = 0.288，受长度混淆）和指令条件化的阶段词汇对齐（r = 0.382）上更高。一项无阶段提示的次要检验仍保留Llama更高的阶段对比结果。

Conclusion: 系统提示仍要求了引导动作，因此该检验不能作为自发性阶段意识的证据。这些差异不应被解读为引导质量的排序。所得到的行为画像为后续比较LLM引导者、人类引导者、提示、模型与协作情境提供了参照基准。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly being explored as facilitators of group discussion and collaborative work, but the behavioral differences between LLM-generated and human facilitation remain insufficiently characterized. We compare human Project Manager facilitation turns from scenario-based collaborative design meetings with Llama 3.1 8B Instruct responses across 199 paired contexts from the AMI Meeting Corpus. The analysis covers five confirmatory dimensions: contextual semantic distance, a lexical elaboration proxy, empathyand-hedge lexical density, topic divergence, and phase-vocabulary alignment. An exploratory directiveness measure failed domain validation and is not interpreted. The sampled human Project Manager turns were higher on contextual semantic distance (r = 0.644) and on empathyand-hedge lexical density (r = 0.203). The second contrast is carried mainly by hedge terms, so we read it as lexical hedging rather than psychological empathy. Llama-generated facilitation was higher on topic divergence (r = 0.703), the lexical elaboration proxy (r = 0.288; lengthconfounded), and instruction-conditioned phase-vocabulary alignment (r = 0.382). A secondary no-phase check left the Llama-higher phase contrast in place. The system prompt still requested a facilitation move, so that check is not evidence of spontaneous phase awareness. These differences should not be interpreted as a ranking of facilitation quality. The resulting behavioral profile provides a reference point for later comparisons of LLM facilitators, human facilitators, prompts, models, and collaborative settings.

</details>


### [155] [Can Failed Trajectories Reveal Where Agents Go Wrong? Agentic Failure Attribution Without Step-Level Labels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13105)
*Do Nguyen-Thanh,Junlin Fang,Bing Li,Zhen Fang,Sean Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SOAP框架，在仅知道轨迹失败但不知具体错误步骤（step-unlabeled）的设定下，利用谱空间打分与注意力引导的误差传播实现LLM智能体的失败归因，无需步骤级标注或成功参考轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体的失败归因对调试与改进系统至关重要，但现有方法依赖昂贵的LLM提示、步骤级错误标注，或需要已验证的成功轨迹作为参考，而成功轨迹缺乏失败信号，难以提供有效辅助。本文因此研究在步骤未标注的失败轨迹上进行归因。

Method: 提出SOAP（Spectral scOring with Attention-guided Propagation）。首先从LLM表征构建谱参考空间，依据每个步骤在该空间中的投影行为赋予基础错误分数；针对下游污染问题——后续步骤因继承早期错误而呈现更强失败信号、导致独立打分器偏向下游后果——SOAP利用注意力模式推导软步骤依赖关系，并将下游的错误证据向上游可能的源头传播。

Result: 大量实验表明，SOAP在归因性能上持续优于具有竞争力的基线方法。

Conclusion: SOAP在有竞争力的基线上取得一致的性能提升，证明其在无步骤级标签、无成功参考集的条件下进行失败归因的有效性，为智能体调试与改进提供了实用途径。

Abstract: Failure attribution for LLM agents aims to identify decisive steps responsible for task failure, which is critical for debugging and improving agentic systems. Existing methods rely on costly LLM prompting, step-level error annotations, or verified successful trajectories that lack failure signals for assistance. In this paper, we study failure attribution from step-unlabeled failure trajectories, where each trajectory is known to have failed but its decisive step is unknown. We propose SOAP (Spectral scOring with Attention-guided Propagation), an effective framework requiring neither step-level training labels nor a separate success-only reference set. SOAP first constructs a spectral reference space from LLM representations and assigns each step a base error score according to its projection behavior. Importantly, later steps may exhibit stronger failure signals because they inherit an earlier mistake, causing independent scorers to favor downstream consequences over the decisive error. To address this downstream contamination, SOAP derives soft step dependencies from attention patterns and propagates downstream error evidence toward its likely upstream sources. Extensive experiments show that SOAP consistently improves attribution performance over competitive baselines.

</details>


### [156] [Scaling RNA Foundation Models through Iterative Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13106)
*Pengan Chen,Liang Hong,Xinyuan Liu,Han Huang,Ziqian Lin,Jiayang Chen,Yimin Fan,Jiyue Jiang,Yu Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Eco-RNA 是一个 80M 参数的 RNA 基础模型，通过训练后可调的迭代精炼（推理深度）作为新的缩放维度，以 500 倍更少参数在多项 RNA 任务上超越 40B 的 Evo 2。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RNA 的生物学功能跨尺度编码，从局部序列模体到长程结构依赖。传统 RNA 基础模型将参数容量与推理深度耦合在固定架构中，为每次推理规定固定的计算图，缺乏推理阶段的可调计算能力。

Method: Eco-RNA 采用共享且以输入为条件的 Transformer 核心，通过重复应用对序列表示进行迭代精炼；迭代次数在训练后选择，从而在固定参数下控制有效深度。训练时跨越多个循环深度，使用掩码重建、状态正则化，以及将深层重建锚定到浅层参考的单侧损失（one-sided loss）。

Result: 在 70 项检测的 RNAGym 适应度基准上，精炼次数由 1 增至 4 使总体绝对 Spearman 相关从 0.242 提升至 0.286，且增益在 8 次迭代时仍保持。4 次迭代时，Eco-RNA 在全部三项总体适应度指标上超越当前 SOTA 的 Evo 2 40B，参数却少 500 倍；其迭代配置在所评估模型中推进了计算-性能 Pareto 前沿。此外在非编码 RNA 家族分类、RNA 二级结构预测和化学探测谱预测上均优于所评估基线。

Conclusion: 结果表明，内部推理计算可作为 RNA 基础模型的一种有效缩放维度，在固定参数下通过可调推理深度提升性能与计算效率。

Abstract: Biological function in RNA is encoded across scales, from local sequence motifs to long-range structural dependencies. Conventional RNA foundation models couple parameter capacity and inference depth within a fixed architecture, prescribing a fixed computation graph for each inference. Here we present Eco-RNA, an 80M-parameter RNA foundation model that makes inference depth adjustable through learned iterative refinement. Its shared, input-conditioned Transformer core refines sequence representations through repeated application. The iteration count is selected after training and controls effective depth at fixed model parameters. We train across recurrent depths with masked reconstruction, state regularization, and a one-sided loss that anchors deeper reconstruction to a shallow reference. On the 70-assay RNAGym fitness benchmark, increasing refinement from one to four iterations raises aggregate absolute Spearman correlation from 0.242 to 0.286, with the gain retained through eight iterations. At four iterations, Eco-RNA surpasses the current SOTA Evo 2 40B on all three aggregate fitness metrics despite 500x fewer parameters. Its iteration configurations advance the compute-performance Pareto frontier among the evaluated models. It also outperforms the evaluated baselines in non-coding RNA family classification, RNA secondary-structure prediction, and chemical probing profile prediction. These results establish internal inference computation as an effective scaling dimension for RNA foundation models.

</details>


### [157] [Continually Post-training Small Models via Synthetic Self-distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13113)
*Woosung Koh,Hyunsoo Lee,Kyungjae Lee,Haeju Park,Dahyun Lee,Dasol Hwang,Moontae Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文针对小模型持续后训练中"学习新能力与保留旧知识"的两难问题，指出自蒸馏微调(SDFT)依赖强上下文学习与指令跟随能力因而在小模型上失效，并提出合成自蒸馏(SynthSelf)，通过更大同族对齐模型生成分布内监督信号，实现小模型的有效持续学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续后训练需要同时兼顾新能力获取与旧知识保留。SDFT虽借助上下文示例的自监督缓解该冲突，但其有效性依赖强上下文学习与指令跟随能力，因此在面向低延迟、端侧部署的小模型上表现不佳。作者希望厘清小模型失败的根因，并使持续学习在小规模模型上可行。

Method: 首先进行失败归因分析，发现获取能力差与遗忘程度同后训练模型的分布偏移呈线性相关，说明分布内学习是必要条件。据此提出SynthSelf：将SDFT的分布内重写任务外包给一个规模略大的同族对齐模型来生成合成监督信号，并结合离策略(off-policy)监督与拒绝采样，从而在不破坏原有分布的前提下为小模型提供高质量监督。

Result: 在4个获取数据集(含一个混合数据集)和14个保留基准上评测，SynthSelf在3种小模型规模上均持续优于6种对比方法(包括离策略与在策略蒸馏)。

Conclusion: 分布偏移是决定小模型持续后训练成败的关键因素，分布内监督是必要前提；SynthSelf通过外置同族大模型的分布内重写，为小模型持续学习提供了可行且有效的路径，多项进一步分析验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Continual post-training faces a dual challenge of acquisition and retention: models must learn new capabilities while preserving prior knowledge. While Self-Distillation Fine-Tuning (SDFT) mitigates this using self-supervision with in-context demonstrations, it relies on strong in-context learning and instruction-following abilities. Consequently, SDFT fails on smaller models critical for low-latency, on-device use. To enable continual learning at smaller scales, we first identify the root cause of failure. We show that poor acquisition and forgetting correlate linearly with the post-trained model's distribution shift, revealing the necessity of in-distribution learning. We thus introduce Synthetic Self-Distillation (SynthSelf), imitating SDFT's in-distribution rewrite by outsourcing the task to a minimally larger, same-family aligner. Coupled with off-policy supervision and rejection sampling, this provides the high-quality supervision small models require without breaking their original distribution. Evaluations spanning 4 acquisition datasets (including a mixture) and 14 retention benchmarks show SynthSelf consistently outperforms 6 methods (including off- and on-policy distillation) across 3 small model scales. Extensive further analyses confirm SynthSelf paves the way toward effective small-model continual learning.

</details>


### [158] [WaSA: Wavelet Scattering Autoencoders for Unsupervised Offline Detection of Malicious Clients in Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13186)
*Alessandro Licciardi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 WaSA，一种完全无监督、离线的联邦学习恶意客户端检测框架，通过客户端上传的隐私保护频谱摘要与仅在干净辅助数据上训练的降噪自编码器检测恶意客户端，并结合玻尔兹曼采样进行客户端选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习虽能避免暴露原始数据，但仍易受恶意客户端投毒攻击。现有防御多为反应式（训练过程中干预）或有监督（需预先知道攻击类型），这些假设在开放式物联网部署中难以成立。因此需要一种无需攻击标签、可泛化到未知异常的无监督离线检测方法。

Method: WaSA 在训练前让每个客户端向服务器发送一份隐私保护的频谱摘要。服务器使用仅在干净辅助数据上训练的降噪自编码器，依据客户端数据偏离所学习到的良性流形的程度进行打分，从而识别污染，无需区分攻击类型。此外引入玻尔兹曼采样策略用于客户端选择，在硬排除与标准聚合之间平滑插值。

Result: 在 MNIST、CIFAR-10 和 CIFAR-100 上，针对多种攻击场景（恶意客户端比例高达 90%）的实验表明，WaSA 相比有监督基线和鲁棒聚合方法取得一致提升，且通信开销可忽略。

Conclusion: WaSA 是一种无需攻击标签、可泛化到未见异常的完全无监督离线恶意客户端检测框架，在极端恶意比例下仍具鲁棒性，并以极低通信开销实现优于有监督基线的性能。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) enables collaborative model training without exposing raw data, yet remains vulnerable to malicious clients injecting poisoned datasets. Existing defenses are typically reactive (operating during training) or supervised (requiring prior knowledge of attack types) - assumptions that rarely hold in open-ended IoT deployments. We propose WaSA, a fully unsupervised, offline framework for malicious client detection. Before training, each client transmits a single, privacy-preserving spectral summary of its data to the server. A denoising autoencoder, trained solely on clean auxiliary data, scores clients based on their deviation from the learned benign manifold, identifying corruptions regardless of the attack type. WaSA requires no attack labels, generalizes to unseen anomalies, and introduces a Boltzmann sampling strategy for client selection that smoothly interpolates between hard exclusion and standard aggregation. Experiments on MNIST, CIFAR-10, and CIFAR-100 under diverse attack scenarios (up to 90 % malicious clients) demonstrate consistent improvements over supervised baselines and robust aggregation methods, with negligible communication overhead. Code is available at https://github.com/alelicciardi99/wavelet_autoencoders_malicious.

</details>


### [159] [Does Learning Recurrent Networks Matter in Partially Observable RL?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13197)
*Zekang Wang,Zhe He,Gaojie Jin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探讨部分可观测强化学习中循环网络是否必须训练，发现冻结（不训练）的循环网络配合可训练非线性输出模块，即可在多种POMDP任务上取得有竞争力的控制性能，且可训练参数量不足全训练方案的一半。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部分可观测强化学习（POMDP）通常依赖循环网络编码时序信息，并与策略或价值函数联合训练。作者由此提出一个自然问题：学习循环网络是否真的必要？其动机在于借助储层计算（RC）与广义储层计算（GRC）的理论基础，检验固定（冻结）循环网络能否为POMDP提供足够的记忆能力。

Method: 研究一种简单架构：将固定的循环网络与可训练的非线性输出模块组合。在具有不同记忆需求、观测模态和动作空间的任务上进行评估，并与完全可训练的对应方案比较可训练参数量与性能。

Result: 冻结循环网络能够支持数百步的回溯记忆，并在多种POMDP上取得有竞争力的控制性能，同时所需可训练参数量不到完全可训练方案的一半。

Conclusion: 结果表明，学习循环记忆模块对求解POMDP并非总是必要。冻结循环网络为部分可观测强化学习提供了一个简单、参数高效且表现意外强劲的基线。源代码见 https://github.com/Aphlios/FRN-POMDP 。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) under partial observability typically re- lies on recurrent networks to encode temporal information, with the recurrent module trained jointly with the policy or value function. This raises a natural question: is learning the recurrent network actually necessary? In this paper, we investigate whether a frozen recurrent network can provide sufficient memory for solving par- tially observable Markov decision processes (POMDPs). Motivated by the theoretical foundations of Reservoir Computing (RC) and Generalized Reservoir Computing (GRC), we study a simple archi- tecture that combines a fixed recurrent network with a trainable nonlinear output module. We evaluate this approach across tasks with varying memory demands, observation modalities, and action spaces. Surprisingly, frozen recurrent networks support recall over hundreds of steps and achieve competitive control performance across diverse POMDPs, while using less than half the number of trainable parameters required by their fully trainable counterparts. These results suggest that learning the recurrent memory module is not always necessary for solving POMDPs. Frozen recurrent networks therefore provide a simple, parameter-efficient, and sur- prisingly strong baseline for partially observable RL. Our source code is available at https://github.com/Aphlios/FRN-POMDP

</details>


### [160] [S$^3$R: Fast and Faithful RL Rollouts via Self-Sparse Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13202)
*Yuhang He,Hongyu Ge,Zihan Lin,Siyi Liu,Yongqi Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 S$^3$R：用稀疏注意力草拟、全注意力验证的精确投机采样生成 rollout，在不改变 dense 策略响应组分布的前提下加速长上下文 RLVR 训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文 RLVR 的 rollout 生成需降低 KV-cache 访问量，且不能改变用于策略优化的响应组。实测中注意力占自回归解码核时间 72–76%。稀疏注意力虽减少读取，但直接从稀疏策略采样会改变学习用的响应组；对于组相对优化，即使做精确的按响应重加权，也一般无法恢复 dense 策略的期望更新，因为每个响应的优势依赖同组其它响应的奖励。

Method: S$^3$R 采用稀疏注意力提出 token，再通过全注意力验证与精确投机采样生成响应；草拟与验证共享同一策略参数，无需训练或更新独立 drafter，使响应组服从 dense 策略分布。并刻画加速条件及草拟与验证开销；针对 dense 验证仍需完整历史 KV cache 的问题，设计 KV-aware 调度器以限制 KV-cache 反复换出/换入，同时保持 rollout 并行度。

Result: 在激进稀疏草拟预算下的匹配消融中，S$^3$R 保持训练稳定，而带训练侧校正的直接稀疏采样变得不稳定。在长上下文数学推理任务上，S$^3$R 在 Qwen3 模型上取得自回归 rollout 的 1.39×–1.35× 端到端训练吞吐，任务性能相当。

Conclusion: 通过稀疏草拟+全注意力验证的精确投机采样，S$^3$R 在不改变 dense 策略响应组的前提下加速长上下文 RLVR 训练，并配合 KV-aware 调度缓解显存搬运开销，实现稳定训练与吞吐提升。

Abstract: Fast rollout generation for long-context reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) requires reducing KV-cache traffic without changing the response groups used for policy optimization. In our profiled RLVR workloads, attention accounts for 72--76\% of autoregressive decoding kernel time. Sparse attention reduces these reads, but sampling directly from a sparse policy changes the response groups used for learning. For group-relative optimization, we show that even exact response-wise reweighting does not generally recover the expected dense-policy update because the advantage of each response depends on the rewards of other group members. We propose Self-Sparse Speculative Rollouts (S$^3$R). It uses sparse attention to propose tokens, then generates responses through full-attention verification and exact speculative sampling. Drafting and verification share the same policy parameter, eliminating the need to train or update a separate drafter. The resulting response groups follow the dense-policy distribution. Under S$^3$R, we characterize speedup conditions and the costs of drafting and verification. We found that dense verification still requires the complete historical KV cache. A KV-aware scheduler therefore limits KV-cache repeated offloading and restoration while maintaining rollout parallelism. % OR thrashing In a matched ablation with an aggressive sparse drafting budget, S$^3$R maintains stable training while direct sparse sampling with training-side correction becomes unstable. On long-context mathematical reasoning, S$^3$R achieves $1.39\times~1.35\times$ the end-to-end training throughput of autoregressive rollouts on Qwen3 model, with comparable task performance.

</details>


### [161] [Rethinking Relative Policy Differences Transfer of On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13205)
*Rong Wu,Daocheng Fu,Yu Yang,Xuemeng Yang,Siqi Li,Shu Zou,Yao Zhu,Botian Shi,Bo Zhang,Cong Zhang,Pinlong Cai,Jianbiao Mei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将“代理专家—代理参考”策略的log概率比视为KL正则化策略优化中的隐式奖励，统一刻画on-policy蒸馏，并系统回答相对策略差异在何种条件下能作为有效监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统on-policy蒸馏依赖教师显著强于学生，近期工作通过迁移专家与参考策略间的相对差异放宽该要求，但何种差异有用、何种目标模型能受益仍不清楚。

Method: 提出统一视角：代理专家与代理参考的log概率比定义KL正则化策略优化中的隐式奖励，导出目标策略的on-policy更新，并将传统OPD作为特例，支持跨规模、训练阶段与生成模型的any-to-any迁移；据此开展系统实验。

Result: 三点发现：一，更强的专家未必是更好的监督来源，任务特定训练（RL、SFT）诱导的差异可稳定迁移，而单纯规模差异迁移弱或不迁移；二，专家与参考互补，专家提供待迁移能力，参考过滤共享行为以隔离残差；三，有用差异并非对任意目标都可迁移，需使更新朝向专家且在采样轨迹外泛化，原始策略距离无法预测可迁移性，而合适的参考可挽救失败迁移。

Conclusion: 相对策略差异仅在差异来源为任务特定训练、参考有效过滤共享行为、且目标可朝向专家泛化时才构成有效蒸馏监督；策略距离本身不具预测力，参考的设计是可迁移性的关键杠杆。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) trains a target policy on its own rollouts using token-level supervision from a stronger teacher, but typically relies on the teacher being substantially more capable than the target. Recent work relaxes this requirement by transferring the relative policy difference between an expert and a reference policy rather than the expert's absolute distribution. Yet it remains unclear which differences provide useful supervision and which targets can benefit from them. We develop a unified view in which the log-probability ratio between a proxy expert and a proxy reference defines an implicit reward for KL-regularized policy optimization. This formulation induces an on-policy update for the target, recovers conventional OPD as a special case, and enables any-to-any transfer across models that differ in scale, training stage or generation. Our systematic experiments yield three findings. First, a stronger expert is not necessarily a better source of supervision: differences induced by task-specific training, including reinforcement learning and supervised fine-tuning, transfer consistently, whereas those arising from model scale alone transfer weakly or not at all. Second, the expert and reference serve complementary roles: the expert provides the capability to be transferred, while the reference filters out shared behavior and isolates the relevant residual. Third, even a useful difference does not transfer to every target: success requires the induced update to move the target toward the expert and generalize beyond sampled rollouts. Raw policy distance does not predict transferability, whereas an appropriate reference can rescue otherwise unsuccessful transfer. These results characterize when relative policy differences provide effective supervision for OPD.

</details>


### [162] [How Much Computation Does Every Forecast Need? Structured Block Skipping in Two Time-Series Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13208)
*Max Bourgeat*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究测试了在推理阶段整块移除预训练时间序列基础模型（Chronos-Bolt、TimesFM 2.5）Transformer块的可能性，发现存在结构冗余且加速潜力依赖输入，但尚无法形成可部署的动态推理方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列基础模型对所有输入采用统一网络深度，而不同预测窗口在结构与难度上存在差异，因此作者探究能否在推理时跳过整个Transformer块以实现加速，并验证跨架构的冗余性。

Method: 设计可旁路（bypass）整块的方案，使全部块启用时严格等价于稠密模型；掩码仅在校准窗口上选择，并在ETTh1、Traffic、Weather、Exchange的1,088个留出窗口上评估，同时测量A100单样本批量延迟。

Result: 中等稀疏度下，Chronos-Bolt保留7/11块、TimesFM保留16/20块时，各数据集平均相对预测质量变化很小，中位延迟分别降低11.2%和13.4%；更激进地跳过TimesFM块可进一步加速但显著损害Traffic和Weather。不同等预算掩码在不同窗口上胜出，事后oracle显著优于最佳静态掩码，但数据集专属掩码迁移不稳定，Chronos表示不支持可靠的未见域路由。

Conclusion: 结果证明了跨架构的结构化冗余与输入依赖的加速机会，但尚不足以构成可部署的动态推理方法。

Abstract: Time-series foundation models use the same network depth for every input, although forecasting windows differ in structure and difficulty. We test whether whole Transformer blocks can be removed at inference time in two pretrained models: Chronos-Bolt and TimesFM 2.5. Our bypasses avoid the original block computation and preserve the dense model exactly when all blocks are enabled. Masks are selected only on calibration windows and evaluated on 1,088 held-out windows from ETTh1, Traffic, Weather, and Exchange. At moderate sparsity, Chronos-Bolt with 7/11 candidate blocks and TimesFM with 16/20 blocks show little change in mean relative forecasting quality across datasets while reducing median A100 batch-1 latency by 11.2% and 13.4%. More aggressive TimesFM skipping accelerates inference further but substantially harms Traffic and Weather. Across both models, different equal-budget masks win on different windows, and a retrospective oracle significantly improves over the best static mask on every dataset. However, dataset-specific masks transfer inconsistently, and Chronos representations do not support reliable unseen-domain routing. These results establish cross-architecture structured redundancy and input-dependent opportunity, but not a deployable dynamic inference method.

</details>


### [163] [Monarch-MoE: Structured Experts for Multimodal Continual Instruction Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13215)
*Xi Zhang,Boyu Diao,Zesen Chen,Ruiqi Liu,Hangda Liu,Yongjun Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Monarch-MoE，用块对角因子与固定置换替换 LoRA 专家中的稠密因子，实现块级梯度隔离，在显著压缩适配器参数的同时缓解多模态持续指令微调中的灾难性遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态持续指令微调中学习新任务会导致对已有知识的灾难性遗忘。多专家 LoRA 虽将学习分散到各专家，但对稠密低秩专家的更新仍会影响大量输出特征，遗忘问题未被根本解决；同时专家结构在显存受限硬件上部署困难。

Method: Monarch-MoE：将 LoRA 专家中的稠密低秩因子替换为由固定置换连接的块对角（Monarch）因子。该结构提供块级梯度隔离——单个块对更新仅影响一个输出块，同时通过置换保持所有输入块与输出块的连通性。为在显存受限硬件上实用化，结合批量化计算、融合前向算子与优化的反向传播。

Result: 在 MLLM-DCL 与 UCIT 上以 LLaVA-1.5-7B 评测三种持续学习方法：首任务保持能力提升，适配器参数分别减少约 75% 与 50%；主对比中首任务遗忘低至 MLLM-DCL 0.52 个百分点、UCIT 0.10 个百分点，最终性能存在方法相关的权衡。实现支持在单张 24 GB RTX 4090 上微调与推理，实测显存更低、运行时间具竞争力。

Conclusion: 算法与系统层面的改进共同使结构化专家成为显存受限硬件上持续适配的可行方案。

Abstract: Learning new tasks in multimodal continual instruction tuning can cause catastrophic forgetting of previously acquired knowledge. Although multi-expert LoRA distributes learning across experts, updates to a dense low-rank expert can still affect many output features. We introduce Monarch-MoE, which replaces the dense factors in LoRA experts with block-diagonal factors connected by a fixed permutation. This structure provides blockwise gradient isolation: individual block-pair updates affect only one output block, while connections between all input and output blocks are maintained. To make these compact experts practical on memory-limited hardware, we combine batched computation, fused forward operators, and an optimized backward pass. Across three continual-learning methods evaluated on MLLM-DCL and UCIT with LLaVA-1.5-7B, Monarch-MoE improves first-task retention while reducing adapter parameters by approximately 75% and 50%, respectively. In the main comparison, first-task forgetting is as low as 0.52 percentage points on MLLM-DCL and 0.10 on UCIT, with method-dependent trade-offs in final performance. Our implementation supports fine-tuning and inference on a single 24 GB RTX 4090, with lower measured memory use and competitive runtime in the evaluated settings. Together, these algorithmic and systems improvements make structured experts a practical option for continual adaptation on memory-limited hardware.

</details>


### [164] [Tensor-based Fusion of Multi-modal Data for Predicting Cancer Risk Levels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13221)
*Ali Anaissi,Yuanzhe Jia,Qishun Peng,Weidong Huang,Ali Braytee,Junaid Akram*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个无监督机器学习框架，整合特征提取、张量分解与聚类，用于多模态癌症数据的风险分层，并在LinkedOmics的11,158例、32种癌症数据上验证，tSVD+GMM组合表现最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态诊断技术产生的高维数据集虽富含病理信息，但在解读、降维和临床适用性方面存在挑战；传统机器学习方法难以提取有效的跨模态特征，导致患者风险分层的预测精度受限。

Method: 构建无监督框架，融合特征提取、张量分解与聚类。采用张量分解融合异质基因组与临床数据以提取潜在且具生物学意义的特征；比较多种特征提取策略，并用多种聚类算法对特征进行聚类；通过内部与外部指标进行对比评估以筛选最优配置。

Result: 在LinkedOmics数据集（11,158名患者、32种癌症类型）上验证，以肿瘤纯度、病理分期和生存结局等临床相关属性作为风险分层的真值指标；实验显示tSVD+GMM组合在各临床基准上均持续优于其他配置。

Conclusion: 所提出的无监督多模态框架能有效提取跨模态潜在特征并提升癌症风险分层性能，其中tSVD+GMM为最优配置。

Abstract: Cancer diagnosis and treatment increasingly rely on multi-modal diagnostic technologies that generate high-dimensional datasets. Although these datasets are rich in pathological information, they pose considerable challenges in interpretation, dimensionality reduction, and clinical applicability. Conventional machine learning approaches often fail to extract meaningful cross-modal features, thereby limiting predictive accuracy in patient risk stratification. To address these limitations, this paper proposes an unsupervised machine learning framework that integrates feature extraction, tensor decomposition, and clustering to improve cancer risk prediction. The framework employs tensor decomposition to fuse heterogeneous genomic and clinical data, enabling the extraction of latent, biologically meaningful features that outperform unimodal feature sets. Multiple feature extraction strategies are investigated, and the resulting features are subsequently clustered using a range of clustering algorithms. Comparative evaluations based on internal and external metrics are then used to identify the most effective configurations. The framework is validated on LinkedOmics data comprising 11,158 patients across 32 cancer types. For patient risk stratification, clinically relevant attributes such as tumor purity, pathological stage, and survival outcomes serve as ground-truth indicators. Experimental results demonstrate the superiority of tSVD+GMM, which consistently outperforms other configurations across clinical benchmarks.

</details>


### [165] [ReSFlow: Rectifying Categorical Flows on Statistical Manifolds for One-Step Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13225)
*Xiaohuan Jia,Renzhe Xu,Xiao Wang,Jiayun Wu,Shaohua Fan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ReSFlow，通过在统计流形上学习测地线轨迹对连续流进行整流，实现单步离散数据（类别）生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散数据的生成建模依赖统计流形上的连续向量场，但现有连续时间流未受测地线约束，需多步数值积分，推理计算成本高。

Method: 借鉴欧氏空间Rectified Flow的"直且快"特性，在统计流形上对连续流进行整流，显式学习遵循内在黎曼几何的测地线轨迹，并理论保证先验与目标分布间的轨迹近似测地线。

Result: 在图像、文本到生物领域的多种真实生成任务上，ReSFlow展现出优越的单步生成质量。

Conclusion: ReSFlow通过沿测地线整流流，降低传输代价并能单步将纯噪声映射为目标离散数据，实现测地最优且快速的类别生成。

Abstract: Generative modeling for discrete data has been significantly advanced by learning continuous vector fields on the statistical manifolds to transport simple priors to target discrete distributions. However, because these continuous-time flows are not constrained to follow geodesics on the statistical manifold, thereby requiring multi-step numerical integration for simulation and leading to substantial computational costs during inference. In this work, drawing inspiration from the "straight and fast" properties of Rectified Flow in continuous Euclidean space, we propose the Rectified Statistical Flow (ReSFlow) model, which enables one-step categorical generation on statistical manifolds by explicitly learning geodesic trajectories that adhere to the intrinsic Riemannian geometry. By rectifying the flow on the statistical manifold, we theoretically guarantee that trajectories between prior and target distributions approximate geodesics. This rectification significantly reduces the transport cost by aligning the learned trajectories directly along geodesics on the statistical manifold, allowing the model to map pure noise to target discrete data in a single step. Through extensive experiments on diverse real-world generative tasks, ranging from image and text to biological domains, we demonstrate ReSFlow's superior one-step generation quality, which truly embodies geodesically optimal and rapid categorical generation. Our code is available at https://github.com/xiaohuanjia/ReSFlow.

</details>


### [166] [Learning Schwarzschild Geodesic Motion with Lagrangian Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13234)
*Sukru Caglar,Sena Kalabalik,Abdullah Umut Hamzaogullari,Arkadas Ozakin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出两项协同改进——解耦位置-速度归一化与基于ISCO的能量约束对数均匀采样数据集生成策略，使拉格朗日神经网络（LNNs）首次成功学习史瓦西测地线动力学，能准确再现近日点进动和相对论时间膨胀，在多圈轨道后能量与角动量守恒误差仅几个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拉格朗日神经网络（LNNs）通过参数化标量拉格朗日量并借助欧拉-拉格朗日方程导出加速度，具有变分归纳偏置，物理一致性优于纯数据驱动模型，近期已被推广到广义相对论场景，但史瓦西测地线仍是未解决的开放挑战。其困难在于：相对论系统动力学多样、数值不稳定，且难以充分采样物理相关且多样的轨道区域。

Method: 提出两项协同改进：（1）解耦的位置-速度归一化方案，独立地对每个坐标及其时间导数重新缩放，并重加权加速度目标，以平衡所有通道的贡献；（2）基于最内稳定圆轨道（ISCO）结构、能量约束的对数均匀采样数据集生成策略。

Result: 模型准确再现近日点进动与相对论时间膨胀；在完成绕中心质量的多圈轨道后，能量与角动量守恒误差在几个百分点以内；并能在近场、中场和远场三种区域下的八种轨道场景中预测轨道轨迹。

Conclusion: 结果表明，结合物理动机的归一化与采样后，LNNs能够可靠学习弯曲时空中的测地线动力学。该工作首次成功在史瓦西测地线上训练LNNs，为复杂相对论系统的数据驱动建模提供了有前景的方向。

Abstract: Lagrangian Neural Networks (LNNs) learn dynamics from trajectory data by parameterizing a scalar Lagrangian and deriving accelerations through the Euler--Lagrange equation, providing a variational inductive bias that improves physical consistency over purely data-driven models. LNNs have recently been extended to general relativistic settings, leaving Schwarzschild geodesics as an open challenge. Training on complex relativistic systems is hindered by the diversity of the dynamics, as well as numerical instabilities and the difficulty of adequately sampling physically relevant, diverse orbital regimes. We introduce two coordinated improvements: a decoupled position--velocity normalization scheme that independently rescales each coordinate and its time derivatives, reweighting acceleration targets to balance contributions across all channels; and an energy-constrained log-uniformly sampled dataset generation strategy informed by the innermost stable circular orbit (ISCO) structure. With these improvements, our model accurately reproduces perihelion precession and relativistic time dilation, conserves energy and angular momentum to within a few percent even after completing multiple orbits around the central mass and predicts orbital trajectories across eight orbital scenarios in close, medium, and far-field regimes. These results establish that LNNs, augmented with physically motivated normalization and sampling, can reliably learn geodesic dynamics in curved spacetime. Our work also represents the first successful training of LNNs on Schwarzschild geodesics and offers a promising direction for data-driven modeling of complex relativistic systems.

</details>


### [167] [State-OPD: State-Grounded On-Policy Distillation for Multi-Turn Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13238)
*Zhentao Zhang,Yanzhao Zheng,Yanlin Liu,Yuanqiang Yu,Tianze Xu,Chao Ma,Jihuai Zhu,Lelun Wu,Baohua Dong,Hangcheng Zhu,Ruohui Huang,Gang Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 State-Grounded On-Policy Distillation (State-OPD)，通过交互状态视角研究多轮智能体场景下的在线策略蒸馏（OPD），将教师监督锚定于交互状态并按响应熵重新分配采样预算，在 ALFWorld、ScienceWorld、WebShop 上取得所评估方法中最高的宏平均成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将前沿模型的推理能力蒸馏到小模型已成为有效范式，但将其扩展到多轮智能体环境仍具挑战。现有方法未能区分"交互状态"（客观任务与已执行的动作-观测记录）与学生内部语义推理轨迹，导致学生推理在蒸馏中的作用被忽视，教师监督受到污染且采样效率不足。

Method: 以交互状态为分析视角，识别学生推理在 OPD 中的两种角色：历史（prior）推理会扭曲教师监督，当前（current）推理塑造同一状态下的动作候选项。据此提出 State-OPD 框架：将教师监督锚定在交互状态上，并在高响应熵状态处把采样预算重新分配给"完整推理-动作"响应生成，同时保留全历史的学生生成。

Result: 在 ALFWorld、ScienceWorld 与 WebShop 上，State-OPD 对 Qwen3-1.7B 与 Qwen3-4B 学生均取得所评估方法中最高的宏平均成功率；Qwen3-4B 的宏平均成功率由最强基线的 26.07% 提升至 31.74%，并在 ALFWorld Seen 上超越了 Qwen3-32B 教师。分析显示，State-OPD 以更少受监督的轮次响应实现更高成功率，并显著减少持续性失败动作的重复；定性轨迹展示了基于交互反馈的决策修正。

Conclusion: 区分交互状态与学生推理轨迹是多轮智能体 OPD 的关键；将监督锚定于交互状态并对完整推理-动作响应进行预算重分配，能在更少监督开销下提升任务成功率与决策灵活性，并使学生模型在部分任务上超越更大规模的教师模型。

Abstract: On-Policy Distillation (OPD) has emerged as a powerful paradigm for transferring reasoning capabilities from frontier models to smaller models, while extending its success to multi-turn agentic environments remains challenging. We study agentic OPD through interaction states, distinguishing the objective task and executed action-observation record from the internal semantic trajectory of student reasoning. Through this lens, we identify two roles of student reasoning in multi-turn OPD: prior reasoning distorts teacher supervision, while current reasoning shapes the action alternatives sampled at the same state. Specifically, at matched interaction states, removing prior student reasoning improves teacher continuation success and changes the teacher's token-level targets, with larger continuation gains at later turns. Meanwhile, resampling complete reasoning-action responses yields greater action diversity at the same state than resampling actions under a fixed reasoning trace. We introduce State-Grounded On-Policy Distillation, a framework that grounds teacher supervision in interaction states and reallocates the sampling budget to generate multiple complete reasoning-action responses at states with high response entropy, while retaining full-history student generation. Comprehensive evaluations across ALFWorld, ScienceWorld and WebShop demonstrate that State-OPD achieves the highest macro-average success rate among the evaluated methods for both Qwen3-1.7B and Qwen3-4B students. Notably, for Qwen3-4B, State-OPD raises macro-average success from 26.07\% with the strongest baseline to 31.74\% and surpasses its Qwen3-32B teacher on ALFWorld Seen. Further analyses reveal that State-OPD achieves higher task success with fewer supervised turn responses and substantially reduces persistent failed-action repetition; qualitative traces illustrate decision revision based on interaction feedback.

</details>


### [168] [Tabular Foundation Models Are Effectively Shallow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13253)
*Irene Cannistraci,Julia E. Vogt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出无标签闭式仿射翻译器，可替换表格基础模型最多96%的连续transformer块；揭示表格transformer严重过参数化，半数深度即可保持性能并超越全深度模型与GBDT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 上下文学习表格基础模型（TFMs）无需额外训练即可求解新任务，适合资源受限或隐私场景，但多transformer块结构带来高计算与内存开销；而传统GBDT在CPU上高效且具竞争力，暗示表格任务可能不需要深层架构。因此追问：能否移除TFMs的架构冗余而不损失性能。

Method: 引入闭式、无标签的仿射翻译器（affine translator），替换某段连续窗口内最多96%的块，其余块保持不变。在171个分类数据集上评估8个前沿TFM，分析不同模型族的过参数化模式（如TabPFN-3依赖编码器；单轴模型分散计算，仅需首块；双轴模型集中于关键块）。

Result: 替换模型一半块至多损失0.002中位AUROC，半深度TabFM仍优于所有全深度TFM和调优GBDT；减少深度可省最多1.5B参数，GPU推理最多加速6倍，中位代价0.03 AUROC；单块TabPFN-3缓存查询快2.4倍、内存少6倍，代价可忽略。

Conclusion: 表格transformer严重过参数化，架构深度存在可移除冗余；所提闭式仿射翻译器能在几乎不损性能下大幅压缩深度、节省参数与内存并加速推理，同时揭示不同模型族冗余分布各异。

Abstract: In-context-learning Tabular Foundation Models (TFMs) are uniquely suited to scenarios with limited resources or privacy constraints, as they solve new tasks without additional training. However, their architecture consists of multiple transformer blocks, resulting in high computational and memory footprints. Meanwhile, traditional alternatives like Gradient-Boosted Decision Trees (GBDTs) remain highly competitive and run efficiently on CPUs, suggesting that tabular tasks may not inherently require deep architectures. This contrast raises a fundamental question: can TFMs be simplified by removing architectural redundancies without sacrificing performance? To answer this, we introduce a closed-form, label-free affine translator that replaces a contiguous window of up to 96% of the blocks, leaving the rest unchanged. Evaluating eight frontier TFMs on 171 classification datasets, we reveal that tabular transformers are vastly overparameterized: replacing half of a model's blocks costs at most $0.002$ median AUROC across all architectures, with the half-depth TabFM still outperforming every other full-depth TFMs and tuned GBDTs. Furthermore, different model families are overparameterized in different ways: TabPFN-3 relies on its encoders, maintaining performance regardless of which single block is retained. Other single-axis models spread computation thinly, needing only their first block, while dual-axis models concentrate it in one critical block. Ultimately, removing depth saves up to 1.5B parameters, speeds up GPU inference by up to $6\times$ at a median cost of $0.03$ AUROC, while a single-block TabPFN-3 serves cached queries $2.4\times$ faster using $6\times$ less memory at negligible cost.

</details>


### [169] [CAST: Credit Alignment for Cross-Tokenizer On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13257)
*Jingru Yang,Jinhang Wu,Minjing Shi,Shengfeng He,Xuyan Li,Man Tan,Biye Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CAST：面向跨分词器在线策略蒸馏的分块信用分配与选择性锚定框架，解决"信度分配"与无对应token约束缺失问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨分词器OPD需先对齐师生序列才能比较得分。解码文本对齐仅提供chunk级对应，但遗留"信度对齐"问题：chunk级信号只说明某片段应变动多少，未说明如何在学生token间分配；且无教师对应的token缺乏约束。

Method: 提出CAST（Chunk-aware credit Assignment with Selective Token Anchoring）"移位或保持"框架：对已对齐chunk，依据预测不确定性与局部教师信号平滑度将chunk优势重分配至各学生token，突出局部一致教师反馈支持的学习机会，同时保持chunk总优势不变，其权重恰为KL正则化信用分配问题的解；对未对齐位置，通过KL惩罚选择性将学生锚定至初始策略，限制无监督区域的漂移。信用分配与chunk构建解耦，故也适用于共享分词下的标点或定长chunk。

Result: 在六个数学、科学与代码基准上，跨分词器迁移Qwen3-8B→Mistral-7B与Llama-3.1-8B分别较最强基线平均提升2.45和1.32分；共享分词迁移至Qwen3-1.7B提升2.10分。

Conclusion: CAST通过将信用分配与chunk构造解耦，同步解决跨分词器OPD中的信度分配与无对应token漂移问题，并具通用性。

Abstract: Cross-tokenizer on-policy distillation (OPD) requires aligning teacher and student sequences before their scores can be compared. Decoded-text alignment provides such correspondence at the chunk level, but leaves a distinct problem unresolved: credit alignment. A chunk-level signal specifies how much a span should change, but not how that signal should be distributed across student tokens, while tokens without teacher correspondence remain unconstrained. We propose CAST (Chunk-aware credit Assignment with Selective Token Anchoring), a shift-or-stay framework that addresses both issues. For aligned chunks, CAST redistributes each chunk's advantage across student tokens using predictive uncertainty and local teacher-signal smoothness, emphasizing learning opportunities supported by locally consistent teacher feedback while preserving the chunk's total advantage. The resulting weights exactly solve a KL-regularized credit-allocation problem. For unaligned positions, CAST selectively anchors the student to its initial policy through a KL penalty, limiting drift where teacher supervision is unavailable. Because credit assignment is separated from chunk construction, CAST also applies to punctuation-defined or fixed-length chunks under shared tokenization. Across six mathematics, science, and code benchmarks, CAST improves the average over the strongest evaluated baseline by $2.45$ and $1.32$ points for cross-tokenizer transfer from Qwen3-8B to Mistral-7B and Llama-3.1-8B, respectively, and by $2.10$ points for shared-tokenizer transfer to Qwen3-1.7B.

</details>


### [170] [Neural Surrogate Modeling of Ultrasonic Wave Propagation in Heterogeneous Media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13258)
*Burla Nur Korkmaz,Petro Moilanen,Ari Salmi,Harsha Vardhan Tetali,Arto Klami*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出融合FNO与局部积分重采样的U-Net神经代理模型，用于高频异构介质中的波传播建模，兼具泛化性与跨尺度能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
