Motivation: 超声传感场景具有高频、传播介质异构的特点，现有神经代理模型计算高效但精度不足：基于FNO的方法难以捕捉反射等关键信号特征，而U-Net类方法虽能较好学习波场，却缺乏跨空间尺度信息传递能力。

Method: 在U-Net架构中集成傅里叶神经算子（FNO）与局部积分重采样，构建新型神经代理模型，结合FNO的全局频域建模与U-Net的多尺度局部特征提取能力。

Result: 该模型可在有限数据上训练，预测结果对输入噪声具有鲁棒性，并能跨不同空间尺度工作。

Conclusion: 融合FNO与U-Net的混合代理模型有效弥补了单一架构的缺陷，为超声传感中高频异构波传播的快速正/逆问题求解提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Neural surrogates provide computationally efficient auto-regressive modeling of wave propagation, enabling e.g. fast inverse solutions. Despite recent progress, the current surrogates are not yet sufficient for the needs of ultrasonic sensing, characterized by high frequency and heterogeneous propagation media. We show how methods building on Fourier Neural Operators (FNO) fail to capture essential signal features, such as reflections, whereas U-Net -based methods learn the wave fields well but lack the ability to transfer information across different spatial scales. We then propose a new surrogate that captures the best of the two worlds, by integrating FNOs and local-integral resampling into a U-Net architecture. We show it can be trained on limited data, and that the predictions are robust to input noise and work across spatial scales.

</details>


### [171] [Data-Adaptive Mahalanobis Metric Learning for Cross-Head Attention in Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13261)
*Zhenke Duan,Xin Li,Jiqun Pan,Dong Xiaofei,Hanwen Ning,Xinyuan Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出数据自适应马氏交叉头注意力（DAMCHA），从度量学习视角将多头注意力（MHA）推广为学习完整的交叉头度量矩阵，并使其随输入动态变化，从而在计算开销相当或更低的情况下提升Transformer的表达能力与性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准MHA仅隐式学习头内静态的欧氏相似度，等价于统一交叉头度量矩阵的对角块，丢弃了头间交互信息；且注意力几何在训练后固定，无法适应数据的内在流形结构。这两点限制了MHA的表达能力。

Method: 提出DAMCHA，包含两项核心创新：(1) 学习完整的交叉头度量矩阵，诱导类马氏相似度，显式建模头间交互，突破MHA的块对角结构限制；(2) 将度量矩阵参数化为依赖输入的函数，使注意力几何动态适应数据流形，而非训练后固定。同时引入原则性正则化，尤其是逐栈（stack-wise）参数共享，以保证计算效率与优化稳定性。DAMCHA可作为MHA及其变体的即插即用替代模块。

Result: DAMCHA可作为即插即用模块嵌入多种Transformer架构，带来更强的表达能力和学习性能，且计算开销相当或更低。大量实验表明，基于DAMCHA的Transformer在一系列基准任务上持续优于竞争基线。

Conclusion: 通过将MHA重新解释为受限的度量学习，DAMCHA以完整且输入自适应的交叉头度量有效克服了MHA的结构性局限，是一种简单有效、通用且高效的注意力机制，代码已开源。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose Data-Adaptive Mahalanobis Cross-Head Attention (DAMCHA), a simple and effective attention mechanism that generalizes Multi-Head Attention (MHA) through the lens of metric learning. Standard MHA implicitly learns a static, head-wise Euclidean similarity corresponding to the diagonal blocks of a unified cross-head metric matrix, discarding all off-diagonal interactions across heads. DAMCHA addresses this limitation via two key innovations. First, it learns the full cross-head metric matrix, inducing a Mahalanobis-like similarity that explicitly captures inter-head interactions beyond the restricted block-diagonal structure of MHA. Second, it parameterizes the metric matrix as an input-dependent function, enabling the attention geometry to dynamically adapt to the intrinsic manifold structure of the data rather than remaining fixed after training. We further introduce principled regularization strategies, most notably stack-wise parameter sharing, to ensure computational efficiency and stable optimization. DAMCHA serves as a drop-in replacement for MHA and its variants in various Transformer architectures, delivering superior expressive power and learning performance with comparable or reduced computational overhead. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DAMCHA-based Transformers consistently outperform competing baselines across a range of benchmark tasks. Code is available at https://github.com/erwinmsmith/DAMCHA.

</details>


### [172] [CopRoute: Dependence-Aware Conformal Routing for Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13276)
*Siva Rajesh Kasa,Santhosh Kumar Kasa,Karan Gupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 CopRoute：基于模型互补性排序的集合式语言模型路由方法，在满足 all-miss 风险约束下显著减少推理调用次数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有风险可控的路由策略通过校准单一 conformal 阈值并应用于嵌套模型集族，仅对 all-miss 风险（所选集合中所有模型均失败的概率）进行控制。但每个模型被独立打分，忽略了模型失败之间的依赖结构：共享训练数据或架构的模型往往在同一批查询上失败，导致两个单独很强的模型可能联合冗余，而一个较弱但互补的模型能覆盖其他模型遗漏的查询。现有策略未能利用这种依赖性。

Method: CopRoute 按每个模型在已选模型之外"新正确回答"的查询数量对模型排序（即依据互补性排序），对每个查询决定沿该排序走到多深，然后选取仍满足目标风险的最小截断点。理论分析证明：在设计数据上，该排序能达到任何高效固定排序算法对"首次正确模型的平均调用次数"所能给出的最优保证；另设独立的校准步骤来认证所部署集合的 all-miss 风险。

Result: 在 RouterBench 与 RouterEval 上，目标风险 0.05 时十个数据集中有九个取得最少认证调用次数，目标风险 0.10 时十个中有八个最少；相比最先进的风险可控策略，在相同认证 all-miss 风险下最多减少 79% 的调用（中位数 24%）。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模模型失败间的依赖结构并采用互补性排序，CopRoute 在保证 all-miss 风险可控的同时，较现有方法大幅降低推理调用开销，兼顾了覆盖率与计算成本。

Abstract: Set-based language model routing aims to increase the coverage of the event where at least one model answers a query correctly while controlling the number of inference calls made. Existing risk-controlled routing policies bound the all-miss risk (the probability that every model in the selected set fails) by calibrating a single conformal threshold and applying it to a nested family of model sets. Because each model is scored in isolation, these policies ignore the dependence structure of model failures. Models that share training data or architecture tend to fail on the same queries, so two individually strong models can be jointly redundant while a weaker but complementary model covers the queries that the others miss. To account for this dependence, which existing risk-controlled policies ignore, we propose CopRoute, which orders models by how many new queries each one answers correctly beyond those already selected, decides per query how far down that order to go, and then picks the smallest cutoff that still meets the target risk. We show that CopRoute's ordering attains, on the design data, the best guarantee any efficient fixed-ordering algorithm can give for the average number of calls to the first correct model, and that a separate calibration step certifies the all-miss risk of the deployed sets. On RouterBench and RouterEval, CopRoute attains the fewest certified calls on nine of ten datasets at a target risk of 0.05 and on eight of ten at 0.10, making up to 79 percent fewer calls (median 24 percent) than the state-of-the-art risk-controlled policy at the same certified all-miss risk.

</details>


### [173] [MHR-Router: Multimodal Heterogeneous Retrieval-Enhanced Adaptive Routing for VLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13278)
*Wenjuan Zhu,Zheming Yang,Yunqing Hu,Qi Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MHR-Router，一种多模态异构检索增强的自适应路由框架，用于为每个图像-问题查询选择最合适的VLM专家。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型（VLMs）能力互补，但由于多模态输入异构且各专家优势不同，为给定图像-问题查询选择最合适的专家仍具挑战。现有路由方法依赖文本线索或静态模型画像，无法捕捉细粒度视觉证据与专家特定的失败模式。

Method: MHR-Router包含两个阶段：(1) 多模态路由表示，将冻结的多模态特征融合为综合查询表示；(2) 异构检索增强自适应路由，检索专家特定的成功与失败记忆作为局部证据，并使用共享打分器自适应选择最优专家。该方法通过局部检索证据将每个多模态查询准确分派到最合适的VLM专家。

Result: 实验表明，MHR-Router有效实现了异构模态路由，并持续优于传统基线，在准确率上取得一致性提升。

Conclusion: MHR-Router通过多模态特征融合与专家成功/失败记忆的检索增强，实现自适应专家选择，验证了检索增强路由在异构多模态VLM推理中的有效性。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) exhibit complementary capabilities, yet selecting the most suitable expert for a given image-question query remains challenging due to heterogeneous multimodal inputs and varying expert strengths. Existing routing approaches rely on text-based cues or static model profiles, failing to capture fine-grained visual evidence and expert-specific failure patterns. To address this, we propose MHR-Router, a multimodal heterogeneous retrieval-enhanced adaptive routing framework for VLM inference. It accurately dispatches each multimodal query to the most suitable VLM expert via local retrieval evidence. MHR-Router consists of two stages: (1) Multimodal Routing Representation, which fuses frozen multimodal features into a comprehensive query representation; and (2) Heterogeneous Retrieval-Enhanced Adaptive Routing, which retrieves expert-specific success and failure memories as local evidence and employs a shared scorer to adaptively select the optimal expert. Experiments show that MHR-Router effectively achieves heterogeneous modality routing and consistently improves accuracy over traditional baselines.

</details>


### [174] [Recovering Irreducible Three-Body Interactions with Lagrangian Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13292)
*Sena Kalabalik,Sukru Caglar,Abdullah Umut Hamzaogullari,Arkadas Ozakin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究用拉格朗日神经网络（LNN）从轨迹数据中学习不可约三体相互作用，以Axilrod-Teller三偶极项为对象，成功恢复其径向标度、角度依赖与符号反转，并通过符号回归还原闭式表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往LNN研究的相互作用均为成对可加形式，尚不清楚LNN能否仅从运动数据中恢复不可约的多体相互作用。Axilrod-Teller三偶极项依赖三粒子的集体几何构型，无法由任何成对项之和表示，是检验该问题的理想对象。

Method: 精确给定动能，仅学习势能（表示为粒子间距离与角度的函数）；采用信号感知采样策略，在保持构型空间覆盖的同时将训练数据集中在三体信号最强的区域；将学习到的三体势嵌入成对Lennard-Jones势中以考察可观测性；最后对训练得到的势进行符号回归。

Result: 仅凭加速度数据即恢复了Axilrod-Teller相互作用，学习到的势再现了径向标度、角度依赖及特征符号反转，并能生成稳定轨迹。可观测性分析表明，当三体项的动力学特征超过残余建模误差时可被恢复：在放大耦合下清晰恢复，而在真实稀有气体耦合下则不能，从而界定了二者之间的转变。符号回归在无预设函数形式下返回了完整保留角度分子、径向律和耦合常数的Axilrod-Teller表达式。

Conclusion: 具备领域特定结构的LNN能够从轨迹中以闭式定律形式恢复不可约三体相互作用，为学习结构未知的相互作用定律提供了方向。

Abstract: Lagrangian Neural Networks (LNNs) learn mechanical systems from trajectory data by parameterizing a scalar Lagrangian and deriving the dynamics through the Euler-Lagrange equations, biasing the model toward motion that respects the variational structure of mechanics. The interactions studied so far have been pairwise-additive, leaving open whether LNNs can recover an irreducible many-body interaction from motion alone. We address this using the Axilrod-Teller triple-dipole interaction, the leading three-body dispersion term, whose dependence on the collective geometry of three particles cannot be represented by any sum of pairwise terms. We prescribe the kinetic energy exactly and learn only the potential, as a function of interparticle distances and angles. Combined with a signal-aware sampling strategy that concentrates training data where the three-body signal is strongest while preserving broad coverage of configuration space, this recovers the interaction from accelerations alone. The learned potential reproduces the radial scaling, angular dependence, and characteristic sign reversal of the Axilrod-Teller interaction, and generates stable trajectories. Embedding it within a pairwise Lennard-Jones potential lets us examine its observability: the three-body term is recovered once its dynamical signature exceeds the residual modeling error, which it does cleanly at amplified coupling but not at the coupling of a real noble gas, bracketing the transition between the two. Finally, symbolic regression on the trained potential, with no assumed functional form, returns the Axilrod-Teller expression with angular numerator, radial law, and coupling constant intact. An LNN with domain-specific structure can thus recover an irreducible three-body interaction from trajectories as a closed-form law, pointing toward learning interaction laws whose structure is not known a priori.

</details>


### [175] [DAG Structure Learning with Node-Varying Heavy-Tailed Disturbances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13304)
*Robin Francis,Sundeep Prabhakar Chepuri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出节点异质 Student-t DAGMA 方法，联合估计图结构、节点尺度与自由度，在重尾异质扰动下提升结构恢复与拟合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于平滑无环约束（如 DAGMA 的对数行列式）的 DAG 学习方法，通常依赖线性结构方程模型并假设各节点扰动为同质高斯分布，难以刻画跨节点变化的尺度与尾部行为。

Method: 提出节点可变 Student-t DAGMA，联合估计图结构、节点特定扰动尺度及自由度参数；利用高斯尺度混合表示导出迭代重加权形式，对大标准化残差降权；并建立图与扰动参数的可识别性。

Result: 合成实验表明在异质重尾扰动下结构恢复更优；真实金融数据实验显示测试负对数似然低于对比基线。

Conclusion: 节点异质重尾建模可有效改进 DAG 结构学习与分布拟合，兼具理论可识别性与实证优势。

Abstract: DAG structures can be learned from observations by incorporating acyclicity via smooth constraints, such as DAGMA's log-determinant function. Typically, observations and the underlying DAG are related through linear structural equation models, with Gaussianity assumption on the disturbances at each node. Extending DAG learning methods that account for scale and tail behavior of disturbances that vary across nodes remains a challenge. We propose a node-varying Student-$t$ DAGMA that jointly estimates the graph structure, node-specific disturbance scales, and the degrees-of-freedom parameter. A Gaussian scale-mixture representation yields an iteratively reweighted formulation that down-weights large standardized residuals. Building on existing results, we also establish identifiability of the graph and disturbance parameters. Synthetic experiments demonstrate improved structural recovery under heterogeneous heavy-tailed disturbances, and experiments on real finance data show that the proposed method yields lower test negative log-likelihood than the evaluated baselines.

</details>


### [176] [MORAD: Reinforcement Learning with Multi-Objective Rewards for RNA Inverse Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13305)
*Jixin Tang,Ji Guo,Jun Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MORAD: 面向骨架条件RNA扩散模型的在线强化学习框架，单策略跨多样骨架训练，联合优化六项指标，在RIDE上显著提升结构精度与配对质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有结构条件RNA逆向设计的强化学习方法要么为每个目标骨架单独优化策略，要么依赖离线偏好优化，缺乏对可复用骨架条件模型的在线后训练探索。

Method: MORAD结合骨架条件组采样、组相对奖励归一化与前向过程扩散目标；将六项测量（三级几何、碱基配对、系综行为、序列组成）映射为有界合意分数，并用加权几何平均聚合，超越仅优化三级结构的奖励。

Result: 相比仅三级结构奖励，多目标公式在11项有偏好方向的指标中改进10项。应用于RIDE：GDT-TS提升16.0%（0.3300→0.3827），配对MCC提升19.4%（0.6110→0.7295），归一化系综缺陷降低27.5%（0.3906→0.2830）。测试集上在三级结构精度、二级配对与热力学质量上优于RiboDiffusion、gRNAde和RDesign。

Conclusion: MORAD建立了结构条件RNA逆向设计的新最优性能，并验证了在共享策略上进行在线后训练的可行性。

Abstract: We introduce MORAD, an online reinforcement learning framework for backbone-conditioned RNA diffusion models that trains a single shared policy across diverse target backbones. Structure-conditioned RNA inverse design seeks nucleotide sequences compatible with a target three-dimensional backbone, yet existing reinforcement learning approaches either optimize a separate policy for each target backbone or rely on offline preference optimization, leaving online post-training of a reusable backbone-conditioned model largely unexplored. MORAD combines backbone-conditioned group sampling, group-relative reward normalization, and a forward-process diffusion objective. Beyond existing RL rewards that primarily target tertiary structural quality, MORAD jointly optimizes six measurements spanning tertiary geometry, base pairing, ensemble behavior, and sequence composition. To aggregate these heterogeneous objectives despite differences in scale and optimization direction, MORAD maps each measurement to a bounded desirability score and combines them with a weighted geometric mean. Compared with a tertiary-structure-only reward, this multi-objective formulation improves 10 of the 11 evaluation metrics that have a preferred direction. Applied to pretrained RIDE, MORAD improves GDT-TS by 16.0% (from 0.3300 to 0.3827) and pairing MCC by 19.4% (from 0.6110 to 0.7295), while reducing normalized ensemble defect by 27.5% (from 0.3906 to 0.2830). On the test set, MORAD outperforms RiboDiffusion, gRNAde, and RDesign across tertiary structural accuracy, secondary-structure pairing, and thermodynamic quality, establishing a new state of the art in structure-conditioned RNA inverse design.

</details>


### [177] [Structure-Aware Numerical Stabilization for Half-Precision Proximal Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13306)
*Linxuan Wu,Liang Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出PPO-FP16，通过三个结构感知保护模块（全局标量保护、策略变化残差保护、持久更新保护）在消费级GPU上实现稳定的半精度PPO训练，在不改变目标、超参和训练预算的前提下提升了效率并降低了显存占用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 资源受限平台上的本地在线训练需要较低的计算与训练状态开销。直接将PPO训练转为FP16不可靠，因为数值误差会通过策略更新和后续数据收集形成反馈，且PPO需反复比较当前策略与固定行为策略。

Method: 提出PPO-FP16，包含三个结构感知模块：1）全局标量保护，稳定跨minibatch或参数更新共享的统计量；2）策略变化残差保护，提取并保留当前策略与行为策略间的小差异；3）高效持久更新保护，稳定优化器状态与参数回写并减少状态存储。

Result: 在6个连续控制任务、5个随机种子上，PPO-FP16取得最低平均回报-AUC排名（1.90，四种方法中），在30个环境-种子比较中17次排名第一。9种性能配置中8种实现1.16至2.03倍的PPO更新加速。相比Full FP32 Adam，优化器状态内存减少71.7%至74.9%，峰值分配GPU内存最多减少44.5%。

Conclusion: 保护少量结构上重要的量即可实现稳定高效的FP16 PPO，且无需重新调参。

Abstract: Local online training on resource-constrained platforms requires low computational and training-state overhead. We study the numerical stability and computational efficiency of half-precision proximal policy optimization (PPO) on commodity GPUs. Directly converting PPO training to FP16 is unreliable because numerical errors feed back through policy updates and subsequent data collection, while PPO repeatedly compares a current policy with a fixed behavior policy. We propose PPO-FP16 with three structure-aware modules. Global scalar protection stabilizes statistics shared across a minibatch or parameter update. Policy-change residual protection extracts and preserves small differences between the current and behavior policies. Efficient persistent-update protection stabilizes optimizer state and parameter writeback while reducing state storage. These changes preserve the PPO objective, hyperparameters, and training budget. Across six continuous-control tasks and five seeds, PPO-FP16 achieves the lowest mean return-AUC rank (1.90) among the four methods and ranks first in 17 of 30 environment-seed comparisons. Eight of nine performance configurations yield PPO update speedups ranging from 1.16 to 2.03 times. Relative to Full FP32 Adam, optimizer-state memory is reduced by 71.7 to 74.9 percent, and peak allocated GPU memory is reduced by up to 44.5 percent. The results show that protecting a small set of structurally important quantities can deliver stable, efficient FP16 PPO without retuning.

</details>


### [178] [SBNO : Schrödinger Bridge Neural Operator for the Forward and Inverse Problems under Missing Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13317)
*Jaehyeon Park,Inhyeok Jeong,Noseong Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SBNO：无方程、成对数据扩散桥匹配框架，在部分观测下学习正/逆PDE问题的条件算子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PDE代理建模通常依赖密集观测或已方程/物理约束，而实际中观测稀疏且部分、物理部分已知或不可用。

Method: 利用扩散Schrödinger桥匹配，从掩码感知的端点耦合中学习一对时间依赖漂移，保留经验输入-解配对，并在单一框架内支持正/逆PDE任务；推理时通过概率流ODE得到确定性点重建，无需推理期观测或PDE引导权重调参。

Result: 在5个PDE基准、5%观测下，SBNO精度优于或可比基线，测试实例误差离散度更低；相比引导扩散基线至少快9.7倍，GPU内存减少至少4.3倍。

Conclusion: SBNO为部分观测下的无方程PDE代理建模提供统一、高效且稳定的正/逆问题求解框架。

Abstract: Partial differential equation (PDE) surrogate modeling often relies on either dense observations or access to governing equations/physics constraints. In practice, observations can be sparse and partial, and the underlying physics may be partially specified or unavailable. We introduce SBNO, an equation-free paired-data diffusion bridge matching framework that learns conditional operators for both forward and inverse PDE problems under partial observations. SBNO uses diffusion Schrödinger bridge matching to learn a pair of time-dependent drifts from a mask-aware endpoint coupling that preserves empirical input-solution pairing during training and supports both PDE-task directions within a single framework. At inference time, SBNO produces a deterministic point reconstruction by integrating the probability-flow ODE constructed from the learned drift pair, without inference-time observation or PDE guidance-weight tuning. On five PDE benchmarks with 5% observations, SBNO achieves superior or comparable accuracy to baselines while demonstrating lower test-instance error dispersion. Furthermore, SBNO is at least $9.7 \times$ faster and reduces GPU memory by at least $4.3 \times$ compared to guided-diffusion baselines.

</details>


### [179] [DuoRate: Two-Rate Per-Expert Precision Allocation for Mixture-of-Experts Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13328)
*Mengqi Guo,Zhe Wei,Yuan Yuan,Jiunn Bin Lim,Boyi Pan,Michael Bi Mi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 DuoRate：将 MoE 权重存储与解码读取解耦，离线存单一前缀可读权重流并烘焙失真张量，在线无标签地按工作负载自身 prefill 求解解码速率-失真分配，单工件兼顾存储率与工作负载读取率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE 服务要付出两次专家精度代价：常驻占用为路由器可达的每个专家付费，而 batch-one 解码每步只为被选中的专家付费。这是同一组 per-expert 宽度上的两个不同速率泛函，但现有 per-expert MoE 分配器只约束前者。作者将两者之差称为 store-read gap（存储-读取差距）：在已发布的 BitsMoE checkpoint 中，2.54 bpw 的存储会转化为 7.50 bpw 的解码流量。目标是在单一工件下同时兼顾两种速率。

Method: DuoRate 采用“一次存储、按负载读取”思路。离线：存储一个统一层级的前缀可读（prefix-readable）权重流，并烘焙失真张量。在线：无需标签地剖析该工作负载自身的 prefill，在解码泛函下求解拉格朗日速率-失真分配；路由频率被证明可从每个专家决策中消去，求解代价为每专家一次点积加一次二分。部署时保留单一工件：prefill 按存储率读取，解码按该工作负载自身速率读取。

Result: 在 Qwen3-30B-A3B 上，DuoRate 以 2.3 bpw 读取超过所有 2-bit 级基线 6.3 至 47.0 个平均准确率点；在 2.9 bpw 时匹配读取率高出 1.4–1.6 bpw 的 4-bit 格式。长解码场景优势最大：GSM8K 上 2.9 bpw 匹配或超过所有 4-bit 格式，仅落后 BF16 0.25 点；2.6 bpw 时仍匹配或超过 4.5-bit 的 NF4 与 FP4。在同格式对照中，仅分配本身即相当于 0.07–0.11 bpw 的读取率。代码将在发表后发布。

Conclusion: MoE 的 per-expert 量化需区分存储与解码两种速率泛函；DuoRate 以单工件实现存储一次、按工作负载读取，在长解码场景下以显著更低的读取率匹配更高比特格式，表明分配策略本身即具可观收益。

Abstract: Serving a Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) model pays for expert precision twice: the resident footprint pays for every expert the router can reach, while each batch-one decode step pays only for the experts it selects. These are two different rate functionals over the same per-expert widths, yet published per-expert MoE allocators constrain only the first. We call the difference the store-read gap; in the released BitsMoE checkpoint, 2.54 bits per weight (bpw) of storage become 7.50 bpw of decode traffic. We propose DuoRate, which stores once and reads per workload. Offline, it stores one uniform tier of a prefix-readable weight stream and bakes a distortion tensor. Online, it profiles a workload's own prefill without labels and solves a Lagrangian rate-distortion allocation under the decode functional; routing frequency provably cancels from every per-expert decision, and the solve costs one dot product per expert and a bisection. A deployment therefore keeps a single artifact that prefill reads at the stored rate and decode reads at the workload's own rate. On Qwen3-30B-A3B, DuoRate reading 2.3 bpw exceeds every 2-bit-class baseline by 6.3 to 47.0 points of average accuracy, and at 2.9 bpw it matches 4-bit formats that read 1.4-1.6 bpw more. The advantage is largest under long decoding: on GSM8K, DuoRate at 2.9 bpw matches or exceeds every 4-bit format and trails BF16 by only 0.25 points, and at 2.6 bpw it still matches or exceeds the 4.5-bit NF4 and FP4. Against controls in its own format, the allocation alone is worth 0.07-0.11 bpw of read rate. Code will be released upon publication.

</details>


### [180] [PDE-PFN: Prior-Data Fitted Neural PDE Solver](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13334)
*Jaehyeon Park,Mingu Kang,Dongseok Lee,Woojin Cho,Kookjin Lee,Anthony Gruber,Youngjoon Hong,Noseong Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PDE-PFN，一种基于先验数据拟合网络（PFN）的神经求解器，通过上下文学习近似PDE解的后验预测均值，可在噪声近似预训练数据下实现跨方程泛化与零梯度更新的上下文推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受大语言模型广泛泛化性及对噪声预训练数据鲁棒性的启发，探索使用近似预训练数据进行PDE解场的上下文预测；并借鉴PFN上下文学习中基于后验预测均值的推理机制。

Method: PDE-PFN建立在带自注意力和交叉注意力机制的Transformer架构的PFN之上，使用由物理信息神经网络（PINN）生成的有噪声近似解作为多样化但非精确的先验进行预训练。

Result: 在一系列二维PDE上的实验表明，PDE-PFN实现了跨异质方程的经验泛化、噪声先验下的鲁棒性以及零梯度更新的上下文推理能力，并在保持灵活上下文与查询表示的同时取得与基线相当或更优的预测精度。

Conclusion: PDE-PFN证明了将PFN式上下文学习应用于PDE求解的可行性，能够在噪声近似先验下实现高效、鲁棒且无需微调的神经PDE求解。

Abstract: Motivated by the success of large language models (LLMs) with broad generalizability and robustness to noisy or unreliable pre-training data, we explore in-context prediction of PDE solution fields using approximate pre-training data. In addition, inspired by the posterior predictive mean-based inference mechanism of the in-context learning in prior-data fitted networks (PFNs), we propose PDE-PFN, a prior-data fitted neural solver that approximates the posterior predictive mean of PDE solutions via in-context learning. PDE-PFN builds on a PFN architecture with self- and cross-attention mechanisms of Transformer and is pre-trained on noisy approximate solutions generated by physics-informed neural networks, serving as diverse but not necessarily exact priors. Through experiments on a range of two-dimensional PDEs, we demonstrate that PDE-PFN achieves empirical generalization across heterogeneous equations, robustness under noisy priors, and zero-gradient-update in-context inference capability. PDE-PFN achieves competitive or improved predictive accuracy relative to the evaluated baselines while supporting flexible context and query representations.

</details>


### [181] [AgentTune: A Unified Training Lifecycle for Tool-Using LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13335)
*R E Zera Marveen Lyngkhoi,Abhivansh Gupta,Vidushee Vats,Ram Mohan Rao Kadiyala,Vinay Kumar Sankarapu,Pratinav Seth*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AgentTune 是一个统一的开源 Python 库，通过两级 EventLog 表示与可组合接口，整合工具型智能体的构建、训练、评测与维护流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具型智能体的开发涉及构建、训练、评测、蒸馏和维护等多个阶段，但现有工具链通常将各阶段割裂处理，缺乏统一的底层支撑，导致表示与流程重复、难以复用。

Method: 核心设计是两级 EventLog 表示，既保留智能体轨迹，又支持训练、评测与审计的一致投影。在此基础上提供共享智能体运行时、工具调用强化学习、智能体蒸馏、检索增强生成、决策工作流、评测工具，以及面向自愈智能体的设计路径，并通过可组合接口将策略、训练方法、评测流程与恢复机制解耦组合。

Result: 在法律合同检索及特定领域任务等智能体场景中验证了框架有效性，展示了其在基于轨迹的优化、工具使用评测与跨任务智能体开发中的应用。

Conclusion: AgentTune 以统一表示和可组合接口打通工具型智能体的全生命周期，减少重复管线，已在 GitHub 以 source-available 形式发布。

Abstract: Tool-using agents increasingly require a common substrate for building, training, evaluating, distilling, and maintaining agentic systems, yet existing tooling typically addresses these stages in isolation. We present AgentTune, a source-available Python library that provides a unified framework for developing and optimizing tool-using agents. At its core, AgentTune introduces a two-tier EventLog representation that preserves agent trajectories while supporting consistent projections for training, evaluation, and auditing. Building on this substrate, the library provides a shared agent runtime, tool-calling reinforcement learning, agentic distillation, retrieval-augmented generation, decision workflows, evaluation utilities, and a designed path toward self-healing agents. These components are exposed through composable interfaces so that agent strategies, training methods, evaluation procedures, and recovery mechanisms can be combined without requiring separate representations or pipelines. We demonstrate the framework across agentic tasks including legal contract search and domain-specific applications, illustrating its use for trajectory-based optimization, tool-use evaluation, and cross-task agent development. AgentTune is released as a source-available library at https://github.com/Lexsi-Labs/AgentTune

</details>


### [182] [Understanding Mamba-3 Through Oscillatory Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13341)
*Shutian Liang,Yue Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将Mamba-3中引入的输入相关旋转重新解释为欠阻尼二阶动力学，通过受控对比实验证明振荡（欠阻尼）机制在状态跟踪、长上下文预测与长度泛化上优于非振荡阻尼，并揭示旋转通过调节信息累积方式支持长上下文处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Mamba-3在循环状态更新中加入了输入相关的旋转，但除状态保持之外，这些旋转的具体作用尚不明确，缺乏对其功能的系统性解释与验证。

Method: 将输入相关旋转解释为欠阻尼二阶动力学，构建分析振荡在序列建模中作用的理论框架；开展受控对比实验（欠阻尼 vs. 非振荡阻尼），涵盖状态跟踪任务与语言模型预训练；对预训练模型进行干预实验以考察学习到的相位作用，并进行状态分析。

Result: 欠阻尼动力学在状态跟踪任务上优于非振荡阻尼机制；在语言模型预训练中，对长上下文预测与长度泛化带来收益；干预实验证实学习到的相位具有重要性；状态分析表明旋转调节了保留信息与新增更新的结合方式。

Conclusion: 欠阻尼动力学是循环计算中一种有用的机制，振荡通过调节信息累积方式来支持长上下文处理。

Abstract: Mamba-3 introduces input-dependent rotations into recurrent state updates, but their contribution beyond state retention remains unclear. We interpret these ro- tations as underdamped second-order dynamics, providing a framework for in- vestigating the role of oscillation in sequence modeling. Controlled comparisons show that underdamped dynamics outperform non-oscillatory damping regimes on state-tracking tasks, while language-model pretraining demonstrates benefits for long-context prediction and length generalization. Interventions in pretrained models further establish the importance of learned phase, and state analysis sug- gests that rotation regulates how retained information combines with incoming up- dates. Taken together, these findings identify underdamped dynamics as a useful mechanism for recurrent computation and suggest that oscillation supports long- context processing by regulating how information accumulates.

</details>


### [183] [Hidden Data Challenges in Clinical Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13350)
*Carolin Cissée,Dennis Fast,Raneen Younis,Alexander Löser,Wolfgang Nejdl,Zahra Ahmadi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文以MIMIC-IV为例，系统审计电子健康记录预处理中的三类对齐问题（时序、语义、人群），揭示其如何造成时序泄漏、特征-任务错位与有偏插补，并给出可复现的预处理建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广泛复用的EHR预处理流程隐含了关于信息可得时间、标签对应临床事件、缺失值含义的假设，这些假设很少被系统审计，并会随流程复用或改造在后续研究中传播，从而影响模型可用信息与预测解释。

Method: 以MIMIC-IV为案例，识别并量化三类预处理对齐违规：时序对齐（时序泄漏）、语义对齐（特征-任务错位）、人群对齐（有偏插补）。分析时间戳语义、事件定义、终点构建、模态选择与缺失处理中的歧义，并结合数据分析和实证案例研究验证其影响，最后提出实用建议。

Result: 这些预处理选择在总体预测性能上往往不可见，但会改变模型所依赖的信号；时间戳语义、事件定义、终点构建、模态选择与缺失值处理中的歧义会改变模型可用信息并影响预测解释。

Conclusion: 需要开展时序有效、临床对齐且可复现的MIMIC-IV及相关EHR数据预处理，并提出相应实践建议；实验代码与所提预处理流程已开源。

Abstract: Widely reused preprocessing pipelines for electronic health records can encode assumptions about when information became available, what clinical episode a label represents, and what missingness means. These assumptions are rarely audited systematically and may propagate across subsequent studies when preprocessing pipelines are reused or adapted. Using MIMIC-IV as a case study, we identify and quantify violations of three complementary forms of preprocessing alignment, i.e., temporal, semantic, and population alignment, which manifest as temporal leakage, feature-task misalignment, and biased imputation. We show how ambiguities in timestamp semantics, episode definitions, endpoint construction, modality selection, and missingness handling can alter the information available to a model and affect the interpretation of its predictions. Through analyses of MIMIC-IV data and empirical case studies, we demonstrate that such choices may remain largely invisible in aggregate predictive performance while changing the signals on which models rely. We derive practical recommendations for temporally valid, clinically aligned, and reproducible preprocessing of MIMIC-IV and related electronic health record datasets. The code for our experiments, including our proposed preprocessing, is available at: https://github.com/m2l-lab/mimic-iv-preprocess.

</details>


### [184] [Opening the Black Box of Classifier-Free Guidance via Information Bottleneck](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13369)
*Jiayang Gao,Tianyi Zheng,Jiayang Zou,Fengxiang Yang,Luyao Fan,Lv Tang,Bo Li,Jia Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将扩散采样轨迹建模为时间相关的信息瓶颈，证明条件信息的有效性在采样中并不均匀，而是在满足Fisher信息平衡条件的临界阶段后显著增强；据此提出免训练的动态CFG策略，通过条件与无条件分数统计构建随机过程，在估计的引导增益超过阈值时激活引导，可自适应对齐生成动力学，并将区间式引导作为极限特例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Classifier-free guidance（CFG）是提升条件扩散生成质量的标准技术，但其激活时间表多依赖启发式设定：现有方法普遍采用预先定义的固定激活策略，近期动态变体也多为手工设计调度，缺乏关于"条件信息何时最有用"的原则性理论解释。因此作者希望从信息论视角理解条件输入与分数函数之间信息相关性的演化规律，回答引导应在何时激活的问题。

Method: 将扩散轨迹形式化为时间相关的信息瓶颈，分析条件信息有效性的时变特性，并给出以Fisher信息平衡条件刻画的临界阶段。基于此提出免训练的动态CFG推理策略：不再预设固定调度，而是由条件与无条件分数统计构造随机过程，当估计的引导增益（guidance gain）超过给定阈值时激活引导，形成自适应激活规则；区间式引导可作为该规则的极限特例被回收。

Result: 在多种网络架构与任务上的大量实验表明，该方法有效、稳健且适用性广；同时为动态引导方法提供了理论解释，并为条件扩散模型的推理时控制提供了原则性基础。

Conclusion: 条件信息在采样过程中的作用并非均匀分布，而是在临界阶段后显著增强；基于信息瓶颈与Fisher信息平衡条件的分析可自然导出免训练、自适应的引导激活规则，兼具经验收益与理论解释力。

Abstract: Classifier-free guidance (CFG) is a standard technique for improving conditional generation in diffusion models, yet its activation schedule is usually selected heuristically. In practice, most existing CFG-based methods rely on a pre-defined activation policy throughout inference, while recent dynamic variants often depend on hand-designed schedules without a principled theoretical explanation of when conditional information is most useful. In order to understand how the informational relevance between conditioning inputs and the score function evolves over the course of generation, we explore when guidance should be activated from an information-theoretic perspective. In this work, we formulate the diffusion trajectory as a time-dependent information bottleneck and show that the effectiveness of conditional information is not uniform throughout sampling, but becomes significantly stronger after a critical stage characterized by a Fisher-information balance condition. Motivated by this insight, we propose a training-free dynamic CFG strategy for inference. Rather than prescribing a fixed schedule in advance, our method constructs a stochastic process from conditional and unconditional score statistics and activates guidance when the estimated guidance gain crosses a prescribed threshold. This yields an adaptive activation rule that aligns condition injection with the underlying generation dynamics, while also recovering interval-based guidance as a limiting special case. Extensive experiments across diverse architectures and tasks, demonstrate that our method is effective, robust, and broadly applicable. Beyond empirical improvements, our framework provides an interpretation of dynamic guidance methods and a principled foundation for inference-time control in conditional diffusion models.

</details>


### [185] [BLOKS: Scaling Kernel Solvers with Sketched Block Preconditioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13382)
*Giordano Vitale,Giacomo Meanti,Marco Rando,Blanca Ayuso de Dios,Lorenzo Rosasco*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BLOKS：一种基于共轭梯度的Nyström草图KRR求解器，采用分块Jacobi与草图结合的预条件器，内存开销与数据集规模和参数数量无关，支持大模型扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确KRR的时间和内存开销使其难以用于大规模数据集，催生了近似求解器。这些求解器依赖迭代方法、草图/分批和预条件。预条件虽对快速收敛至关重要，但其内存成本通常需通过降低模型自由参数来控制，可能牺牲表达能力和精度。因此需要一种既能保持预条件加速、又能控制内存的求解方案。

Method: 提出BLOKS，一种面向Nyström草图KRR的共轭梯度求解器。其核心是新型预条件器，将分块Jacobi近似与草图技术相结合，使预条件器的内存成本独立于数据集规模和参数数量，从而支持大模型。并提供了内存高效、GPU加速的实现。

Result: BLOKS在时间/内存复杂度上保持优势，同时达到优异精度。在用于基准测试核求解器的标准大规模数据集上，其表现与最先进方法相当或更优。

Conclusion: BLOKS通过分块Jacobi与草图结合的预条件器，在保持快速收敛的同时实现内存开销与数据规模和参数量解耦，为大规模核回归提供可扩展且高精度的求解方案。

Abstract: Kernel Ridge Regression (KRR) is a theoretically sound algorithm for nonparametric learning, which is also often used to investigate properties of deep learning. However, the exact KRR formulation incurs time and memory costs preventing applicability to large datasets, motivating the development of approximate solvers. These algorithms rely on (i) iterative methods, to avoid the computational burden of direct solutions; (ii) sketching or batching, to reduce the problem size; and (iii) preconditioning, to accelerate convergence. While crucial for fast convergence, preconditioning incurs memory costs that can be managed by lowering the number of free parameters of the model, potentially at the expense of expressivity and hence accuracy. To address this challenge, we propose BLOKS, a Conjugate Gradient-based solver for Nyström-sketched KRR built around a novel preconditioner that combines a block Jacobi approximation with sketching. The BLOKS preconditioner has memory cost independent of both the dataset size and the number of parameters, allowing scaling to large models. We provide a memory-efficient, GPU-accelerated implementation and show that BLOKS achieves excellent accuracy while retaining favorable time/memory complexity. Indeed, on standard large-scale datasets used to benchmark kernel solvers, BLOKS achieves comparable or better performances than state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [186] [PlacePulse: Learning Transferable Place Representations from Structural Geographic Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13390)
*Lucía García-Duarte,Miguel Álvarez,Diego Vicente,Javier Pérez Trufero,Javier de la Torre*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出 PlacePulse——一个刻画场所静态结构构成的 geospatial 基础模型，将 572 个属性（经 H3 邻域聚合扩展为 2,288 维输入）压缩为 1,055,537 个美国 H3 分辨率-7 网格的 256 维单位范数嵌入，并配套提出双轴可复用基准，从内在表示质量与外在预测效用两方面进行评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地理空间机器学习常依赖针对特定任务、基于大量社会人口、商业和环境变量的特征工程。现有地理空间基础模型多编码动态信号（移动性、搜索活动）或卫星影像视觉信号，较少将场所的静态结构构成（居民与就业者、经营企业、面临风险）表示为可迁移、细空间分辨率的嵌入。论文动机在于确立结构构成作为一种独立且互补的地理空间基础模型模态，而非行为或影像数据缺失时的替代方案。

Method: 方法上构建 PlacePulse：输入 572 个属性，通过 H3 邻域聚合扩展为 2,288 维输入；对美国 1,055,537 个 H3 分辨率-7 网格压缩为 256 维单位范数嵌入。同时提出可复用的双轴基准，将内在表示质量与多目标、多空间支撑下的下游预测效用分开评估。

Result: 内在表现：嵌入可重构标准化输入，平均 R² = 0.853；256 维中约 251 维近似去相关维度被使用；空间组织性强，第一主成分 Moran's I = 0.93，且自相关延续至输入半径之外。外在表现：在来自 12 个外部来源、4 种空间支撑的 74 个保留目标上，PlacePulse 在训练输入无关目标上优于维度匹配的特征选择基线，并与 Google 的 Population Dynamics Foundation Model 具有竞争力，在健康、经济、选举、经济流动性及社会脆弱性结果上领先，相对优势随领域变化。

Conclusion: 这些发现表明，结构构成是地理空间基础模型中一种独立且互补的模态，并非仅在行为或影像数据不可用时才采用的退路。

Abstract: Geospatial machine learning often depends on task-specific feature engineering over large collections of sociodemographic, business, and environmental variables. Geospatial foundation models offer reusable representations of place, but leading approaches typically encode dynamic signals, such as mobility and search activity, or visual signals from satellite imagery. Few models represent the static structural composition of places (who lives and works there, which businesses operate there, and which risks they face) as transferable embeddings at fine spatial resolution. We introduce PlacePulse, a structural geospatial foundation model that compresses 572 attributes, expanded to 2,288 inputs through H3 neighborhood aggregates, into 256-dimensional unit-norm embeddings for 1,055,537 H3 resolution-7 cells in the United States. We also introduce a reusable two-axis benchmark that separates intrinsic representation quality from downstream predictive utility across multiple targets and spatial supports. Intrinsically, the embeddings reconstruct standardized inputs with mean $R^2 = 0.853$, use 251 of 256 approximately decorrelated dimensions, and show strong spatial organization, with Moran's $I = 0.93$ on the leading principal component and autocorrelation persisting beyond the input radius. Extrinsically, across 74 held-out targets from 12 external sources and four spatial supports, PlacePulse outperforms a dimensionality-matched feature-selection baseline on targets unrelated to the training inputs and is competitive with Google's Population Dynamics Foundation Model, leading on health, economic, election, economic-mobility and social-vulnerability outcomes, with relative strengths that vary by domain. These findings position structural composition as a distinct and complementary modality for geospatial foundation models, not simply a fallback where behavioral or imagery data are unavailable.

</details>


### [187] [Why Linear Models Can Still Work in a Complex and Changing World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13404)
*Fangzhou Lin,Dengzhe Hou,Yinxing Li,Kazunori D Yamada*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 线性模型在复杂领域预测表现良好，原因在于世界复杂度超过有限、部分观测数据能可靠学习的复杂度；当不稳定性主要来自非线性成分时，省略该成分反而可在新环境中降低预测误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者试图解释一个反直觉现象：在机制本质为非线性、且复杂的现实领域（如医疗、政策）中，线性模型常能取得良好甚至优于灵活模型的预测表现。其动机是厘清这种"简单模型取胜"的适用条件，并将该现象与线性规则、偏差-方差、正则化、测量误差、分布偏移、不变性、Rashomon集与欠定等既有理论联系起来。

Method: 论文采用理论论证与命题证明的方式。核心论点是：灵活模型会利用曲率、阈值和交互作用去拟合某一医院、时期、人群、政策环境或测量过程，但这些结构往往无法迁移；当不稳定性主要集中于非线性成分时，省略它可使线性模型在新环境中获得更低预测误差。命题1给出了一个平方误差情形，说明在训练环境中学到的非线性规则因迁移过弱而提高目标环境误差；有限样本估计误差会强化该结论。作者同时指出仅部分观测并不充分，观测变量与未观测因素之间的联系必须随环境变化。

Result: 命题1表明，在平方误差设定下，训练环境中学习到的非线性规则会因迁移过弱而增大目标误差，且有限样本估计误差会进一步放大这一效应。此外，只有当观测变量与未观测因素之间的链接随环境变化时，部分观测才会导致灵活模型失效；稳定的非线性结构则有利于灵活模型，而线性模型仍易受线性虚假关系影响。

Conclusion: 模型复杂度的选择不应仅依据训练数据精度，还应以跨环境稳定性为标准。简单模型在复杂领域中的优势源于世界复杂度超出有限部分观测数据所能可靠学习的范围，而非非线性机制本身不存在。

Abstract: Linear models can predict well in complex domains even when underlying mechanisms are nonlinear. We argue that this can occur because the complexity of the world exceeds the complexity that can be learned reliably from finite, partially observed data. Flexible models can exploit curvature, thresholds, and interactions that fit one hospital, period, population, policy setting, or measurement process but fail elsewhere. When this instability lies mainly in the nonlinear component, omitting it can give linear models lower prediction error in a new environment. We connect this claim to work on simple linear rules, bias and variance, regularization, best linear prediction, measurement error, distribution shift, invariance, Rashomon sets, and underspecification. Proposition 1 gives a simple squared-error case in which a nonlinear rule learned in the training environment raises target error because it transfers too weakly; finite-sample estimation error can strengthen this result. Partial observation alone is not enough: the link between observed variables and unobserved factors must vary across environments. Stable nonlinear structure favors flexible models, and linear models remain vulnerable to linear spurious relationships. Model complexity should therefore be chosen by cross-environment stability as well as training-data accuracy.

</details>


### [188] [Accelerating Linear Bandits with Persistent Candidate Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13407)
*Bingkui Tong,Daria Korovaitceva,Marina Sheshukova,Sergey Samsonov,Junpei Komiyama*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Persistent Candidate LinUCB (PC-LinUCB)，通过维护 k 个活跃动作并每轮从剩余 K-k 个动作中均匀采样 q 个挑战者，将每轮评分次数从 O(K) 降至 O(k+q)，并给出与之匹配的遗憾上下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在动作集固定且规模 K 很大的线性 bandit 中，穷举式乐观评分每轮需 O(K) 次评分，动作选择成本过高，需要降低每轮计算量。

Method: PC-LinUCB 维护 k 个活跃动作，每轮从其余 K-k 个动作中均匀采样 q 个挑战者，仅对这 k+q 个候选进行评分；随后建立问题无关与问题相关的高概率遗憾界。

Result: 遗憾界由标准线性 bandit 学习项与加性发现成本 O((K-k)log(K-k)/q) 组成，且该成本不随视野增长；在固定间隙与足够长视野下，针对均匀挑战者采样给出 Ω((K-k)/q) 的遗憾下界，表明部分候选访问本身存在不可避免的遗憾代价。

Conclusion: 实验表明 PC-LinUCB 在保持可比遗憾的同时显著加速 LinUCB，且持久候选机制可推广到 LinUCB 之外的方法。

Abstract: Exhaustive optimistic scoring can make action selection costly in linear bandits with a large fixed set of $K$ actions, requiring $\mathcal{O}(K)$ score evaluations per round. We introduce Persistent Candidate LinUCB (\textsc{PC-LinUCB}), which maintains $k$ active actions and samples $q$ challengers uniformly from the remaining $K-k$ actions at each round. By scoring only these $k+q$ candidates, \textsc{PC-LinUCB} reduces the number of score evaluations per round to $\mathcal{O}(k+q)$. We establish problem-independent and problem-dependent high-probability regret bounds consisting of a standard linear-bandit learning term and an additive discovery cost of order $\mathcal{O}\left((K-k)\log(K-k)/q\right)$ at fixed confidence. Notably, this additional cost does not grow with the horizon. For fixed gaps and sufficiently long horizons, we establish an $Ω\left((K-k)/q\right)$ regret lower bound under uniform challenger sampling, showing that partial candidate access itself imposes an unavoidable regret cost. Experiments show that \textsc{PC-LinUCB} substantially accelerates LinUCB while maintaining comparable regret, and that the persistent candidate mechanism can extend beyond LinUCB.

</details>


### [189] [In-Context Learning for System Identification: Probabilistic Formulation, Long-Sequence Handling, and Benchmark Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13411)
*Matteo Rufolo,Dario Piga,Filippo Pura,Marco Forgione*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文推进了系统辨识的元学习框架：提出统一的概率元辨识架构（Transformer编码器-解码器+长序列处理机制），实现长时域校准预测；展示预训练元模型可作为不确定性感知的合成数据生成器，为数据稀缺下的参数化模型提供正则化；在非线性系统辨识基准与分布外场景中验证了竞争性精度与强泛化能力，仅输入适配即可应对显著分布偏移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统系统辨识需两步（先估计模型再预测），而近期上下文学习方法通过离线大规模合成数据训练元模型，实现从训练数据与查询输入到输出的零样本直接映射。本文旨在从三个方向推进该元学习系统辨识框架。

Method: 1) 设计统一的概率元辨识架构，结合基于Transformer的编码器-解码器与针对长训练序列的定制机制，以支持长时域校准预测；2) 利用预训练元模型作为不确定性感知的合成数据生成器，为真实数据稀缺的经典系统辨识提供任务特定数据，正则化参数化模型。

Result: 在标准非线性系统辨识基准及具有挑战性的分布外场景上进行了系统评估，精度与最先进方法具有竞争力，并展现出强泛化能力，包括仅靠输入适配即可处理显著分布偏移的情形。

Conclusion: 该工作从架构统一、数据生成与泛化评估三方面推进了元学习系统辨识框架，验证了其在长时域校准预测、小样本正则化及分布外泛化方面的有效性。

Abstract: Traditional system identification typically involves two steps: initially, training data is processed to estimate a model of the system of interest; then, the model is used to predict outputs for new input queries. In contrast, recent in-context learning approaches learn a meta model that maps directly from training data and query inputs to outputs in a zero-shot fashion, bypassing explicit model estimation. This approach leverages a meta model trained offline, on large sets of synthetically generated data, to emulate the behavior of a system identification algorithm. In this work, we advance this meta-learning framework for system identification in three directions. First, we develop a unified probabilistic meta-identification architecture that combines a Transformer-based encoder-decoder with tailored mechanisms for handling long training sequences, enabling calibrated predictions over long horizons. Second, we show how a pre-trained meta model can act as an uncertainty-aware synthetic data generator for classical system identification, providing task-specific data that regularize parametric models when real data are scarce. Third, we systematically evaluate the framework on standard nonlinear system identification benchmarks and on a challenging out-of-distribution scenario, demonstrating competitive accuracy with state-of-the-art methods and strong generalization, including cases where input-only adaptation is sufficient to handle significant distribution shifts.

</details>


### [190] [PPG-Bench: A Unified Benchmark for PPG Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13435)
*Arvasu Kulkarni,Mahir Jain,Aditya Ray Mishra,Sandeep Singh,Siddharth Panwar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 PPG-Bench，一个统一的开源基准，用于在 9 个数据集、26 个任务和 4 种下游适配范式下系统比较 5 个公开 PPG 基础模型，揭示现有 PPG 基础模型在多数任务上并无显著优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PPG 数据在可穿戴与临床设备上大规模采集，PPG 基础模型（FM）不断涌现，但其评估缺乏统一标准：数据集、任务定义、预处理、下游头、超参数调优与基线各不相同，导致模型之间难以公平比较，也无法判断这些模型是否真正学到了通用可迁移的 PPG 表征。

Method: 构建 PPG-Bench 开源基准：评估 5 个公开 PPG 基础模型，覆盖 9 个开放数据集和 26 个任务（心血管状态估计、活动、睡眠、筛查、身份识别）；采用 4 种递增适配性的下游范式（线性探测、随机森林、冻结 MLP 探针、端到端微调），进行模型特定超参数优化与受试者不重叠的交叉验证；设置三类对照基线：随机/常数基线、确定性脉搏率变异性（PRV）特征集、通用时序基础模型 MOMENT。

Result: 在全部 68 个任务-范式组合中，35.3% 未显示出基础模型相对随机/常数基线的有意义优势；83.8% 的组合中没有任何 PPG 专用基础模型能同时优于 PRV 特征集和 MOMENT。此外，增加下游头容量通常能提升性能，但当模型处于随机/常数水平时可能导致显著性能退化。

Conclusion: 当前 PPG 基础模型尚不能声称提供了对 PPG 信号的通用、可迁移表征；该领域未来的评估应纳入与简单、非学习型对照基线的比较。基准代码与扩展评估说明已开源于 https://github.com/mannas-ai/ppg-bench。

Abstract: Photoplethysmography (PPG) is increasingly recorded by wearable and clinical devices at enormous scale, and a growing number of PPG foundation models (FMs) have been developed to compute reusable representations for various downstream tasks. These models are difficult to compare: different evaluations use different datasets, task definitions, preprocessing, downstream heads, hyperparameter tuning, and baselines. We present PPG-Bench, a unifying open-source benchmark evaluating five public PPG FMs across nine open-access datasets and 26 tasks spanning cardiovascular state estimation, activity, sleep, screening, and identification. We evaluate models under four downstream regimes of increasing adaptability (linear probing, random forests, frozen MLP probes, and end-to-end finetuning), with model-specific hyperparameter optimization and subject-disjoint cross-validation. We also evaluate against three controls: random/constant baseline, a deterministic pulse-rate-variability (PRV) feature set, and a generic time-series FM (MOMENT). Across all 68 task-regime combinations, 35.3% show no meaningful FM advantage over the random/constant baseline, and in 83.8% no PPG-specific FM outperforms both PRV and MOMENT. We also find that added downstream head capacity generally improves performance, but can cause significant degradation if the model is performing at chance/constant level. Current PPG FMs therefore cannot yet claim to provide generalised and transferrable representations of PPG signals, and future evaluation in this field should include checks against simple, non-learned controls. Code for the benchmark and instructions to evaluate more models can be found here - https://github.com/mannas-ai/ppg-bench.

</details>


### [191] [FedPCD: Priority-Constrained Federated Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13508)
*Yousef Irshaid,Dara Varam,Mohamed I. AlHajri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 FedPCD 优先级约束联邦优化框架，将全局优化目标与客户端保护分离，在保持传统联邦聚合为主目标的同时，仅做满足客户端性能约束所需的最小修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习虽能优化共享全局模型，但在统计异构数据下会造成客户端间性能不公平。现有公平性或多目标联邦方法通过客户端重加权、梯度修正或共同下降等方式处理，但往往会改变全局优化方向，或对称地对待各客户端目标。

Method: FedPCD 将全局优化目标与客户端保护解耦：以传统联邦聚合作为主目标，仅在满足客户端性能约束所必需时施加最小幅度的修正，从而在异构数据下兼顾全局性能与客户端公平性。

Result: 在异构联邦学习基准上，FedPCD 持续优于现有最佳基线，在所有配置下取得最高全局准确率，并在几乎所有设置下取得最佳 worst-10% 客户端准确率；同时保持有竞争力的全局准确率与客户端性能变异性的权衡，并展现出更快的经验收敛速度，以显著更少的通信轮数达到相当或更优性能。

Conclusion: FedPCD 通过优先级约束的联邦优化，在不偏离传统全局目标的前提下有效缓解客户端性能不公平，并兼顾收敛效率，为公平感知联邦学习提供了一种最小干预式方案。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) seeks to optimize a shared global model from decentralized client data. While effective for average performance, this process can produce unfair performance across clients, particularly under statistically heterogeneous data distributions. Existing fairness-aware and multi-objective FL methods address this through client re-weighting, gradient correction, or common-descent formulations, but often change the global optimization direction or treat client objectives symmetrically. We propose FedPCD, a priority-constrained federated optimization framework that separates the global optimization objective from client protection. FedPCD preserves the conventional federated aggregate as the primary objective, while allowing only the minimum correction necessary to meet client performance constraints. Across heterogeneous FL benchmarks, FedPCD consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, achieving the highest global accuracy across all configurations and leading worst-10% client accuracy in nearly all. FedPCD further preserves competitive trade-offs between global accuracy and client performance variability. Beyond this trade-off, FedPCD demonstrates faster empirical convergence, reaching matching or superior performance in substantially fewer communication rounds.

</details>


### [192] [Simulating Training Spikes of Deep Neural Networks by Layer-wise Random Feature Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13524)
*Ran Tian,Courtney Paquette*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文用带重尾幂律输入的二次模型复现LLM预训练中的loss尖峰，并提出逐层训练动力学建模框架与模拟算法，可预测Adam中β₂接近1时的尖峰行为，并据此导出改善性能的β₂调度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM预训练中loss出现间歇性"尖峰"（spikes）现象，其理论机制与经验规律尚不清楚，缺乏可解释的建模工具。

Method: 引入含重尾、幂律分布输入的简单二次模型来模拟DNN的逐层训练动力学，并提出相应的理论框架与模拟算法；以已知会诱发Transformer LM尖峰的Adam超参配置（β₂接近1）为案例进行验证。

Result: 模拟能从给定checkpoint成功预测出指示尖峰行为的bump；同时从观测指标中推导出合适的β₂调度策略，经验上提升了性能。

Conclusion: 重尾输入下的二次模型足以复现LLM预训练loss尖峰，该逐层动力学框架既具预测能力，又能指导超参（β₂）调度以改善训练表现。

Abstract: Intermittent ``spikes'' in loss, during the pre-training of Large Language Models (LLMs), remains a mysterious phenomenon both theoretically and empirically. In this paper, we show that simple quadratic models with heavy-tailed, power-law distributed inputs can replicate these spikes. We introduce a theoretical framework and simulation algorithm to model per-layer training dynamics of Deep Neural Networks (DNNs), using such quadratic models. In a case study on an Adam hyperparameter configuration (where $β_2$ is set close to $1$) known to induce spikes in Transformer LMs, our simulation successfully predicts bumps indicating spiky behavior from a given checkpoint. Furthermore, we derive an appropriate $β_2$ schedule from observed metrics, which empirically improves performance.

</details>


### [193] [Probabilistic Ship Trajectory Forecasting using Transformer Models on AIS Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13557)
*Frederik E. T. Schöller*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用AIS数据研究Transformer与Performer模型在受限水域船舶轨迹预测中的应用，并通过KDE生成局部位置热力图、经CNN编码后输入模型，结合分位数损失获得预测区间，性能显著优于以往工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海上航行中（无论人工驾驶还是自主水面舰艇）都需要对船舶轨迹进行预判，以便碰撞避免等子程序及时评估态势并做出反应。因此，准确的轨迹预测及可靠的预测区间对及时避碰至关重要。

Method: 使用广泛可得的AIS位置数据，在受限水域训练并评估Transformer与Performer轨迹预测模型。通过核密度估计（KDE）生成位置热力图，以表征各智能体周围局部区域内其他船舶的历史运动信息，并用卷积神经网络（CNN）编码后输入Transformer模型；采用分位数损失（quantile loss）训练，以获得模型感知的预测区间。

Result: 所提出的模型架构大幅优于以往工作；引入局部热力图后，能够预测仅凭历史位置无法体现的船舶运动。

Conclusion: Transformer/Performer结合局部KDE热力图与分位数损失，可有效提升受限水域船舶轨迹预测性能，并提供对及时避碰有价值的预测区间。

Abstract: Anticipation of vessel trajectories is an essential task when navigating at sea. This applies to both human navigators and autonomous surface vessels. Trajectory forecasting enables sub-routines such as collision avoidance to assess the situation and react accordingly in time. This paper investigates Transformer and Performer models for trajectory prediction of surface vessels in confined waters. Using widely available position data from the Automatic Identification System (AIS), candidate models are trained and evaluated. Historical information on how other vessels have moved in the local area surrounding each agent is conveyed using positional heat maps. The heat maps are generated using Kernel Density Estimation (KDE) and encoded with a Convolutional Neural Network (CNN) before entering the Transformer models. The models are trained with quantile loss to efficiently obtain a model-aware prediction interval, as this is crucial for timely collision avoidance. The proposed model architectures are shown to greatly outperform previous work while the inclusion of local heat maps enables the prediction of vessel movement that is not otherwise indicated from the previous positions.

</details>


### [194] [Rationally Linearized MLPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13568)
*Arijit Sehanobish,Byeongchan Kim,Avinava Dubey,Min-hwan Oh,Krzysztof Choromanski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RaLiMPs方法，利用低阶有理逼近激活函数实现MLP高效线性化，在保持或提升精度的同时显著降低推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多项式方法对MLP进行线性化时，通常需要较高阶才能获得准确表示，效率受限。因此希望借助低阶有理逼近来更精确地刻画标准激活函数，从而在更低阶数下实现有效线性化。

Method: RaLiMPs使用标准激活函数的低阶有理逼近替代多项式逼近，并基于该逼近构建带有窄瓶颈的核表示，从而实现MLP各层之间的高效复合，同时给出相应的逼近理论保证。

Result: 在从隐式3D模型到大型语言模型等多种应用中评估，RaLiMPs展现出良好的精度—效率权衡，达到有竞争力甚至更优的精度，同时大幅降低推理开销。

Conclusion: 有理逼近为MLP线性化提供了一种比多项式更高效的途径，RaLiMPs在多种任务中验证了其精度与效率优势。

Abstract: We present Rationally Linearized Multilayer Perceptrons(RaLiMPs), a new approach to efficiently linearizing MLPs using low-degree rational approximations of standard activation functions. Inspired by polynomial-based MLP linearization, we show that rational approximations can provide accurate representations at substantially lower degree. RaLiMPs exploit these approximations to construct kernel representations with narrow bottlenecks, enabling efficient composition across MLP layers. We provide theoretical guarantees for the resulting approximation and evaluate RaLiMPs across applications ranging from implicit 3D models to large language models. Our experiments demonstrate favorable accuracy--efficiency tradeoffs, with RaLiMPs achieving competitive or improved accuracy while substantially reducing inference cost.

</details>


### [195] [Simultaneous Competitive and Regret Guarantees for TCP Acknowledgment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13571)
*Tsubasa Harada,Yasushi Kawase,Hanna Sumita*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究TCP确认（ACK）问题中同时实现竞争比与遗憾保证的随机在线算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TCP确认问题中，单个确认可服务所有待确认分组，目标是最小化确认开销与等待开销之和。已知确定性在线算法竞争比下界为2，因此需要借助随机化与专家学习来同时获得近最优竞争比与低遗憾。

Method: 给定k个随机在线专家与0<ε<1，构造随机在线算法：通过控制专家间切换开销，并利用允许的竞争上界与当前成本之间的差距来决定何时学习；算法无需预先知道分组数量或OPT。

Result: 算法的实际总成本始终不超过(2+ε)·OPT，且相对实际成本最小专家的期望遗憾为O(√(OPT·log(k+1))+log(1/ε))。同时证明当k≥2时该遗憾界在OPT、k与ε上渐近最优，并证明ε>0对次线性遗憾是必要的。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了TCP确认问题中同时兼顾竞争性与遗憾性的随机在线算法，并配以匹配的下界，表明其保证在渐近意义下最优。

Abstract: We study simultaneous competitive and regret guarantees for TCP acknowledgment. Each acknowledgment serves all pending packets, and the objective is to minimize the total acknowledgment and waiting costs. It is well known that no deterministic online algorithm achieves a competitive ratio better than $2$. Given $k$ randomized online experts and $0<\varepsilon<1$, we construct a randomized online algorithm whose realized total cost is always at most $(2+\varepsilon)$ times the offline optimal cost $\mathrm{OPT}$ and whose expected regret against the expert with the smallest realized cost is $O(\sqrt{\mathrm{OPT}\log(k+1)}+\log(1/\varepsilon))$. The algorithm requires no advance knowledge of the number of packets or $\mathrm{OPT}$. Our algorithm controls switching costs between experts and uses the gap between the permitted competitive bound and its current cost to determine when to learn. We also prove lower bounds showing that, for $k\ge 2$, this regret bound is asymptotically optimal in $\mathrm{OPT}$, $k$, and $\varepsilon$, and that $\varepsilon>0$ is necessary for sublinear regret.

</details>


### [196] [$L_0$ Feature Selection via Monte Carlo Tree Search in High-Dimensional Financial Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13574)
*Cameron Mouangue,Tristan Cazenave*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于蒙特卡洛树搜索(GNRPA)的特征选择框架，以样本外验证性能为目标，在高维金融回归中优于Lasso。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维金融回归中，大量工程化变量与预测因子间的强相关性会削弱模型的稳定性与可解释性。Lasso虽被广泛用于稀疏回归，但其收缩性质在信号分散于相关变量时存在局限；而精确稀疏回归虽避免收缩，却需解组合优化问题，计算困难，且通常以样本内误差最小化为目标，而非样本外误差。

Method: 将特征选择建模为序贯决策过程，采用蒙特卡洛树搜索结合广义嵌套策略自适应(GNRPA)进行求解；候选特征子集以样本外验证性能而非训练误差进行评分，并支持兼顾子集规模与多重共线性的字典序目标。实验任务为同期解释性回归：用覆盖市场、宏观经济与因子数据的1072个工程化特征解释纳斯达克100指数周收益率。

Result: 在该任务上，所提方法识别出的稀疏特征子集样本外解释力高于经过优化的Lasso基线，样本外R²达0.6961，而Lasso为0.6313。

Conclusion: 蒙特卡洛树搜索是凸正则化方法在金融回归稀疏解释性特征选择中的一种实用替代方案，在因子归因与风险分解等既重视样本外拟合又重视透明度的场景中具有潜在价值。

Abstract: Feature selection is a central problem in high-dimensional financial regression, where large numbers of engineered variables and strong correlations among predictors reduce model stability and interpretability. $L_1$ regularization (Lasso) is widely used for sparse regression, but its shrinkage can be restrictive when informative signals are spread across correlated variables. Exact sparse regression avoids this shrinkage but requires solving a combinatorial optimization problem that is computationally difficult and is typically fit to minimize in-sample rather than out-of-sample error. This paper presents a feature selection framework based on Monte Carlo Tree Search with Generalized Nested Rollout Policy Adaptation (GNRPA). Feature selection is formulated as a sequential decision process, and candidate subsets are scored by their out-of-sample validation performance rather than training error. The method also supports a lexicographic objective accounting for subset size and multicollinearity. We study this approach on a contemporaneous explanatory regression task: explaining the weekly return of the NASDAQ-100 index from 1,072 engineered features spanning market, macroeconomic, and factor data. This isolates the value of subset selection under multicollinearity from the separate problem of predictive forecasting. On this task, the proposed method identifies sparse feature subsets with higher out-of-sample explanatory power than an optimized Lasso baseline, reaching an out-of-sample $R^2$ of 0.6961 versus 0.6313 for Lasso. These results suggest that Monte Carlo Tree Search is a practical alternative to convex regularization for sparse explanatory feature selection in financial regression, with potential value for factor attribution and risk decomposition where transparency matters as much as out-of-sample fit.

</details>


### [197] [EchoTab: Learning Measurement-Grounded Representations for Echocardiographic Diagnosis through Tabular Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13599)
*Michelle Espranita Liman,Ioana Ionac,Alexander Müller,Eimo Martens,Daniel Rueckert,Philip Müller*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EchoTab 是一个基于测量的多视角超声心动图表示学习框架，用测量特异性 token 作为视角与诊断之间的中间表示，通过表格重建训练，无需负样本或大批量，在诊断分类上取得提升且注意力与临床测量-视角关系一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多视角医学影像（如超声心动图）的诊断信息分散在各视角中，临床流程是先做定量测量再由测量支持诊断。但现有表示学习方法未显式将测量建模为视角与诊断之间的中间表示，导致单个测量无法被下游诊断任务按需选用，影响诊断准确性。

Method: 提出 EchoTab：以测量特异性 token 作为视角与诊断间的中间表示。每个测量 token 通过注意力选择性关注与其估计相关的视角，并经表格重建训练以预测对应测量值，从而形成因子化的表示。

Result: 在两个数据集上，EchoTab 学到有效的测量基础表示，提升多视角超声心动图的诊断分类性能；其学习到的注意力与心脏科医生使用的测量-视角关系相吻合。

Conclusion: 模拟临床分层工作流的测量基础表示学习能编码具有临床意义的结构与功能属性，可选择性用于下游诊断，且不依赖负样本或大批量（区别于对比学习方法）。

Abstract: Diagnostic information in multi-view medical imaging studies, like echocardiography, is distributed across views, each capturing different aspects of cardiac structure and function. In clinical practice, these views are first interpreted through quantitative measurements, which then support specific diagnoses. However, existing echo representation learning methods do not explicitly model measurements as an intermediate representation between views and diagnosis. As a result, individual measurements are not explicitly represented in a form that downstream diagnostic tasks can selectively use, hindering accurate diagnosis.
  Inspired by this hierarchical clinical workflow, we introduce EchoTab, a measurement-grounded representation learning framework that represents multi-view echocardiography studies using measurement-specific tokens as an intermediate representation between echo views and diagnosis. Each measurement token selectively attends to the views relevant to its estimation and is trained through tabular reconstruction to predict the corresponding measurement value. The resulting tokens form a factorized echo representation, which encode clinically meaningful structural and functional properties and can be selectively used for downstream diagnosis. Unlike the commonly used contrastive approaches, EchoTab does not require negative samples or large batch sizes. Experiments on two datasets demonstrate that EchoTab learns effective measurement-grounded representations that improve diagnosis classification in multi-view echocardiography and its learned attention aligns with established measurement-view relationships used by cardiologists. Our code is accessible at https://github.com/michelleespranita/EchoTab.

</details>


### [198] [SelectArena: A Modern Benchmark for Feature Selection on Real-world Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13644)
*Bastian Schäfer,Dominika Matus,Lennart Purucker,Ricardo Knauer,Erik Rodner,Frank Hutter,Anton Sokolov,Arnaud Liefooghe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了 SelectArena，一个面向真实世界应用的特征选择基准，在 71 个手工整理的真实表格数据集上，以标准化协议和先进表格（基础）模型比较 15 种常用特征选择方法，揭示其在预测性能、运行时间、稳定性与有效性之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 特征选择在工业、气候科学、临床诊断等领域广泛需求，但现有基准存在局限：依赖过时模型、临时评测协议或人工生成数据，无法反映真实问题，导致实践者难以据此选择方法。作者旨在弥合科学基准与实际应用之间的差距。

Method: 构建 SelectArena 基准：在 71 个手工整理的真实表格数据集（涵盖分类与回归任务）上，采用标准化评测协议与当前最先进的表格（基础）模型，对 15 种广泛使用的特征选择方法进行对比，并从预测性能、运行时间、稳定性与有效性多个维度评估。

Result: 各方法在预测性能、运行时间、稳定性和有效性上呈现明显权衡。基于树的方法（CART、RFImportance）与 F-Test 在兼顾预测性能与运行时间时达到 Pareto 最优；F-Test、基于信息论和基于距离的方法在稳定性与有效性上领先，其中 F-Test 在各指标间表现均衡；基于搜索的方法则速度慢、稳定性差且性能低。

Conclusion: SelectArena 将上述发现转化为实践指导，并作为可扩展平台支持未来的特征选择研究。

Abstract: Selecting a subset of features (columns) from a tabular dataset is a ubiquitous challenge in real-world applications. Industry experts seek to minimize model training costs, climate scientists require an interpretable subset of factors to understand extreme weather events, and clinicians need to choose among diagnostic tests due to financial and time constraints. When looking at the scientific literature to determine which feature selection method to use, practitioners across domains face an unrepresentative landscape of benchmarking. Despite extensive research, existing feature selection benchmarks provide limited guidance because they rely on outdated models, ad hoc evaluation protocols, or artificially generated data that do not reflect real-world problems. To close the gap between scientific benchmarking and real-world applications, we introduce SelectArena, a modern benchmark for feature selection. We compare 15 widely used feature selection methods on 71 manually curated real-world tabular datasets, covering classification and regression problems, using standardized evaluation protocols and state-of-the-art tabular (foundation) models. Our results reveal clear trade-offs across predictive performance, runtime, stability, and validity. Tree-based methods, CART and RFImportance, along with F-Test are Pareto-optimal when taking into account predictive performance and runtime. F-Test, information-theory-, and distance-based methods lead on stability and validity, with F-Test emerging as a balanced choice across our metrics. Search-based methods are slow, unstable and achieve low performance. SelectArena turns these findings into practical guidance and, as an extensible platform, supports future feature selection research.

</details>


### [199] [A small cognitive core explains human behavior at scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13603)
*Marcel Binz,Eric Schulz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CORE模型，将一组既有认知原则整合为一体，联合拟合了137个实验、7,036,442次选择，预测表现优于黑箱模型，并能复现75%的行为效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心理学提出了许多认知原则，但大多在孤立条件下发展，其组合后的解释力未知，缺乏整体性检验。

Method: 构建COntextual Retrieval and Encoding（CORE）模型：由情景记忆层级构成，预测误差被写入其中，编码、提取与遗忘均依赖情境；并联合拟合来自137个实验的7,036,442次选择数据。

Result: CORE对人类选择的预测优于黑箱模型，在仿真中复现了75%的已报告行为效应；其潜变量呈现贝叶斯学习与事件分割的经典特征。

Conclusion: 少数认知核心即可解释大部分人类行为，这为在完整实验记录上构建计算模型、让心理学超越各部分简单相加提供了路径。

Abstract: Psychology has proposed many principles of cognition. Each, however, was developed in isolation, leaving their combined explanatory power unknown. We assembled a small set of established principles into one model, which we call COntextual Retrieval and Encoding (CORE). CORE consists of a hierarchy of episodic memories into which prediction errors are written and whose encoding, retrieval, and forgetting depend on context. Fitted jointly to 7,036,442 choices from 137 experiments, the model predicts human choices better than black-box models and reproduces 75% of reported behavioral effects when simulated. Moreover, its latent variables show classic signatures of Bayesian learning and event segmentation. Taken together, these findings suggest that a small cognitive core accounts for much of human behavior. They also open a path toward making psychology more than the sum of its parts, by building computational models on the entire experimental record.

</details>


### [200] [Pretraining and Test-Time Training Budgets Matter for ARC: A Controlled Study of VARC and TRM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13660)
*Adrien Deliege,Marc Van Droogenbroeck*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在统一归纳协议下研究预训练与测试时训练（TTT）预算对VARC与TRM两个ARC模型性能的影响，发现预算显著影响评估结论，呼吁在可比预算下报告结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期ARC模型在架构之外还存在预训练数据、数据增强、测试时设置与优化预算等训练与评估协议差异，导致难以判断性能提升究竟源于架构优势还是预算差异，因此需要控制预算变量进行系统比较。

Method: 在ARC-AGI-1上采用统一的归纳协议，对vision-inspired的VARC与recursion-based的TRM进行交叉实验：为每个测试任务将5种预训练预算与4种TTT预算组合，每种预算由任务变体数量与总样本呈现次数定义，从而控制训练预算。

Result: 在所有设置下，增加TTT样本呈现次数均提升两个模型的pass@2指标；增加TTT任务变体数量仅持续有利于TRM，而VARC在更少变体时往往表现更好；VARC在无预训练时仍具竞争力，TRM则更依赖预训练。

Conclusion: 预训练与TTT预算会显著影响ARC评估结果，模型比较应在可比预算条件下进行并予以报告。

Abstract: The Abstraction and Reasoning Corpus (ARC) is increasingly used to evaluate whether neural networks can infer task-specific transformations from few demonstrations. Recent ARC models differ in architecture, but also in broader training and evaluation protocols, including pretraining data, data augmentation, test-time setup, and optimization budgets, making conclusions about architecture benefits debatable. In this work, we study how the performance of the vision-inspired VARC and recursion-based TRM models is affected by pretraining and test-time training (TTT) budgets under a common inductive protocol on ARC-AGI-1. We cross five pretraining budgets with four TTT budgets for each test task. Each budget specifies a task-variant count and total sample presentations, defining the controlled training budgets. We observe that increasing TTT sample presentations improves the standard ARC pass@2 metric in all settings for both models. Increasing the TTT task-variant count consistently helps only TRM, whereas VARC often performs better with fewer variants. VARC also remains competitive without pretraining, while TRM benefits much more from it. These results show that pretraining and TTT budgets affect ARC evaluation and motivate reporting at comparable budgets.

</details>


### [201] [Private and Distributionally Robust Federated Multiclass Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13631)
*Andrea P. Hernandez,Michael Ibrahim,Nagi Gebraeel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种差分隐私、分布鲁棒（Wasserstein DRO）的联邦多分类框架，基于Crammer-Singer损失与ADMM求解，在客户端近端线性化更新中注入高斯噪声以保护样本级隐私，并提供每轮及整体隐私保证、收敛性与隐私-效用权衡，实验优于DP-FedERM与DP-FedSGD基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 资产密集型工业故障诊断需在跨地域客户端收集的含噪、敏感数据上训练多分类模型。联邦学习可避免数据集中化，但多数方法对客户端个体记录缺乏形式化隐私保证，且在标签噪声下性能退化。因此需同时解决样本级隐私保护与噪声鲁棒性问题。

Method: 以Crammer-Singer损失构建联邦Wasserstein分布鲁棒多分类目标，采用ADMM型算法求解。为保护上传的客户端更新，在中心服务器聚合前，向本地模型与本地分布鲁棒变量的近端线性化客户端更新注入校准高斯噪声；用矩会计（moments accountant）量化累积隐私损失。

Result: 给出每轮样本级(ε,δ)-差分隐私保证与全部训练轮次的组合隐私保证，并给出收敛性结果与显式的隐私-效用权衡。在模拟工业故障数据与UCI基准数据集上，多数设置下优于差分隐私联邦经验风险最小化（DP-FedERM）和差分隐私联邦随机梯度下降（DP-FedSGD）基线，在强隐私噪声下增益最大。

Conclusion: 将差分隐私与联邦Wasserstein分布鲁棒优化结合，可为客户端存储记录提供样本级隐私保障，同时提升标签噪声下的鲁棒性；所提方法在隐私-效用权衡上表现更优，适用于资产密集型工业故障诊断的联邦多分类场景。

Abstract: Fault diagnosis in asset-intensive industries requires multiclass models trained on noisy, sensitive data collected across geographically distributed client sites. Federated learning enables collaborative training without centralizing data, but most approaches do not provide formal privacy guarantees for individual records stored at client sites and can degrade under label noise. To address these limitations, we develop a differentially private, distributionally robust framework for federated multiclass classification that provides sample-level privacy for records stored at each client. We build on a federated Wasserstein distributionally robust multiclass objective with the Crammer-Singer loss, solved using an Alternating Direction Method of Multipliers (ADMM)-based algorithm. To protect the released client updates, our method injects calibrated Gaussian noise into the proximal linearized client updates of both the local model and the local distributionally robust variable before aggregation at the central server, and we quantify the cumulative privacy loss using the moments accountant. We provide per-round sample-level $(ε,δ)$-differential privacy guarantees and an overall composed privacy guarantee across all training rounds, together with convergence results and an explicit privacy-utility tradeoff. Experiments on simulated industrial fault data and UCI benchmark datasets show that our method outperforms differentially private federated empirical risk minimization and stochastic gradient descent baselines in most tested settings, with the largest gains under strong privacy-induced noise.

</details>


### [202] [Variance-Reduced Policy Gradient is Nearly Minimax-Optimal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13692)
*Leonardo Cesani,Matteo Papini,Marcello Restelli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LVR-PG统一理论框架，抽象一类结合递归更新与防御性重要性采样的一阶估计器，在弱梯度支配下实现$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(\epsilon^{-2})$最优样本复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准策略梯度（PG）算法依赖蒙特卡洛更新，导致高估计方差。现有离策略方差缩减方法虽能缓解该问题，但达到最优样本复杂度需依赖强限制性假设（如重要性权重一致有界）或不切实际的二阶运算。

Method: 提出Loopless Variance-Reduced PG (LVR-PG)框架，统一抽象一类一阶估计器，融合递归更新与防御性重要性采样，涵盖PAGE-PG、STORM-PG等已知估计器；仅使用纯一阶更新，在最小假设下且无需梯度预条件器。

Result: 证明该算法类在弱梯度支配下对一般参数化策略具有$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(\epsilon^{-2})$的末次迭代全局收敛率；通过将现有下界扩展至连续参数化策略，建立$\Omega(\epsilon^{-2})$样本复杂度下界，确认该框架在忽略对数因子与horizon项时达到minimax最优。

Conclusion: LVR-PG以最小假设和纯一阶更新实现最优样本复杂度，达到minimax最优（至多相差对数因子与horizon项），为方差缩减策略梯度提供了统一的理论分析基础。

Abstract: Standard Policy Gradient (PG) algorithms suffer from high estimation variance because they rely on Monte Carlo updates. While off-policy variance reduction mitigates this problem, establishing optimal sample complexity currently requires either restrictive assumptions, such as uniformly bounded importance weights, or impractical second-order operations. In this work, we introduce Loopless Variance-Reduced PG (LVR-PG), a unified theoretical framework that abstracts a class of first-order estimators that integrate recursive updates with defensive importance sampling, including known estimators such as PAGE-PG and STORM-PG. We prove that this algorithmic class achieves $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(ε^{-2})$ last-iterate global convergence under weak gradient domination for general parametric policies. We establish this guarantee using purely first-order updates, with minimal assumptions and without gradient preconditioners. By extending state-of-the-art lower bounds to continuous parametric policies, we establish an $Ω(ε^{-2})$ sample complexity limit, confirming our framework is minimax-optimal up to logarithmic factors and horizon terms.

</details>


### [203] [PANDA: Prior-guided Attentional Dual-path Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13635)
*Rishabh Jain,Federico Belotti,Stefano Coniglio,Pietro Lio,Stefano Fiorini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PANDA（先验引导的双路径注意力架构），作为两阶段消息传递超图神经网络（HNN）的插件，通过一条在接收者归一化注意力与结构关联先验之间插值的主路径，以及一条非竞争性辅助路径，解决了固定权重在异质场景下传播错误信息与归一化注意力系数竞争的问题。PANDA适用于有向和无向设置，在四个HNN与八个真实基准数据集上平均提升5.18个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超图神经网络用于建模超越成对交互的高阶关系，但现有方法通常依赖固定结构权重或归一化注意力。固定权重在异质（heterophilic）场景下会传播误导性信息；而归一化使注意力系数具有竞争性，即在消息传递框架中，提高某一发送者的权重必然降低同一聚合集合中其他发送者的权重。作者希望同时缓解这两个局限。

Method: 提出PANDA（Prior-guided AttentioNal Dual-path Architecture），一种面向两阶段消息传递HNN的插件，同时适用于无向和有向设置。其主路径在学习到的接收者归一化注意力与结构关联先验（incidence priors）之间进行插值；另设一条非竞争性（未归一化）的辅助路径，使每个发送者都能贡献自身变换后的信息。作者还证明，在固定结构系数下，PANDA可退化为先前工作采用的某种复值卷积算子。

Result: 在涵盖空间与谱方法、有向与无向设置的四个HNN上，将PANDA作为插件引入后，在八个真实世界基准数据集上平均提升5.18个百分点。代码已开源于 https://github.com/jainris/panda。

Conclusion: PANDA以插件形式有效融合结构先验与注意力机制，并通过非竞争性辅助路径克服归一化注意力的竞争性限制，在多种HNN架构与数据集上取得一致且显著的性能提升，具有良好的通用性与可迁移性。

Abstract: Hypergraph Neural Networks (HNNs), designed to model higher-order relations beyond pairwise interactions, typically rely either on fixed structural weights or on normalised attention. Fixed weights can propagate misleading information in heterophilic settings, while normalisation makes attention coefficients competitive, in the sense that, in a message-passing framework, increasing the weight of one sender must reduce the weight of others in the same aggregation set. Our main contribution is the introduction of PANDA (Prior-guided AttentioNal Dual-path Architecture), a plug-in for two-stage message passing HNNs applicable to both undirected and directed settings. The main path interpolates between learned receiver-normalised attention and structural incidence priors, while a non-competitive (not normalised) auxiliary path lets each sender contribute its own transformed information. We also show that, with fixed structural coefficients, PANDA recovers a certain complex-valued convolution operator adopted in previous works. Across four HNNs spanning spatial and spectral methods in both directed and undirected settings, plugging PANDA in yields an average improvement of 5.18 percentage points across eight real-world benchmark datasets. The code is available at https://github.com/jainris/panda.

</details>


### [204] [Calibration in One Go: Jointly Learning Accurate and Calibrated Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13693)
*Nektarios Kalampalikis,Pablo M. Olmos,Isabel Valera*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于双层Stackelberg博弈的端到端校准框架，将后处理校准器与主干网络联合优化，无需单独校准集和校准损失，仅引入一个超参数（校准器学习率），实验表明以最终分类层作为校准器可在无额外参数下取得优于SOTA校准器的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络灵活通用但校准性差。现有后处理校准方法需依赖单独的校准数据集、额外的校准参数，并需分别优化校准器与进行后续模型选择，带来额外开销与冗余流程。

Method: 提出双层Stackelberg公式，将标准后处理校准器与主干网络端到端联合训练。用交叉熵同时优化准确率与校准性，消除对单独校准集和专用校准损失的需求，仅新增一个超参数（校准器学习率）。框架支持从温度缩放、矩阵缩放直至NN层的多种校准器。

Result: 以最终分类层本身作为校准器时，可得到准确且良好校准的模型，且不增加任何额外参数。该方法在增加极少复杂度的情况下优于SOTA校准器，并在多任务学习等场景中避免了独立校准组件带来的乘性开销。

Conclusion: 后处理校准可被无缝整合进主干网络的端到端训练中，简化了校准流程、减少超参数与额外组件，同时保持或提升性能，为高效校准提供了新范式。

Abstract: Neural networks (NNs), while highly flexible and versatile, are often poorly calibrated. Post-hoc calibration methods adjust the outputs of an already trained model using a separate calibration dataset and additional calibration parameters. This process adds overhead that requires separate optimization of the calibrator and subsequent model selection. In this paper, we show that standard (post-hoc) calibrators can be trained end-to-end with the backbone via a bilevel Stackelberg formulation. This formulation eliminates the need for a separate calibration set or a dedicated calibration loss by jointly optimizing accuracy and calibration using cross-entropy, introducing only one additional hyperparameter: the calibrator learning rate. Although our framework supports calibrators ranging from temperature and matrix scaling to NN layers, our results show that using the final classification layer itself as the calibrator yields accurate, well-calibrated models with no additional parameters. Our approach outperforms SOTA calibrators with minimal added complexity, while also avoiding the multiplicative overhead of separate calibration components in settings such as multi-task learning.

</details>


### [205] [Amortized Counterfactual Prediction with Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13724)
*Hendrik Stumpf,Francesco Quinzan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出CFGNP，一种端到端的反事实图神经过程，直接从观测数据回答反事实查询，无需先估计因果图，从而避免图估计误差的传播。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反事实推断需要已知潜在因果结构，而现实中该结构往往不可得。常见做法是先估计因果图再做反事实推断，但图估计中的误差会传播到下游的反事实预测中，影响准确性。

Method: CFGNP利用图神经网络（GNN）刻画"双图"结构，将跨世界（cross-world）消息传递限制在对应的实际—反事实变量之间，从而在无需显式估计因果图的情况下直接完成反事实查询。

Result: 在合成数据与图像基准上，CFGNP的预测性能优于现有方法；在German Loan基准上，MSE由4.51×10⁻²降至0.19×10⁻²。此外，仅在10节点图上训练的模型在无需重训的情况下，可对多达30节点的图保持较强预测性能。

Conclusion: CFGNP通过端到端方式规避了因果图估计误差传播问题，并在多个基准上取得更优性能且具备良好的规模泛化能力。

Abstract: Counterfactual inference asks how an observed outcome might have differed under different circumstances, such as whether a patient would have recovered had they received a different treatment. Such questions arise whenever decisions depend on the outcomes of alternatives that were not observed. Answering counterfactual queries generally requires knowledge of the underlying causal structure, which is often unavailable in practice. A common approach is therefore to first estimate the causal graph from observational data and then perform counterfactual inference. However, errors in the estimated causal graph can propagate to downstream counterfactual predictions. To address this challenge, we introduce the Counterfactual Graph Neural Process (CFGNP), an end-to-end approach that answers counterfactual queries directly from observational data without first estimating a causal graph. CFGNP uses Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) to exploit the dual-graph structure by restricting cross-world message passing to corresponding factual--counterfactual variables. Across synthetic and image-based benchmarks, CFGNP achieves stronger predictive performance than existing approaches, including on the German Loan benchmark, where it reduces MSE from $4.51 \times 10^{-2}$ to $0.19 \times 10^{-2}$. Furthermore, on synthetic causal graphs, a model trained only on 10-node graphs maintains strong predictive performance on graphs with up to 30 nodes, without retraining.

</details>


### [206] [TabReliBench: Evaluating Tabular Foundation Models Beyond Predictive Accuracy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13654)
*Tianqi Zhao,Jia Guo,Yan Shuo Tan,Qiong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文提出TabReliBench基准，从点预测、分布质量、边际与子组校准、区间效率多维度评估表格基础模型(TFM)的后验预测分布可靠性，发现TFMs排名随基准变化、边际覆盖良好但条件覆盖未必有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格基础模型(TFMs)预测性能强且日益流行，许多还提供后验预测分布(PPDs)，但其可靠性研究不足。需要超越聚合排名的多维度评估以支持可靠的TFM选择。

Method: 通过点预测、分布质量、边际与子组校准、区间效率四类指标评估可靠性；构建TabReliBench大型分类与回归基准，进行数据集级分析，并考察集成与共形后处理的效果。

Result: TFMs在TabArena与本基准间排名不一致，且在不同可靠性指标间排名变化；回归TFMs通常达到接近名义的边际覆盖并保持窄区间，但边际良好不代表条件覆盖有效；分类难度常伴随校准差，而回归预测性能不预示校准好坏；集成提升预测性能但不稳定改善PPD校准；TFMs从共形后处理获益有限，且不能可靠消除子组差距。

Conclusion: 可靠的TFM选择需要超越聚合排名的多维度评估。代码见https://github.com/Tianqi-Zhao/TabReliBench。

Abstract: Tabular foundation models (TFMs) are increasingly popular for their strong predictive performance. Many also provide posterior predictive distributions (PPDs), whose reliability remains less studied. We assess reliability through point prediction, distributional quality, marginal and subgroup calibration, and interval efficiency. We then introduce TabReliBench, a large classification and regression benchmark for these comparisons and dataset-level analyses.
