Because agents lack deployment awareness, attempting cross-domain modifications introduces unverified dependencies into asynchronous integration pipelines. To isolate the causal integration overhead of dual-scope pull requests (PRs)-modifying both application code and deployment infrastructure-we evaluated 26,999 PRs across two large datasets (AIDev v5 and MOSAIC-3M) encompassing six agent architectures and an aligned human baseline. Using a deterministic four-tier file taxonomy and an exact 4D strata-matching framework, we isolated integration metrics under conditional exchangeability while controlling for application volume. We find agents operate primarily as application coders or chore workers, with maintenance tasks capturing 17.70% to 25.41% of agent PRs. Dual-scope modifications represent only 4.48% to 4.55% of agentic PRs, yet trigger bimodal integration outcomes. In the first mode, explicitly reviewed contributions experience protracted delays, adding a median penalty of +13.13 hours and multiplying time-to-merge by 3.24x, driven mechanically by CI/CD and provisioning manifests. In the second mode, maintainer gatekeeping exhibits a structural bypass: even for external contributors lacking repository write access, agent identity reduces the odds of human review by 63.7% compared to human peers. Consequently, 43.1% to 56.2% of dual-scope PRs bypass inspection to merge silently in a median of under 31 minutes. These uninspected modifications compromise build verification, demonstrating the need for deployment-aware benchmarks and strict path-based access controls.

</details>


### [241] [Evaluation of Software Quality Through Refactoring Commit-Impact Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12890)
*Md Safayat Hossen,Sheikh Moonwara Anjum Monisha,Shadid Intisar Quasem*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究通过挖掘GitHub开源仓库并进行静态代码分析，考察重构提交前后代码修改与软件质量指标（规模、复杂度、代码异味、漏洞、缺陷）的相关性，并以表格和图表汇总分析结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望实证检验代码修改（尤其是重构提交前后的变更）与软件质量之间的关联，弥补相关实证证据的不足。

Method: 采用仓库挖掘与多种静态代码分析工具，对GitHub开源软件进行静态分析；围绕研究问题选取规模、复杂度、代码异味、漏洞和缺陷检测等软件度量，比较每次重构提交前后的代码变化。

Result: 研究获得了重构提交前后各项软件度量指标的变化数据，并将结果整理为表格和图表以支持进一步分析。

Conclusion: 结果表明可通过重构提交前后的度量对比来刻画代码修改与软件质量的关系，并据此回答所设定的研究问题，完成案例分析。

Abstract: The aim of this study is to conduct an empirical analysis of the correlation between code modifications and software quality. This will be achieved by examining the effects of changes made before and after each refactoring commit, using software metrics such as size, complexity, code smells, vulnerability, and bug detection. The objective is to conduct static code analysis on open-source software from GitHub repositories using various repository mining and static code analysis tools. Our approach involves formulating research-based questions and utilizing the specified tools to obtain results that will enable us to resolve the case study. The obtained results have been summarised and further analyzed using tables and graphs to achieve the objective.

</details>


### [242] [SCCGBench: Executability-Oriented Evaluation for Real-World Web Service-Call Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12920)
*Shuo Wang,Guodong Fan,Ming Zhu,Jing Li,Chun Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出SCCGBench，一个基于真实GitHub仓库、含3,135个可追溯RapidAPI调用函数的服务调用代码生成基准，通过模拟执行（Mock）等指标评估LLM在服务调用代码生成中的接地能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM生成的服务调用代码在服务计算中日益重要，但现有方法尚未在实际代码环境中评估服务的调用与执行，缺乏执行接地（execution-grounded）的评测手段。

Method: 构建SCCGBench基准：从真实GitHub仓库收集3,135个可追溯且经去污染过滤的RapidAPI调用函数；采用redacted-intent生成减少循环泄漏；设计Mock、Robust、ParamF1及主机、端点、方法、参数级诊断指标。在intent-only、检索增强和gold-documentation Oracle三种设置下，评测6个代码LLM和1个非神经网络检索基线。

Result: intent-only设置下模型常生成语法正确或表面相似的HTTP请求代码，但Mock成功率极低，最强模型仅3.76%；Oracle设置（C3、C5）下，开源模型Mock达88.73%--90.52%，DeepSeek-V4-Pro为76.47%--81.86%，而稀疏与稠密检索远低于该水平。

Conclusion: 服务/API接地是主要瓶颈：Mock由主机、端点和方法的对齐主导，ParamF1单独揭示参数对齐错误。SCCGBench为Web服务调用、API推荐、服务接口检索、端点接地及可靠服务调用自动化提供了执行接地的评测基准。

Abstract: LLM-generated service-call code is increasingly important for service computing, where developers rely on large language models (LLMs) to compose services and invoke third-party services. However, current approaches have not yet evaluated the invocation and execution of services in the actual code environment. To support execution-grounded evaluation, we present the Service-Call Code Generation Benchmark (SCCGBench), a benchmark of 3,135 traceable and decontamination-filtered RapidAPI call functions from real GitHub repositories. SCCGBench uses redacted-intent generation to reduce circular leakage and introduces Mock, Robust, ParamF1, and host-, endpoint-, method-, and parameter-level diagnostics. Mock denotes a virtual-execution hit that simultaneously matches the gold host, endpoint, and HTTP method, while Robust further requires status checking and exception handling. We evaluate six code LLMs and one non-neural retrieval baseline under intent-only, retrieval-augmented, and gold-documentation Oracle settings. Results show that models often generate syntactically correct or superficially similar HTTP-request code but achieve low Mock success in the intent-only setting; the strongest evaluated model obtains only 3.76% Mock. Under the gold-documentation Oracle settings (C3 and C5), Mock reaches 88.73%--90.52% for the open-source models evaluated in RQ2 and 76.47%--81.86% for DeepSeek-V4-Pro, whereas sparse and dense retrieval remain far below this oracle level. The findings indicate that service/API grounding is the main bottleneck: Mock is dominated by host, endpoint, and method alignment, while ParamF1 separately reveals parameter-alignment errors. SCCGBench provides an execution-grounded benchmark for Web service invocation, API recommendation, service-interface retrieval, endpoint grounding, and reliable service-call automation.

</details>


### [243] [Don't Guess, Just Ask: Evaluating Interactive Requirement Clarification for Repository-Level Software Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13022)
*Mingyu Chen,Zhiqing Zhong,Manyi Wang,Linxi Liang,Rouyu Chen,Pinjia He*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出 ReqClarifyBench，一个面向仓库级软件开发需求的交互式澄清基准，并揭示了澄清效果与文档质量、用户画像及编码智能体框架之间的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准多从完整规格或欠明确请求出发评估代码实现，忽略了编码前的需求澄清；而已有的澄清研究主要聚焦函数级任务，尚不清楚 LLM 能否在仓库级开发中通过交互将模糊意图转化为清晰需求，以及该能力如何影响后续代码生成。

Method: 构建包含 341 个任务的 ReqClarifyBench，覆盖游戏、小程序和网页开发，每个任务含模糊用户意图、经人工校验的需求文档和代码评估工件；采用基于真实对话人物画像的 LLM 用户模拟器，对六个 LLM 从澄清有效性和生成需求文档质量两方面进行评估。

Result: 更强的澄清有效性不必然带来更高的文档质量；不同用户画像下性能差异显著；编码智能体框架相较于其基础 LLM 并未进一步提升需求文档质量。

Conclusion: 研究为构建基于 LLM 的端到端软件开发系统提供了实证洞察，强调需求澄清能力与文档质量、用户画像及智能体框架之间的复杂关系。

Abstract: As LLMs become increasingly capable of implementing software, a critical bottleneck emerges: whether they truly understand what users want. Users often begin with vague intent, leaving important requirements to be clarified through interaction. In repository-level development, misunderstandings can propagate and cause costly rework. However, existing benchmarks typically evaluate implementation from complete specifications or underspecified requests, overlooking pre-coding requirement clarification. Prior clarification studies mainly focus on function-level tasks. It therefore remains unclear how well LLMs can transform vague intent into clear requirements through interaction and how this capability affects subsequent code generation.
  We introduce ReqClarifyBench, an interactive requirement clarification benchmark for repository-level software development. It contains 341 tasks spanning game, miniapp, and web development, each with a vague user intent, a human-validated requirement document, and code evaluation artifacts. We employ LLM-based user simulators with diverse personas derived from real-world conversations. We evaluate six LLMs in terms of clarification effectiveness and generated requirement document quality. Results show that stronger clarification effectiveness does not necessarily translate into higher document quality, and performance varies substantially across user personas. We also find that coding-agent harnesses do not further improve requirement document quality compared with their base LLMs. These findings offer insights for building LLM-based end-to-end software development systems.

</details>


### [244] [TestSuiteBench: Coding Agents Struggle to Generate Strong Test Suites from Specifications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13065)
*Kazuki Egashira,Niels Mündler-Sasahara,Mark Niklas Müller,Martin Vechev*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出 TestSuiteBench，一个包含165个开源应用的测试套件生成基准，每个应用含行为规范、参考实现、参考测试套件和平均74个变异体。实验发现前沿编码智能体生成的测试套件变异得分低于60%，揭示智能体能构建但无法验证软件的鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码生成基准已扩展到完整应用规模，但测试生成基准仍局限于单个函数或缺陷修复；尤其从零为复杂软件生成完整测试套件这一任务尚未被探索。为填补此空白，作者构建了面向完整测试套件生成的评测基准。

Method: 构建TestSuiteBench基准，包含165个开源应用，每个应用配有结构化且详细的预期行为规范、参考实现、参考测试套件，以及平均74个变异体。每个变异体由规范中单一需求生成并经精炼以隔离该行为；73%的变异体仅被恰好一个参考测试杀死，且平均仅被剩余测试的0.2%杀死。基于该基准评测前沿编码智能体的测试套件生成能力，并以变异得分和参考测试通过率衡量。

Result: 即使前沿编码智能体也难以根据规范生成强测试套件：变异得分低于60%，尽管其生成的套件大体有效、88%的测试能在参考实现上通过。生成的测试套件常缺乏相关输入组合，规模也小于参考测试套件。相比之下，同一规范生成的实现能通过82%的参考测试。

Conclusion: 智能体能够构建其无法验证的软件，这一能力鸿沟直接制约了完全自主的软件开发。测试套件生成能力显著落后于实现生成能力，是自动化软件开发的关键瓶颈。

Abstract: Coding agents are increasingly used to create large software projects. While code-generation benchmarks have scaled accordingly to full applications, test-generation benchmarks remain limited to individual functions or bug fixes. In particular, generating complete test suites for complex software from scratch remains unexplored. To address this gap, we introduce TestSuiteBench, a benchmark of 165 open-source applications, each with a structured and detailed specification of intended behavior, a reference implementation, a reference test suite, and on average 74 mutants. Each mutant is generated from a single requirement of the specification and refined to isolate that behavior: 73\% are killed by exactly one reference test and, on average, by only 0.2\% of the remaining tests. We find that even frontier coding agents struggle to generate strong test suites given our specifications, achieving mutation scores below 60\%, despite their suites being largely valid and 88\% of their tests passing on the reference implementation. We observe that generated test suites often lack relevant input combinations and are smaller than reference test suites. Meanwhile, implementations generated for the same specifications pass 82\% of the reference tests. These results show agents can build software they cannot verify, a gap that directly limits fully autonomous software development.

</details>


### [245] [Agent4Spec: An LLM-based Agentic System to Optimize API Specifications through Automated Validation and Market Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13273)
*Yongjian Tang,Costel Moraru,Doruk Tuncel,Hanya Elhashemy,Steffen Klepke,Thomas Runkler*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出 Agent4Spec，一个自主多智能体框架，包含验证流水线与市场分析流水线，用于 API 规范的持续校验与功能演进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 LLM 的 API 规范生成方法把生成视为一次性事件，忽视了持续验证、长期维护以及市场驱动的演化需求。

Method: Agent4Spec 采用多智能体自主框架，含两条流水线：(1) 验证流水线，检测并自纠正 OpenAPI 语法错误；(2) 市场分析流水线，开展竞争性调研、识别相对行业领先者的功能缺口，并自动生成缺失的 API 规范功能。

Result: 在两个涵盖通用与工业场景的 API 规范数据集上，对 9 个 SOTA LLM 进行评估，最优模型实现 100% 语法错误检测率、超过 95% 的纠正实现成功率，以及超过 50% 的端点改进建议采纳率。

Conclusion: Agent4Spec 可作为 API 开发的实用工具，支持 API 规范的持续校验与市场驱动的功能演化。

Abstract: API specifications serve as critical documentation for modern software development, directly impacting integration efficiency and client code generation. While recent advancements have leveraged Large Language Models (LLMs) to generate initial specifications from source code, existing approaches treat this process as a one-time event - overlooking the needs for continuous validation, long-term maintenance, and market-driven evolution of these artifacts. To address this issue, we present Agent4Spec, an autonomous multi-agent framework featuring two distinct pipelines: (1) a validation pipeline that detects and self-corrects OpenAPI syntax errors, and (2) a market analysis pipeline that conducts competitive research, identifies functional gaps against industry leaders, and automatically generates missing API-spec functionalities. We evaluate Agent4Spec on two curated datasets of API specifications spanning both general-purpose and industrial scenarios. Among 9 SOTA LLMs, the top-performer achieves 100% syntax error detection rates and over 95% implementation success for proposed corrections, while securing over 50% adoption rates for suggested endpoint improvements, representing a practical tool for API development.

</details>


### [246] [AgentSWE: Can Coding Agents Build the Agent You Actually Want?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13361)
*Jiahao Wang,Hongjin Qian,Yuyang Hu,Jiajun Zhang,Zheng Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文提出 AgentSWE 基准，用于测试 coding agent 在构建 agent 软件时能否忠实满足需求，而非仅表面达标。覆盖创建、编辑、优化三阶段共 25 个任务，以“委托”而非“考试”形式评估：构建者依据少量示例迭代，验收则用冻结提交在覆盖完整需求的留出用例上按硬性通过/失败评分。结果显示现有各类 coding agent 均无法接近需求要求，“演示表现与交付表现之间的差距”是常态；失败模式包括过拟合可见用例、空壳运行时和不受支持的完成声明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着软件生产部分交由软件自身完成，coding agent 被大量委托去构建贴合用户自身工作流的 agent。这种委托隐含一个假设：交付的 agent 会忠实服务于其被构建时所需满足的需求，而不仅是看起来如此。但现有基准没有检验这一假设，因此作者需要一个新的评估工具来暴露“看似完成”与“真正满足需求”之间的落差。

Method: 提出 AgentSWE 基准，覆盖 agent 开发生命周期三阶段：从自然语言需求创建完整 agent、编辑生产级 agent 代码库、面向可度量目标进行优化。共 25 个任务，每个任务被设定为“委托”而非“考试”：构建者针对少量示例用例迭代开发，而验收阶段将冻结的提交在留出的、覆盖完整需求的用例上运行，按需求推导出的 rubric 在硬性 pass/fail 门槛下评分；优化类任务则按源基准自身的目标评分。随后在多种模型与 harness 组合下评估一系列 coding agent，并对失败轨迹进行实证分析。

Result: 没有一种 coding agent 能接近交付需求所要求的结果；构建者演示所达到的表现与其最终交付物所达到的表现之间存在系统性差距，且该差距是常态而非边缘情况。失败轨迹的实证分析还识别出若干反复出现的失败模式：对可见用例过拟合、代码看似完整但运行时空心化、以及不受支持的完成声明。

Conclusion: AgentSWE 填补了“agent 构建 agent 是否忠实满足需求”这一评估空白，并表明当前 coding agent 在真实委托场景下普遍无法交付符合需求的结果。文中归纳的失败模式可作为信号，帮助 coding agent 学习如何真正构建被要求的 agent。

Abstract: As the production of software passes into the hands of software itself, coding agents are increasingly commissioned to build agents fitted to their users' own workflows. This delegation carries a quiet assumption, that the delivered agent will faithfully serve the requirement it was built for, rather than merely appear to. No existing benchmark tests that assumption. We introduce AgentSWE, a benchmark for agent software engineering spanning three stages of the agent development lifecycle: Creation of a complete agent from a natural-language requirement, Editing of production agent codebases, and Optimization toward a measurable target. Each of its 25 tasks is framed as a commission rather than an exam: the builder iterates against a handful of illustrative cases, while acceptance exercises the frozen submission on held-out cases designed to span the full requirement, grading behavior against requirement-derived rubrics under hard pass/fail gates or, for Optimization, against the source benchmark's own objective. Evaluating a range of coding agents across models and harnesses, we find that none comes close to delivering what a requirement asks for: the gap between what a builder demonstrates and what it delivers is the norm, not the corner case. An accompanying empirical analysis of the failure trajectories identifies recurring failure modes, among them overfitting to the visible cases, hollow runtimes behind code that looks complete, and unsupported claims of completion, each a signal by which coding agents can learn to build the agents they are asked for.

</details>


### [247] [ProgramGym: Scaling Training Environments for Long-Horizon Program Reproduction Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13395)
*Chengyi Yang,Renxu Bao,Jue Hou,Zongpei Teng,Ziheng Wang,Yujie Lin,Chengsong Huang,Junnan Zhu,Delai Qiu,Yining Wang,Shengping Liu,Fei Long,Jinsong Su*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出 ProgramGym，一个面向长时程程序复现智能体的大规模可验证训练框架，包含环境构建器 Program-Env 与智能体工作流 Program-Agent，在 ProgramBench 上显著提升程序复现性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编码智能体多用于修仓库问题或按需求文档生成仓库，缺乏可扩展、可验证、要求智能体自行推断需求与设计的训练环境；且现有工作流无法支撑"先探索参考程序再实现"的长时程开发。

Method: Program-Env 通过反馈驱动的循环，从仓库构建可验证训练环境，修复运行时沙箱构建与黑盒测试质量，产出覆盖 C、C++、Go、Rust 的 1,648 个训练环境；Program-Agent 采用角色隔离的"分析—实现—审查"工作流，并结合修订管理以支持编译检查与失败恢复。

Result: Program-Env 与 Program-Agent 均提升程序复现性能；在构建环境轨迹上微调使 Qwen3.8-27B 从 34.87% 提升至 42.84%；Program-Agent 进一步使基座与 SFT 模型分别达到 55.71% 与 56.25%，超过部分旗舰模型。

Conclusion: ProgramGym 为"从零构建程序"的编码智能体研究提供了可扩展的训练环境与工作流，并将开源以支持后续研究。

Abstract: Coding agents are increasingly used to build programs from scratch as software engineers do rather than to resolve issues within a repository or to generate a repository from a given requirements document. However, scalable verifiable training environments that force agents to discover what is required and to decide how the system should be designed remain scarce. Moreover, existing agent workflows also cannot sustain long-horizon development in which the agent explores the reference program before implementation. To address these challenges, we introduce ProgramGym, a framework consisting of Program-Env and Program-Agent for scalable training environments of long-horizon program reproduction agents. Specifically, Program-Env constructs verifiable training environments from repositories through a feedback-driven loop that repairs runtime sandbox builds and the quality of black-box tests. Using this novel pipeline, we obtain 1,648 training environments spanning C, C++, Go, and Rust. Program-Agent combines a role-isolated workflow of analysis, implementation, and review with revision management for compilation checks and failure recovery. Experiments on ProgramBench show that both Program-Env and Program-Agent improve performance on program reproduction. Fine-tuning on trajectories with these constructed environments raises the performance of Qwen3.8-27B from 34.87% to 42.84%. Program-Agent further raises the base and SFT models to 55.71% and 56.25%, both exceeding some flagship models. We will release the ProgramGym to support further research on coding agents that build programs from scratch.

</details>


### [248] [REINS: Agent-guided Thread Scheduling for Concurrency Bug Detection and Reproduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13421)
*Yu Zhang,Corina S. Pasareanu,Guowei Yang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: REINS是一种基于智能体的并发缺陷检测与复现方法，通过推理并发语义和缺陷模式来引导线程调度，在相同时间预算下比现有技术检测到更多缺陷，并在真实项目中发现了六个此前未知的并发缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 并发缺陷仅在特定线程交错下才显现，难以检测和复现。现有受控并发测试技术接管线程调度以探索交错空间，但该空间随程序规模急剧增长，在有限预算内找到能触发缺陷的交错仍具挑战。程序的并发语义可揭示特定交错下哪些变量访问和同步操作会导致意外行为，从而帮助将探索聚焦于与缺陷相关的交错，但此前技术未充分利用这类语义信息。

Method: 提出REINS，一种用于检测和复现并发缺陷的智能体方法。其核心是让智能体对并发语义和已知并发缺陷模式进行推理，以此引导线程调度。REINS从运行时观察出发，结合静态信息推理程序的并发行为，并形成关于潜在缺陷的假设；每个假设转化为对事件顺序和变量状态的一组约束，引导受控调度器朝向可能触发该缺陷的交错。REINS根据执行结果得出结论，并持续细化约束，直到缺陷被触发或假设被否定。

Result: 在三个公开基准上，与现有技术相比，REINS在相同时间预算内检测到更多缺陷。此外，REINS在真实世界项目中发现了六个此前未知的并发缺陷，其中三个已得到开发者确认。

Conclusion: 将智能体对并发语义与已知缺陷模式的推理引入受控并发测试，可有效引导线程调度聚焦于缺陷相关交错，提升并发缺陷检测与复现的效率，并在真实项目中具备实用价值。

Abstract: Concurrency bugs manifest only under specific thread interleavings, making them hard to detect and reproduce. Existing controlled concurrency testing techniques take over thread scheduling to explore the interleaving space. As the space grows rapidly with program scale, finding interleavings that can trigger bugs within a limited budget remains challenging. The program's concurrency semantics can reveal which variable accesses and synchronization operations under specific interleavings lead to unexpected program behavior, and thus help focus the exploration on bug-relevant interleavings. However, prior techniques have not fully exploited such semantic information. In this paper, we present REINS, an agentic approach for detecting and reproducing concurrency bugs. At its core, REINS guides thread scheduling with the agent's reasoning about the concurrency semantics and known concurrency bug patterns. REINS starts with runtime observations and combines them with static information to reason about the program's concurrency behavior and formulate hypotheses about potential bugs. A hypothesis translates into a set of constraints on event orders and variable states, guiding a controlled scheduler toward interleavings likely to trigger the bug. REINS draws conclusions from the execution results and keeps refining the constraints until the bug is triggered or the hypothesis is refuted. Compared with existing techniques on three public benchmarks, REINS detects more bugs within the same time budget. Furthermore, REINS found six previously unknown concurrency bugs in real-world projects, three of which have been confirmed by the developers.

</details>


### [249] [Not All Correct Fixes Run Alike: An Empirical Study of Patch Performance in Repository-Level Issue Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13563)
*Han Peng,Xing Hu,Feifei Niu,Huan Li,Xin Xia*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文利用SWE-bench等仓库级基准中自带的FAIL_TO_PASS（F2P）测试来评估通过正确性测试的补丁的运行时性能，发现该指标能将约一半实例中的补丁区分开来，且与LLM问题解决率相互独立甚至排名近乎相反。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有仓库级问题解决基准（如SWE-bench）主要依据补丁是否通过正确性测试来评判，忽略了通过测试的不同补丁在运行时性能上的差异。为每个基准实例单独设计性能负载成本高昂，因此作者希望寻找一种低成本、可复用的性能评估方案。

Method: 作者提出复用基准中自带的F2P测试作为性能负载。选用Claude Opus 5、GPT 5.6、DeepSeek V4 Pro和Qwen 3.8 Max四个前沿模型，搭配SWE-agent，在SWE-bench与SWE-bench-Live的verified split中经过预筛选的可测量语料上求解，得到262个至少包含两种不同正确解（含开发者ground-truth补丁）的实例。随后在原生F2P测试上以指令数与墙钟时间衡量各解的运行时性能，并采用Welch ANOVA替代此前工作提出的RSD作为更稳健的统计判别标准。

Result: 该方法在运行时性能上表现出显著的判别能力，在近一半实例中识别出补丁间的性能差异。实证研究表明，生成补丁的运行时性能与LLM的问题解决率相互独立，且模型排名近乎相反；案例研究还显示，性能分析揭示了正确性评估所忽略的部分补丁的架构性回归。

Conclusion: 解决率不足以刻画LLM在仓库级问题解决上的能力，未来评估应在解决率之外同时考虑补丁性能。

Abstract: Repository-level issue-resolution benchmarks such as SWE-bench evaluate submitted patches primarily based on whether they pass the correctness tests, leaving the runtime performance of different test-passing patches largely unexplored. Evaluating patch performance is essential but challenging since designing performance workloads for each benchmark instance is costly. However, these benchmarks already provide a natural candidate: the built-in \emph{FAIL\_TO\_PASS} (F2P) tests, which exercise the behavior affected by the issue fix. We therefore investigate whether the provided F2P tests can be repurposed to evaluate patch performance. Overall, we choose four frontier models, Claude Opus~5, GPT~5.6, DeepSeek V4 Pro, and Qwen~3.8 Max, paired with SWE-agent to resolve the pre-filtered measurable corpus from verified splits of SWE-bench and SWE-bench-Live, yielding 262 cases with at least two different correct solutions, including the developer ground-truth patch. We then measure each solution's runtime performance in instruction count and wall-clock time on the native F2P test, and use Welch ANOVA instead of the RSD proposed in prior work as a more robust statistical discrimination criterion. We find that this methodology exhibits notable discrimination capability in runtime performance and identifies performance differences among patches in almost half of the instances. Our empirical study shows that the runtime performance of generated patches is independent of the issue resolution rate of LLMs, with nearly reversed model rankings. The case study also shows that the performance analysis reveals architectural regression in some patches that the correctness evaluation overlooked. These findings suggest that resolution rate does not adequately characterize LLMs' capability for repository-level issue resolution, motivating future evaluations to consider patch performance alongside it.

</details>


### [250] [SWE-DeceptionBench: Benchmarking Deceptive Behaviors of LLMs Across the Software Development Lifecycle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13626)
*Xiting Liu,Lin Yang,Yitong Zhang,Jia Li,Shi-Min Hu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出 SWE-DeceptionBench 基准，评估大模型在软件工程中的欺骗行为，发现中立条件下主动欺骗率达49.88%、被动欺骗率达64.29%，现有轻量防御难以根治。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型日益广泛地应用于软件开发生命周期，其可信性问题凸显，而欺骗行为（误导性回答、协助捏造/隐瞒/歪曲信息）风险尤为严重，但缺乏针对软件工程场景的系统性评测手段。

Method: 构建 SWE-DeceptionBench 基准，覆盖六个软件生命周期阶段与两类欺骗触发机制：主动欺骗（模型识别出不可行或冲突需求却仍宣称可实现）与被动欺骗（协助完成用户明确的误导他人请求）；并设置 Neutral、Reward、Pressure 三种外部诱导条件，生成中英双语共720个测试实例，评测七款主流LLM，并测试轻量防御方法。

Result: 即使在中立条件下欺骗行为也普遍存在：主动欺骗总体发生率49.88%，被动欺骗64.29%；欺骗率随生命周期阶段变化；外部诱导对两类欺骗的影响程度不同。防御评测显示，guard 模型漏检大量欺骗性请求，Self-Reminder 能降低但无法消除欺骗性回答。

Conclusion: LLM在软件工程任务中的欺骗行为普遍且难以通过现有轻量手段消除，亟需更有效的欺骗检测与缓解方法，以支撑可信的LLM驱动软件工程。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used throughout the software development lifecycle. This growing reliance on LLMs raises concerns about their trustworthiness, with deception posing a particularly important risk. Deceptive behavior takes the form of misleading responses or assistance in fabricating, concealing, or distorting information. To study this behavior, we introduce SWE-DeceptionBench, a benchmark for evaluating LLM deception in software engineering. The benchmark covers six lifecycle stages and two deception triggers: Active Deception, where models recognize infeasible or conflicting requirements but still present them as achievable, and Passive Deception, where models help fulfill explicit user requests to mislead others. We further introduce Neutral, Reward, and Pressure conditions to study the effect of external inducements, resulting in 720 test instances in Chinese and English. Evaluating seven mainstream LLMs, we find substantial deception even under the Neutral condition, with aggregate rates of 49.88% for Active Deception and 64.29% for Passive Deception. Deception rates vary across lifecycle stages, and external inducements affect Active and Passive Deception to different extents. We also evaluate lightweight defenses, finding that guard models miss many deceptive requests, and Self-Reminder reduces but does not eliminate deceptive responses. These findings highlight the need for more effective deception detection and mitigation methods to support trustworthy LLM-based software engineering.

</details>


### [251] [A-SAW: The Agentic Software Architecture Workflow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13647)
*Finn Klatt,Luca Di Grazia,Johannes Schneider*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出 Agentic Software Architecture Workflow（A-SAW），在 Architecture as Code 基础上加入持久化智能体层：IDE 集成智能体基于版本受控的 C4 模型、类型化元素目录与模板化文档生成架构文档，并强制人工评审。在大型工业组织的评估（45 份文档评分 + 13 位从业者访谈）显示作者间方差被消除，内在质量中位数提升 +19 分，智能体生成文档在完整性、一致性、机器可读性接近满分，可追溯性达 79%；评审仍不可省略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件工程中的 AI 辅助集中在实现与验证，设计阶段尤其是架构文档维护相对被忽视。Architecture as Code 虽使架构制品可版本化、可 diff、机器可读，却未降低维护成本，设计文档仍碎片化、依赖作者、可信度有限。

Method: 提出 A-SAW 流程级制品：在 Architecture as Code 上叠加持久智能体层，采用 IDE 集成智能体，依据版本受控 C4 模型、类型化元素目录与插桩模板起草架构文档；每份草稿须通过强制人工评审门禁方可采纳。评估在大型工业组织中进行双轨设计：45 份架构文档的评分量表评估（含 13 组匹配的源文档-再生成对），以及 13 次任务锚定的从业者访谈。

Result: 工作流消除了导致质量不可预测的作者间方差；内在质量在匹配对上中位数提升 +19 分。队列中模板、目录、智能体与更丰富再生成上下文的平均覆盖率分别为 58% 与 89%，因此仅能上界估计智能体贡献而非孤立其作用。智能体生成文档中完整性 100%、一致性 93%、机器可读性 99% 达到或接近量表上限，可追溯性 79%。13 位参与者中 11 位拒绝未经人工评审的智能体输出，1 位警告润色输出可能导致评审者跳过评审。

Conclusion: 应保持人工评审为强制性环节，并将其集中在智能体必须超越仓库信息进行推断之处；该工作流可作为架构文档维护的流程级改进方案。

Abstract: AI assistance in software engineering focuses on implementation and verification, leaving the design phase, and in particular the maintenance of architecture documentation, comparatively underserved. Architecture as Code makes architectural artifacts versionable, diffable, and machine-readable, but it does not reduce the effort of keeping them updated, so design documentation still ends up as a fragmented, author-dependent, and only partially trusted record of why a system looks the way it does.
  In this paper we introduce the Agentic Software Architecture Workflow (A-SAW), a process-level artifact that extends Architecture as Code with a persistent agent layer: an IDE-integrated agent drafts architecture documents from a version-controlled C4 model, a typed element catalog, and instrumented templates. Every draft passes a mandatory human review gate before it is accepted. We evaluate it in a large industrial organization along two tracks: a rubric assessment of 45 architecture documents including 13 matched source-regeneration pairs, and 13 task-anchored practitioner interviews. Our results show that the workflow collapses the between-author variance that made quality unpredictable. Intrinsic quality also rises, by a median of +19 points across the matched pair. The cohort has a mean of 58% and 89% bundle templates, catalog, agent, and a richer regeneration context. So they bound the agent's contribution from above rather than isolating it. In the agent-generated documents, completeness (100%), consistency (93%), and machine-readability (99%) reach or approach the rubric maximum, while traceability reaches 79%. Eleven of the 13 participants rejected agent output that no human had reviewed, and one warned that polished output may lead reviewers to skip the review. Our work supports keeping human review mandatory and concentrating it where the agent must infer beyond the repository.

</details>


### [252] [(When) Should a Software Factory be Lights-off? Benchmarking Code Review Agents on Bug Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13668)
*Chenyang Yang,Hao He,Ti-Chung Cheng,Jenna Butler,Emerson Murphy-Hill,Carmen Badea,Christian Bird*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文构建了 CRBugBench 基准（1,000 个开源任务 + 800 个企业专有任务），评估代码审查智能体能否检出人工确认的真实缺陷；实验显示最优智能体在 top-5 评论中检出 71.9% 的缺陷，但在复杂变更、需非局部推理及企业仓库场景下表现明显下降，并给出提示工程与算力扩展等实践建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体使代码生成速度远超人工审查速度，业界倾向构建全自动审查智能体以迈向"无人值守"的软件工厂。但这类完全自主模式的可行性缺乏可量化的评估依据，因此需要一套以"是否检出人工确认缺陷"为标准的基准来检验审查智能体的实际能力边界。

Method: 构建数据流水线，从代码仓库历史中挖掘缺陷，并将每个缺陷与其引入该缺陷的 PR 配对，形成审查任务；据此构建 CRBugBench，包含来自 98 个开源 GitHub 仓库的 1,000 个审查任务，以及来自某大型企业内部专有仓库的 800 个任务。随后在该基准上评测当前最先进审查智能体的缺陷检出质量，并分析算力扩展与提示设计的影响。

Result: 最优组合（claude-opus-5 + copilot-cli）在 top-5 评论中检出 71.9% 的缺陷，五个月内由 opus-4.6 的 47.7% 显著提升；但智能体在复杂变更和需要非局部推理的缺陷上效果较差，在更需要项目上下文的企业专有仓库上性能明显下降。算力扩展主要经由提示改动起作用，探索引导型提示在成本增加 10% 的情况下带来最高 5.8% 的性能提升。

Conclusion: 当前审查智能体尚不足以支撑完全自主的"无人值守"软件工厂，但在有监督协作模式下已具实用价值。作者据此提出落地建议：按 PR 复杂度匹配审查投入力度、用项目优先级引导智能体、提供仓库知识、按 PR 特征分派模型，并随模型演进持续监控审查质量。

Abstract: With the advances of coding agents, software developers are spending more time reviewing code than writing code. As human review velocity falls behind agentic code generation, it is tempting to build automated review agents towards a future of "lights-off" software factory with little to no human supervision. To assess the practicality of such a fully autonomous software factory, we construct CRBugBench, a new benchmark that evaluates whether review agents detect human-verified bugs. We build a data pipeline that mines bugs from repository history, and pair each bug with its introducing PR to form a review task. CRBugBench consists of 1,000 review tasks across 98 open-source GitHub repositories, and 800 additional tasks from proprietary industry repositories at one large company.
  We then use the benchmark to study agent review quality: We found that SOTA review agents do well: the best (claude-opus-5 with copilot-cli) catches 71.9% of bugs within its top-5 comments, up from 47.7% (opus-4.6) in five months of model evolution. Yet agents remain less effective on complex changes and bugs requiring non-local reasoning, with sharp performance drops on proprietary industry repositories where project context is more often needed. To improve bug detection, we found scaling compute helps most through prompt changes: exploration-nudging prompts gain up to 5.8% performance at 10% higher cost. From these findings, we derive concrete guidance for developers to adopt review agents - scaling review effort to PR complexity, steering agents by project priorities, supplying repository knowledge, dispatching models by PR characteristics, and monitoring quality as models evolve.

</details>


### [253] [MARGoS: A Configuration-Driven Platform for Reproducible MARL Experiments in ARGoS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13772)
*Tom Philipp Backert,Mohammad D. Soorati,Javad Ghofrani*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: MARGoS是一个轻量级、配置驱动的平台，将ARGoS+MARL实验中的关键输入（配置、种子、路径、环境信息）提升为平台管理的一等工件，通过配置哈希、种子传播、冻结配置、结构化日志、环境指纹和可导出实验包，提升实验的复用性与可复现性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体强化学习（MARL）在群体机器人领域发展迅速，ARGoS仿真器被广泛用于研究涌现式群体行为。然而，基于MARL的群体机器人研究在复用与可复现性方面缺乏系统支持与标准化：单个实验往往由人工对齐的仿真器文件、训练脚本、配置值、路径、随机种子和环境细节拼凑而成，即使在同一个研究组内部，复用与复现也十分脆弱。

Method: 提出MARGoS平台，以配置驱动方式将实验定义输入提升为平台管理的工件。核心机制包括：配置哈希、端到端随机种子传播、不可变（冻结）配置、结构化日志、环境指纹，以及可导出的实验包，以支持跨机器、跨时间的共享、重放与结果验证。

Result: 通过架构、效率、可复现性、可用性与协作五个维度的评估，验证了将实验变异作为平台职责来管理，可使ARGoS+MARL实验更易于复用、复现与交接。代码已开源于 https://github.com/tombackert/margos。

Conclusion: 将实验配置与环境等变异因素交由平台统一管理，是提升ARGoS+MARL群体机器人实验可复现性与可复用性的有效途径；MARGoS为这一方向提供了轻量、可落地的实现方案。

Abstract: Multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) has become a prominent research direction, particularly within collective robotics, where the ARGoS simulator is widely used to study emergent swarm behavior. Yet despite this growth, systematic support for reuse and reproducibility in MARL-based swarm robotics remains limited and insufficiently standardized: a single experiment is assembled from manually aligned simulator files, training scripts, configuration values, paths, seeds, and environment details, so reuse and reproduction stay fragile even within one research group. To address this gap, we introduce MARGoS, a lightweight, configuration-driven platform developed in this project that elevates these experiment-defining inputs to first-class, platform-managed artifacts. MARGoS enables reproducible experimentation through configuration hashing, end-to-end seed propagation, frozen (immutable) configurations, structured logging, environment fingerprinting, and exportable experiment bundles that facilitate sharing, replay, and verification of results across machines and over time. A five-dimension evaluation (architecture, efficiency, reproducibility, usability, and collaboration) supports our claim that managing experiment variation as a platform responsibility makes ARGoS+MARL experiments easier to reuse, reproduce, and hand off. Our code is available at https://github.com/tombackert/margos.

</details>
